Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MiMo-V2.6系列，一个通过大规模扩展强化学习（RL）计算推动模型智能前沿的全模态模型家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以强化学习为核心训练范式，推动大型基础模型实现自我改进。

Method: 在三个维度扩展RL计算：(1)更大批次和更高吞吐量，采用异步训练，每步消耗1,568个样本和2.7-3.7B tokens，支持高达1M上下文长度；(2)更多样化和复杂的环境，涵盖代码、通用、视觉和网络安全领域，采用混合智能体框架；(3)更多评分计算，通过分组智能体评分获得更准确的奖励信号，引导模型生成更短、更token高效的解决方案。为保持大规模训练稳定，冻结MoE路由器并建立多层防御对抗奖励黑客攻击。构建混合任务智能体RL基础设施，包括统一轨迹表示、高并发多框架rollout、解耦控制与数据平面、训练推理一致性。

Result: 开源训练动态、RL环境和RL框架，促进规模化RL和模型自我改进的复现与进一步研究。

Conclusion: MiMo-V2.6系列通过在计算规模、环境多样性和评分机制三个维度系统性扩展RL，配合稳健的训练稳定性机制和完善的工程基础设施，显著推进了全模态模型的智能水平，并通过完全开源促进了领域发展。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is the central training paradigm for advancing large foundation models towards self-improvement. This report introduces the MiMo-V2.6 series, an omni-modal family that pushes the frontier of model intelligence by scaling RL compute. Prior to RL, we conduct mid-training on a broad multimodal corpus to provide ample exploration space, and build a solid infrastructure on the pretrained hybrid-SWA architecture to support subsequent scale-up. We scale RL compute along three dimensions: (1) larger batches and higher throughput, with an asynchronous training that consumes 1,568 samples and 2.7-3.7B tokens per step at context lengths of up to 1M; (2) more diverse and complex environments, spanning code, general, visual, and cyber domains under a mixture of agent harnesses; and (3) more grader compute, via groupwise agentic grading that yields more accurate reward signals for long-horizon tasks and steers the model towards shorter, more token-efficient solutions. To keep training stable at scale, we freeze the MoE router and establish a multi-layer defense against reward hacking. We further build infrastructure for mixed-task agentic RL, including a unified trajectory representation, high-concurrency multi-framework rollout, decoupled control and data planes, and training-inference consistency. We open-source the training dynamics, RL environments, and RL framework to facilitate reproduction and further research on scaled RL and model self-improvement.

</details>


### [72] [Specialized Decision Models vs. General-Purpose LLMs: Benchmarking Jev Across Knowledge, Reasoning, and Multilingual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11978)
*Xing Li,Qingcheng Chang,Jinzhong Ning,Changfeng Xu,Shenlong Zhang,Yijia Zhang,Ling Luo,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Jev是一个专门的“System One”决策模型，在知识和常识基准上与前沿大语言模型（LLM）表现相当，甚至在MMLU-Redux和ARC-Challenge上取得最佳分数；但在数学文字问题上显著落后，MathQA得分比前沿模型中位数低17.7分，且低于所有19个对比LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究专门用于多项选择决策的模型（直接输出选项而非生成文本）与通用大语言模型在知识、推理和多语言理解任务上的性能差距，探讨专用决策模型能否替代通用LLM。

Method: 在13个涵盖知识、推理、多语言理解的多项选择基准上评估Jev，并与19个分为前沿、代表性、小型三个梯队的LLM进行对比实验。

Result: Jev在知识和常识基准上与前沿LLM竞争，MMLU-Redux和ARC-Challenge得分最高；在非数学任务上超越大多数代表性LLM和所有小型LLM；但在数学文字问题上表现不佳，MathQA比前沿中位数低17.7分，且低于所有19个LLM。

Conclusion: 专用决策模型可以在主要依赖知识检索的决策任务上匹配通用LLM，但无法在需要多步计算的复杂推理决策上达到同等水平。

Abstract: Jev is a "System One" model that returns a choice among given options instead of generating text. We study how such a specialized decision model compares with general-purpose large language models (LLMs). We evaluate Jev on 13 multiple-choice benchmarks covering knowledge, reasoning, and multilingual understanding, and compare it with 19 LLMs in three tiers: frontier, representative, and small. Jev is competitive with frontier LLMs on knowledge and commonsense benchmarks and obtains the best score on MMLU-Redux and ARC-Challenge. Outside mathematics, it also outperforms most representative LLMs and all small LLMs. However, it falls behind on mathematical word problems: on MathQA, it is 17.7 points below the frontier median and scores lower than all 19 LLMs. These results indicate that a specialized decision model can match general-purpose LLMs on decisions that rely mainly on knowledge, but not on decisions that require multi-step calculation.

</details>


### [73] [Examining Social Attribution in LLM Reasoning: A Theory-Guided Probing Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12022)
*Zhaoxin Yu,Qingchao Kong,Dajun Zeng,Wenji Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统探索大语言模型（LLM）的社会归因能力，聚焦责任与归咎判断。基于归因理论构建包含 7,639 道题的 SAB-Bench 基准（含经典情境子集与真实叙事子集），评测 32 个代表性 LLM 与 5 个非 LLM 基线。并提出基于探针的方法，从隐藏状态中解码 5 个关键归因维度的潜在表示，考察其对判决的影响与人类归因理论的一致性。结果显示：LLM 与人类判断存在可测度但不完全的一致性，且一致性随模型规模正相关；部分归因维度可从特定层位系统性解码，其对最终判断的影响模式与归因理论预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会归因（将外部事件归因于主体的社会行为原因）在社会技术系统中至关重要，涉及社会原因、责任、归咎/归功等判断。归因理论在社会心理学与认知科学中研究深入，但在 AI 尤其是 LLM 社会推理领域仍未被充分探索。亟需系统性基准与机制分析，以理解 LLM 的归因判断能力及其内部表征机制。

Method: 1. 基于归因理论构建 SAB-Bench：Vignette 子集（经典归因理论情境）与 Reality 子集（真实社会叙事），共 7,639 个责任/归咎判断问题。2. 评测 32 个主流 LLM 与 5 个非 LLM 基线。3. 开发基于探针的方法：在 LLM 隐藏状态中训练线性探针解码 5 个关键归因维度（如因果性、意图性、可控性等），分析其在特定层位的可解码性，并量化这些维度对最终判断的影响权重，与人类归因理论预测对比。

Result: LLM 与人类责任/归咎判断呈可测度但不完全一致，一致性随模型规模显著正相关。5 个归因维度中，部分维度（如意图性、可控性）可从中后层隐藏状态系统性解码；这些维度对 LLM 最终判断的影响方向与权重，与归因理论在人类中的预测高度一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为 LLM 社会归因研究奠定了基准与机制分析范式。结果表明当前 LLM 已部分内化人类归因理论的核心维度，且大模型更接近人类判断模式。潜在表示的可解码性与影响一致性为可解释性与对齐提供了新切入点。数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in sociotechnical systems where social attribution, the reasoning process attributing external events to the causes and reasons of agents' social behaviors, plays a critical role. These processes involve judgments of social cause, responsibility, and blame/credit to agents. Although attributional models are well-studied in social psychology and cognition through Attribution Theory, social attribution remains underexplored in AI, particularly LLM social reasoning. This paper provides the first systematic exploration of LLM social attribution. Our work focuses on responsibility and blame attributions, examining current LLMs' judgments and their underlying internal mechanisms. Guided by attribution theory, we construct a social attribution benchmark consisting of a Vignette subset based on classic scenarios from attribution theory research and a Reality subset based on real-world social narratives, yielding 7,639 responsibility/blame judgment questions. On this basis, we evaluate 32 representative LLMs and 5 basic non-LLM baselines. To further explore the internal mechanisms underlying the LLM judgment process, we develop a probing-based methodology to investigate the latent-space representations of 5 key attribution dimensions and the consistency of their influences on LLM judgments compared to those in human social attribution. Our research findings reveal that current LLMs exhibit measurable but incomplete agreement with human responsibility and blame judgments, and meanwhile, this agreement is positively correlated with model size. Some attribution dimensions are systematically decodable from specific positions in LLM hidden states, and their influences on the final judgment are consistent with those indicated by human Attribution Theory. The dataset and associated code are available at https://github.com/Yuzhaoxin946/SAB-Bench.

</details>


### [74] [Natural Language to First-Order Logic LLM-based Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12030)
*Andrea Brunello,Cristian Curaba,Luca Geatti,Michele Mignani,Angelo Montanari,Nicola Saccomanno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文填补了以一阶逻辑（FOL）为目标形式化的自动形式化领域中，统一任务定义与系统性综述的空白，提出了本体抽取与逻辑翻译的区分原则，综述了数据集、评估指标与大模型方法，并指出了基准测试、语义评估、本体感知方法及端到端应用等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）的发展，自动形式化重获关注，但以一阶逻辑为目标形式化时，领域缺乏统一的任务形式化定义与系统性综述，且本体抽取与逻辑翻译的混淆阻碍了跨研究评估。

Method: 1. 提出原则性任务定义，将FOL自动形式化拆解为本体抽取与逻辑翻译两个子任务；2. 系统综述现有数据集、评估指标与基于LLM的方法（微调、提示工程、基于验证的细化）；3. 分析当前方法的局限与开放问题。

Result: 明确了本体抽取与逻辑翻译的区分对评估的重要性，梳理了主流数据集、指标与LLM方法体系，识别出基准测试不足、语义评估缺失、本体感知方法稀缺及端到端应用落地难等四大核心挑战。

Conclusion: 建立统一的FOL自动形式化任务框架与评估体系是推动领域发展的关键，未来需重点攻克语义级评估、本体感知建模及端到端自动化流程，以实现从自然语言到可验证一阶逻辑的可靠转换。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have renewed interest in autoformalization. Yet, when First-Order Logic (FOL) is considered as the target formalism, the field still lacks a unified task formulation and a systematic survey. This paper addresses this gap: we first provide a principled definition for the FOL-autoformalization task by distinguishing Ontology Extraction from Logical Translation, showing how their conflation obscures (cross-study) evaluation; we review existing datasets, evaluation metrics, and LLM-based methods, including fine-tuning, prompting, and verification-based refinement; we identify open challenges in benchmarking, semantic evaluation, ontology-aware methods, and end-to-end applications.

</details>


### [75] [ILM: An AI-Powered Storytelling Educational Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12064)
*Suhaila Mohammed,Abdelaziz Serour,Allison Lahnala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ILM，一个面向先知故事的交互式教育平台，结合阿拉伯语自然语言处理、知识图谱构建和基于检索的问题生成，支持结构化学习、多语言理解和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字平台虽使伊斯兰叙事更易获取，但缺乏对结构化学习和理解的支持，尤其在阿拉伯语和多语言场景下。需要一种能整合结构化知识表示与基于段落检索的系统，以实现叙事探索、理解和评估。

Method: 1. 管理员审核的阿拉伯语叙事经知识图谱构建引擎抽取实体与叙事关系，存储为结构化知识，生成可视化故事地图及实体/关系型问题。2. 多语言检索管线从原始叙事中检索相关段落，生成选择题和开放式理解题。3. 开放式题目采用LLM-as-a-Judge依据检索段落和参考答案评分。4. 古兰经内容作为独立丰富层，为特定叙事提供有来源支撑的补充信息。

Result: 系统验证了结合结构化知识表示与基于检索生成的可行性，实现了跨阿拉伯语和多语言内容的叙事探索、理解和评估。提供在线演示。

Conclusion: ILM展示了如何通过知识图谱与检索增强生成相结合，构建支持伊斯兰叙事交互式学习的平台，为阿拉伯语及多语言教育技术提供了新范式。

Abstract: Digital technologies have made Islamic narratives more accessible, but existing platforms provide limited support for structured learning and comprehension of these stories, particularly in Arabic and multilingual settings. We present ILM, an interactive educational platform for Stories of the Prophets that combines Arabic natural language processing, structured knowledge representation, and retrieval-based question generation. Admin-approved Arabic narratives are processed by a Knowledge Graph (KG) Constructor Engine that identifies entities and narrative relationships and stores them as structured knowledge, enabling learners to explore stories through a visual story map and answer entity- and relation-based questions generated from the KG. Separately, a multilingual retrieval pipeline retrieves relevant passages from the original narratives to generate multiple-choice and open-ended comprehension questions. For open-ended questions, an LLM-as-a-Judge evaluates learners' answers against the retrieved passages and reference answers to determine correctness. The platform also incorporates Quranic content as a separate enrichment layer, allowing selected narratives to be supplemented with source-supported information. By combining structured knowledge with passage-based retrieval, ILM supports narrative exploration, comprehension, and assessment across Arabic and multilingual content. The system demonstrates the feasibility of combining structured knowledge representation and retrieval-based generation to support interactive learning of Islamic narratives. A demo is available at anonymous.4open.science/r/mml-5FCF.

</details>


### [76] [All Verdicts are Not Equal: Rethinking LLM Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12083)
*Vineet Kumar,Darshita Rathore,Anindya Moitra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对LLM-as-a-Judge评估范式进行了全面的可靠性审计，揭示了严重的系统性脆弱性，并提出了可信判决率（T）作为统一指标，证明了整体评分优于成对胜率的可靠性提升策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge已成为NLP评估的标准范式，但其系统性可靠性仍知之甚少，却被广泛视为确定性的真实标签，亟需严格审计。

Method: 对六个前沿模型在四个基准、五种提示格式、两种呈现顺序、三种采样温度、每条件十次重复进行压力测试，实证分析多维故障模式。

Result: 发现严重漏洞：温度为零时相同复现仍判决翻转；位置顺序交换导致多数挑战性任务判决反转；最确定性的评判器通过重复错误判决实现完美一致性，但与真实标签一致率仅51%。引入可信判决率（T）捕捉可复现性、顺序不变性和准确性的联合概率，推导出位置偏差施加的准确性理论上界，证明可靠性是条目级而非模型级的。展示从成对胜率转向整体评分标准比任何单一格式提示干预更能提升可信度。

Conclusion: 为稳健NLP评估提供了可落地的框架，强调需从成对比较转向整体评分标准，并利用T指标量化评估可信度。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge is the standard paradigm for NLP evaluation, yet its systemic reliability remains poorly understood despite being widely treated as a deterministic ground truth. We present a comprehensive reliability audit, stresstesting six frontier models across four benchmarks, five prompt formats, two presentation orders, three sampling temperatures, and ten repetitions per condition. Our empirical analysis reveals severe vulnerabilities: verdicts change across identical replications at temperature zero, position-order swaps flip the majority of verdicts on challenging tasks, and the most deterministic judge achieves perfect consistency by trivially repeating incorrect verdicts, agreeing with ground truth only 51% of the time. To formalize these multi-faceted failure modes, we introduce the trustworthy verdict rate (T ), a unified metric capturing the joint probability that an evaluation is reproducible, order-invariant, and accurate. UsingT , we derive a theoretical upper bound on accuracy imposed by position bias and show that reliability is item-specific rather than modellevel. Finally, we demonstrate that shifting from pairwise win-rate to holistic rubric scoring improves trustworthiness more than any single-format prompting intervention, offering an actionable framework for robust NLP evaluation.

</details>


### [77] [A persistent accuracy ceiling in automated verbal deception detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12118)
*Riccardo Loconte,Jonas Festor,Zane Fatjanova,Mariam Bolkvadze,Bennett Kleinberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统回顾了25年自动化言语欺骗检测研究（289篇报告，6136个模型），元分析3653个模型，发现汇总准确率74.4%，但方法学质量（真实标签、数据来源、类别平衡、评估程序）比模型复杂度更影响性能；嵌入和大语言模型未提升预测表现；仅12.46%使用可验证真实标签，23.96%在独立数据评估；准确率与人工方法持平，暗示70-75%天花板难以突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化方法被提出以克服人工言语欺骗检测的局限，但跨学科证据碎片化，缺乏系统性量化综合，需要全面评估自动化检测的真实效能及其驱动因素。

Method: 系统性文献回顾与多层级元分析：检索25年文献（289篇报告，6136个分类模型），筛选97个数据集中的3653个模型进行元分析，计算汇总准确率及95%置信区间，检验异质性，并考察方法学质量（真实标签验证、数据来源、类别平衡、评估程序）与模型复杂度（嵌入、大语言模型）对准确率的影响。

Result: 汇总准确率74.4%（95% CI: 71.2%-77.4%），存在显著异质性。方法学质量显著驱动准确率，而模型复杂度（嵌入、LLM）未带来性能提升。仅12.46%报告使用可验证真实标签数据，23.96%模型在独立数据上评估。汇总准确率与人工检测元分析结果一致，提示70-75%可能为当前研究范式下的性能上限。

Conclusion: 当前自动化言语欺骗检测研究受限于方法学缺陷（真实标签缺失、缺乏独立验证），而非模型复杂度不足。嵌入和大语言模型的引入尚未转化为实际性能增益。若不改变数据构建与评估规范，准确率难以突破70-75%的天花板。未来研究应优先提升方法学严谨性，而非单纯追求模型创新。

Abstract: Automated methods have been proposed to overcome the limitations of human verbal deception detection, but evidence remains fragmented across disciplines. We systematically reviewed 25 years of research (289 reports, 6,136 classification models) and meta-analyzed 3,653 models nested within 97 datasets. Pooled accuracy was 74.4% (95% CI: 71.2%-77.4%) with substantial heterogeneity. Accuracy was driven by methodological quality (ground truth, data source, class balance, evaluation procedure) more than by model complexity: the adoption of embeddings and large language models has not translated into improved predictive performance. Only 12.46% of reports used data with verifiable ground-truth, and only 23.96% of models were evaluated on independent data. The pooled accuracy aligns with meta-analyses of manual approaches, suggesting a ceiling of 70-75%, unlikely to be lifted by current research conventions.

</details>


### [78] [Rehearse Everything, Remember Nothing: Attic-KV Rehearses What Will Be Read](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12133)
*Zhiyun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Attic-KV，一种无需训练的KV缓存压缩方法，通过让模型用引用上下文的问答对自测（而非全文重读）来选择保留的KV条目，在极低保留率（3%）下显著优于现有方法，且能与梯度/训练方法结合进一步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩依赖“重读重现”策略：模型重读上下文并保留注意力高的条目。但在严格预算下，该策略反而失效——重读会将预算分散到整个上下文，导致答案相关的关键条目仅靠运气保留。论文指出：缓存记住的是它重读的内容；像学生备考靠自测比重读更有效。

Method: Attic-KV（Attic）：一种无训练的重读机制。模型生成引用上下文的问答对（quiz），并结合内容自适应数量的锚点令牌进行自我测验，仅保留被这些自测激活的KV条目。两大原则：1）重读将被读取的内容；2）重读量与内容量匹配。仅修改重读阶段，兼容现有评分主机。

Result: 在RULER和LongBench自然文本任务的八个设置中，Attic单独使用即为最佳无训练方法。接入KVgrad和RestoreKV+后分别提升达17.1和28.1点。优势随预算收缩扩大：3%保留率下比全文重读高41.9点。压缩速度也快于全文重读。

Conclusion: 重读全文是低预算KV压缩的陷阱；基于自测的针对性重读（Attic-KV）能精准保留任务关键信息。该方法无需训练、即插即用，且在极端压缩比下表现尤为突出，为长上下文LLM部署提供了实用的压缩范式。

Abstract: Many key-value (KV) caches are compressed before anyone knows what will be asked of them: a document cached for retrieval, a prompt prefix shared across requests, the memory of a long conversation. The prevailing approach scores KV entries by rehearsal: the model rereads the context and keeps the entries it attends to, assuming that the more completely a cache rehearses its context, the better it remembers it. We show that under tight budgets this assumption backfires: rehearse everything, remember nothing. At a 3% keep ratio, rereading the whole context keeps 31.5 of 96.5 points on RULER, and on LongBench's natural-text tasks it falls below methods that rehearse nothing at all. The cause is that a cache keeps what it rehearses: rereading spreads the budget across the whole context, so the answer's own entries survive at little more than chance. Like a student before an exam, a cache remembers more by testing itself than by rereading. Two principles follow: rehearse what will be read, and rehearse as much as there is. We instantiate them as Attic-KV (Attic for short), a training-free rehearsal in which the model quizzes itself with question-answer pairs that quote the context, alongside anchor tokens in a content-adaptive amount. Changing only the rehearsal lifts three hosts that score it in three different ways: Attic alone is the best training-free method in all eight settings we test on RULER and LongBench's natural-text tasks, and plugged into the gradient-based KVgrad and the trained RestoreKV+, it raises them by up to 17.1 and 28.1 points. Its advantage grows as the budget shrinks, reaching 41.9 points over full rereading at a 3% keep ratio, and it compresses faster than rereading the whole context.

</details>


### [79] [Language-Specific Effects of Tokenizer Choice in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12144)
*Clara Meister,Gül Sena Altıntaş,Antoine Bosselut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了分词器选择对多语言语言建模的影响，发现分词器选择对低资源语言影响更大，且分词器训练数据分配策略需谨慎设计。通过训练123个语言模型（54个分词器），证明了分词器固有属性与下游性能的关系因语言而异，并提出了基于分词器指标预测模型性能的实用筛选策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献未解决分词器选择是否对所有语言同等重要的问题。词表容量有限且规模受限，改善某些语言的表示往往以牺牲其他语言为代价。需要量化分词器选择对不同资源语言的差异化影响，并寻找分词器训练数据分配的最优策略。

Method: 训练123个语言模型，跨越54个分词器，覆盖31种语言。控制变量法：固定架构、训练语料、训练token预算和优化策略，仅变化分词器。测量各语言的bits-per-byte (BPB)指标，分析BPB标准差与语言模型训练数据占比的相关性（Spearman相关系数），研究语言被排除在分词器训练外的惩罚效应，对比等权重分配和反比分配策略，并关联分词器固有属性指标与下游BPB性能。

Result: 1) 分词器选择对低资源语言影响更大：BPB标准差随训练数据占比减少而增加（Spearman ρ = -0.52，28种有词界语言为-0.69）。2) 将语言排除在分词器训练外会提高其BPB，惩罚随资源减少而增大。3) 给低资源语言更多分词器训练数据并不无条件有帮助：等权重和反比分配均增加BPB，尤其在LM训练重复数据时。4) 与更好BPB相关的分词器固有属性因语言而异。5) 分词器指标可成功预测下游模型成对BPB排名。

Conclusion: 分词器选择对低资源语言影响显著更大，分词器训练数据分配需避免简单的等权重或反比策略。分词器质量指标因语言而异，但可作为训练前筛选分词器候选的实用预测工具。这为多语言模型的分词器设计提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Tokenizer choice affects multilingual language modeling, but vocabulary capacity is finite and vocabulary size is often constrained: improving representation for some languages often comes at the expense of others. We therefore ask whether tokenizer choice matters equally across languages, a question that the current literature leave unanswered. To this end, we train 123 language models spanning 54 tokenizers. In the main comparison, architecture, training corpus, training-token budget, and optimization are held fixed, so the models differ only in their tokenizer. We find that tokenizer choice matters more for languages with less language-model training data: across the 54 tokenizers, the standard deviation of a language's bits-per-byte (BPB) increases as its model training-data share decreases (Spearman rho = -0.52 over the 31 trained languages and -0.69 over the 28 written with word boundaries). Leaving a language out of tokenizer training raises its BPB in every language we study, and the penalty tends to be larger for languages with less language-model training data. Giving lower-resource languages a larger share of tokenizer-training data, however, does not unconditionally help those languages: both equal weighting and an allocation inverting the shares with respect to the language model training data increase their BPB, particularly when language-model training repeats data. Finally, which intrinsic tokenizer properties are associated with better BPB differs across languages, providing further evidence that what makes a good tokenizer depends on the language. We find that the metrics quantifying these properties can be successfully used to predict downstream models' pairwise BPB rankings, suggesting a practical strategy for screening tokenizer candidates before training language models.

</details>


### [80] [SciTBERT: A family of chronologically consistent language models for scientific and technological language processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12207)
*Thomas Gebhart,Russell J. Funk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SciTBERT系列时间一致性BERT模型，通过按年份截断训练数据（2013-2025）并结合论文专利引用后训练，解决现有预训练模型在时序依赖和归档科学研究中的前瞻偏差与领域偏差问题，并构建PatRepEval基准评估科技交叉任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对论文/专利文本微调的编码器虽在下游任务优于通用模型，但因训练语料时间分布和文本来源无约束，存在前瞻偏差和领域偏差，限制其对科学技术时序演变及交叉特性的研究。

Method: 1. 收集科学论文、专利及高质量教育网络文本，按年份设定截断日期（2013-2025）训练SciTBERT系列模型；2. 利用论文和专利引用关系进行时间一致性后训练，得到SciTBERT-CI系列；3. 构建PatRepEval基准，包含跨越论文与专利的分类、回归、检索等科技交叉嵌入任务。

Result: SciTBERT系列在早期年份数据受限条件下仍超越前代领域专用编码器；时间一致性编码器在PatRepEval多任务上匹配或超越无时间约束模型，验证了将编码器表示与下游任务领域分布对齐的重要性。

Conclusion: 时间一致性预训练结合引用增强后训练能有效缓解前瞻偏差，为研究科学技术演进及交叉提供更可靠的表征工具，PatRepEval为科技交叉嵌入评估提供标准化基准。

Abstract: Pre-trained transformer models are increasingly being used to study scientific and technological progress. Encoders tuned to paper or patent text outperform general-purpose models on downstream classification, regression, and proximity tasks within science and technology. However, the applicability of these models for studying time-dependent or archival properties of science, technology, and their interface is limited due to lookahead and domain biases inherent to these pre-trained models. These limitations arise from training on corpora with unconstrained chronological and text source distributions. We introduce SciTBERT: a family of chronologically consistent BERT-derived language models trained on text from scientific papers, patents, and high-quality educational web text with training data cutoff dates spanning each year between 2013 and 2025. We also post-train these models in a chronologically-consistent manner using paper and patent citations, creating SciTBERT-CI model family. We find that these models generally outperform predecessor domain-specific encoder models even when training data is limited by early year restrictions in the corpus. To further investigate the extent to which this class of models can learn representations that bridge science and technology, we introduce the PatRepEval benchmark, a suite of patent-related text embedding tasks at the science-technology interface. Performance in a variety of classification, regression, and retrieval tasks spanning papers and patents highlights the importance of aligning encoder model representations with the domain distributions of their downstream tasks, and chronologically consistent encoders can match or exceed models trained without temporal constraints.

</details>


### [81] [Language Models as AI Research World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12235)
*Zijun Wang,Zewen Liu,Minhua Lin,Zhaotian Weng,Zhan Shi,Bing He,Yisi Sang,Dakuo Wang,Benoit Dumoulin,Wei Jin,Yuyin Zhou,Cihang Xie,Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了研究世界模型（RWM），利用语言模型预测研究实验的结果，发现真实实验经验能显著提升模型在同一环境及跨环境下的预测性能，并能在多轮自动化研究中带来更大的收益提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI研究智能体提出实验的速度超过其在真实环境中执行实验的能力，因此在有限实验预算下，预测实验结果成为持续自我改进的关键能力。

Method: 基于来自九个研究环境（涵盖预训练、后训练和推理）的2600多条实验记录（超过171,000 H100 GPU小时），评估语言模型作为RWM预测候选干预结果的能力；研究知识来源于真实实验经验，并在同环境及跨环境（如从OLMo3、Marin、Nanochat的预训练经验迁移到Qwen3环境）进行评估，同时进行了13个语言模型的消融实验。

Result: 研究知识使RWM在同一环境中对未见干预的预测显著提升（Spearman相关系数+0.27），跨环境迁移可将Qwen3环境中的选择遗憾减少78%；在多轮自动化研究中，带有同环境和跨环境研究知识的RWM分别使最佳收益提升15.8%和11.6%；消融实验显示，添加研究知识比更换模型或增加推理努力更能改善干预排序。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持语言模型作为研究世界模型的可行性，并激励积累实验数据用于未来RWM的训练。

Abstract: AI research agents automate the cycle of proposing, implementing, and evaluating experiments, opening a path toward recursive self-improvement. Yet their ability to propose experiments outpaces their capacity to execute them in real environments, making outcome prediction a key capability for sustained self-improvement under limited experimental budgets. We investigate language models as Research World Models (RWMs), which predict the outcomes of candidate interventions across research environments. Our evaluation draws on over 2,600 experimental records from nine research environments spanning pretraining, post-training, and inference, representing more than 171,000 H100 GPU-hours of experimentation. Research knowledge acquired from real experimental experience improves RWM predictions of unseen interventions within the same environment (Spearman +0.27), and can be reused across environments. For example, using only pretraining experience from OLMo3, Marin, and Nanochat, an RWM reduces selection regret in the Qwen3 environment by 78% compared with zero-experience setting. These benefits extend to multi-round Autoresearch under a fixed selection budget: RWMs with in-env and cross-env research knowledge increase the best gain achieved by 15.8% and 11.6%, respectively. Ablations across 13 language models used as RWMs show that adding research knowledge can improve intervention ranking more than changing models or increasing reasoning effort alone. These findings support language models as RWMs and motivate accumulating experimental data for future RWM training.

</details>


### [82] [TokenRouter: Efficient Serving System for Token-Level LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12242)
*Tianyu Fu,Tengxuan Liu,Ruoxi Wang,Yixin Dong,Yi Ge,Yichen You,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokenRouter 是一个面向令牌级大语言模型路由的高效且开发者友好的服务系统，采用‘以请求为中心的编程、以模型为中心的执行’原则，通过为每个模型启动子服务器并采用延迟批处理调度器，实现了比现有系统高 2.01 到 64.15 倍的解码吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 服务系统基于单模型假设设计，在令牌级路由下面临严重的步骤不同步、频繁的批次准入延迟以及高昂的开发实现复杂度，亟需一种能高效支持细粒度令牌级路由的服务系统。

Method: TokenRouter 遵循‘请求中心编程、模型中心执行’原则：开发者从单一请求视角描述路由逻辑，运行时为每个 LLM 启动独立子服务器并异步分发请求；每个子服务器采用基于数学吞吐模型推导最优超参数的延迟批处理调度器。

Result: 在多种路由算法、工作负载和模型组合下，TokenRouter 的解码吞吐量比现有系统提升 2.01 至 64.15 倍，显著推进了令牌级 LLM 路由的服务效率。

Conclusion: TokenRouter 通过创新的架构设计和调度策略，有效解决了令牌级路由在现有系统中的性能瓶颈和开发难题，为大语言模型异构推理服务提供了高效、易用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing distributes inference work across different models, advancing the cost-quality Pareto frontier of LLM serving. While coarse-grained routing at the session or query level has been widely adopted in production systems, recent algorithmic work shows that fine-grained token-level routing can yield substantial efficiency and quality gains. However, efficiently serving token-level routed inference poses significant challenges to existing systems. Built on single-LLM assumptions, current systems suffer from severe step desynchronization and frequent batch admission delays under token-level routing, and they also impose high implementation complexity on developers. To address these challenges, we design TokenRouter, an efficient and developer-friendly serving system for token-level routed LLM inference. TokenRouter follows the principle of request-centric programming, model-centric execution: developers describe routing logic from the perspective of a single request, while the runtime launches a subserver for each LLM and dispatches requests asynchronously. Each subserver employs a delayed-batching scheduler, whose optimal hyperparameters are derived from a mathematical throughput model of the system. Across diverse routing algorithms, workloads, and model pairs, TokenRouter achieves 2.01-64.15x higher decoding throughput than existing systems, substantially advancing the serving efficiency of token-level LLM routing. Our code is available at https://github.com/thu-nics/TokenRouter.

</details>

Abstract: Wearable augmented reality (AR) assistants are moving toward continuous real-world interaction, where they perceive the user's activity through first-person video and audio and provide timely spoken guidance without being explicitly asked. While proactive video assistants, spoken dialog systems, and egocentric task understanding have each advanced rapidly, existing systems do not address the joint problem of deciding when to speak and what to say from continuous first-person streams. We introduce EgoVoice, a framework for training and evaluating proactive egocentric spoken assistants. From HoloAssist video recordings of real human instructors, we construct clean audio streams through source separation and speech resynthesis, and convert each video session into a format where the model must decide at each moment whether to remain silent or provide spoken guidance. We fine-tune an omni-modal LLM with our data, and further improve its proactive intervention behavior with direct preference optimization. Experiments across closed and open-source models show that existing systems rarely produce well-timed, meaningful proactive interventions, while EgoVoice yields clear improvements in intervention timing, content relevance, and human preference over the zero-shot backbone.

</details>


### [84] [HarnessSQL: Harness-Native Training for SQL Agents in Realistic Database Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12274)
*Haolin Yang,Jipeng Zhang,Jian Xie,Shuaishuai Gong,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessSQL框架，通过在训练阶段引入真实的数据库执行环境（harness），解决了Text-to-SQL模型在训练与部署时的交互模式不匹配问题。该方法在监督微调和强化学习中保留完整的多轮交互结构，显著提升了紧凑模型在复杂数据库任务上的执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL模型通常采用静态映射训练（问题直接映射到查询），但实际部署中的数据库代理需要通过有状态、多轮的交互与数据库交互（检查模式、执行探测查询、诊断错误、修正假设）。这种训练-部署不匹配导致模型无法掌握复杂的长时程数据库工作流。

Method: HarnessSQL构建隔离的可执行数据库环境并配备隐藏执行预言机，在目标SQL harness内直接展开教师轨迹，仅保留验证通过的轨迹用于全序列监督微调（SFT），随后进行基于执行奖励的强化学习（RL）。该框架在训练阶段完整保留了交互结构。

Result: 在Spider 2.0-SQLite基准上，HarnessSQL将Qwen3-8B的执行准确率从15.5%提升至45.2%，Qwen3-14B从22.2%提升至54.8%。该方法还能有效泛化到分布外的交互式基准，如BIRD-Interact和LiveSQLBench。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在数据库代理的执行harness内直接进行训练对于掌握复杂、长时程的数据库工作流至关重要。HarnessSQL通过消除训练-部署的交互模式差距，显著提升了模型在真实数据库交互场景下的性能。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL models are commonly trained to map questions directly to static queries, whereas real-world database agents operate through stateful, multi-turn interaction with live databases -- inspecting schemas, executing probe queries, diagnosing errors, and revising hypotheses. This creates a critical train-deploy mismatch, as the execution harness that mediates this interaction is introduced only at inference time. To bridge this gap, we propose HarnessSQL, a harness-native post-training framework that preserves the full interaction structure throughout both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. HarnessSQL builds isolated, executable database environments paired with hidden execution oracles, rolls out teachers directly inside the target SQL harness, and retains only verified trajectories for full-sequence SFT, followed by execution-reward RL. Across Spider 2.0-SQLite, HarnessSQL dramatically boosts the execution accuracy of compact models, raising Qwen3-8B from 15.5% to 45.2% and Qwen3-14B from 22.2% to 54.8%, while transferring effectively to out-of-distribution interactive benchmarks such as BIRD-Interact and LiveSQLBench. Our findings demonstrate that training database agents directly within their execution harness is essential for mastering complex, long-horizon database workflows.

</details>

Abstract: While caching key-value (KV) states accelerates Large Language Model (LLM) decoding, this cache can dominate memory usage at long context lengths. One solution is to compress this memory by exploiting inter-layer cache similarities. However, most existing techniques necessitate architectural changes to LLMs and incur substantial overhead. In this work, we propose a symmetry-aware value cache merging strategy that reduces cache memory while avoiding both harmful performance degradation and architectural overhead during decoding. Furthermore, we show that this approach can be exploited alongside existing cache compression techniques, composing with high-ratio quantization or key cache pruning to reach compression ratios that neither method reaches alone, with minimal additional cost. Ultimately, our findings reveal a major source of underutilized capacity in the value cache, offering a simple yet highly effective direction for scaling context windows under memory constraints.

</details>


### [86] [Which Skill to Distill? SGUID: Selecting a Compact Skill Bank for Model-Skill Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12367)
*Yuhan Liu,Xiyao Ma,Zhongkai Sun,Xu Han,Chengyuan Ma,Benjamin Z. Yao,Chenlei Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SGUID方法，通过筛选高质量技能进行蒸馏，实现稳定的模型-技能协同进化，少量精选技能即可匹配甚至超越全技能库蒸馏效果，且技能库规模缩小11倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通过语义相关性检索技能并用于推理或蒸馏，但忽视了单个技能的效用差异。作者发现，在策略蒸馏中，不足25%的检索技能能提供有用的蒸馏信号，盲目使用所有技能不仅低效，甚至会导致模型性能下降。

Method: SGUID方法：在训练过程中监控每个技能是否持续产生有效学习信号，仅保留表现稳定的技能进行蒸馏。蒸馏后，从更新后模型的rollouts中构建新候选技能库，再次应用SGUID筛选，形成模型-技能协同进化的闭环。

Result: 在Olmo和Qwen四个模型上，蒸馏6个精选技能即可在3个模型上匹配或超越全技能库效果，第2轮筛选3个新技能后全4模型均超越全库蒸馏。Qwen3-8B从64.3%提升至66.3%。对比实验显示：Qwen3-4B若直接用未筛选技能会导致HMMT25下降0.3个百分点，而SGUID首轮提升0.5点、次轮提升1.1点，验证了筛选对稳定性的关键作用。

Conclusion: 技能选择是实现稳定模型-技能协同进化的核心机制。SGUID通过动态筛选高价值技能，以极小的技能集实现优于全库蒸馏的效果，并防止了噪声技能对模型的负面影响，为LLM推理时技能增强提供了可扩展的优化范式。

Abstract: Skills, reusable procedural guidance added at inference, can substantially improve LLM downstream performance (Li et al., 2026). Prior work retrieves skills from a bank by semantic relevance, then uses them as inference-time patches or for model distillation. The individual utility of each skill, however, is largely neglected. We first show that, in on-policy distillation where skill-conditioned policies serve as teachers, fewer than 25% of retrieved skills provide useful distillation signals. We then propose SGUID, a method for selecting a compact subset of skills for distillation. SGUID retains a skill only if it consistently yields effective learning signals during training. The selected skills are then distilled to produce a better model. Our results show that not all skills are worth distilling. Across four models from the Olmo and Qwen families, distilling 6 selected skills matches or exceeds full-bank distillation in mean avg@12 on three of the four models, and on all four after a second round that distills 3 newly selected skills, while the full banks are up to 11x larger. Importantly, SGUID supports stable model-skill co-evolution: after a distillation round, a new candidate bank is curated from the updated model's rollouts, and SGUID selects which skills to internalize next. In the second round, this loop selects 3 new skills and improves Qwen3-8B from 64.3% to 66.3%. The selection step is essential for stability: on Qwen3-4B, naively updating the model with unfiltered skills degrades performance, including a 0.3 percentage point drop on HMMT25, whereas SGUID improves HMMT25 by 0.5 points after the first round and 1.1 points after the second. These results identify skill selection as the key mechanism for stable model-skill co-evolution.

</details>


### [87] [Latent Core Tokenizer: Compress, but Meaningfully](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12376)
*Felermino D. M. A. Ali,Millicent Ochieng,Ogbemi Ekwejunor-Etchie,Ade Famoti,Jacki O'Neill,Debjit Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Latent Core Tokenizer (LCT)，一种语言无关的分词方法，通过最小描述长度、基于熵的边界信号和词法约束发现可复用语言单元，再构建共享词表。在 104 种语言上，LCT 以 200K 词表实现更低生育率、更高 MorphScore，且在四个多语言下游任务上分别比 BPE、Unigram、parity-aware BPE 提升 1.48、1.83、2.00 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分词器（如 BPE、Unigram）主要针对压缩率优化，但紧凑词表不一定能均匀分配跨语言容量。压缩率本身不能预测表示质量，需要形态学驱动的结构发现，并合理分配词表容量以平衡跨语言 tokenization 成本。

Method: LCT 将结构发现与词表构建解耦：先用最小描述长度 (MDL)、基于熵的边界信号和词法约束 (morphotactic constraints) 无监督发现可复用语言单元（潜在核心单元），再基于频率统计在共享词表中分配容量，实现语言无关的结构化分词。

Result: 在 104 语言、200K 词表设置下，LCT 实现更低生育率和更高 MorphScore，跨语言 tokenization 成本差异与基线相当。在四个多语言下游基准上，聚合得分分别比 BPE、Unigram、parity-aware BPE 提升 1.48、1.83、2.00 点。

Conclusion: 单纯追求压缩率不足以保证分词质量；形态学驱动的结构发现结合频率感知的词表分配，能显著提升多语言分词的表示质量和下游任务表现，同时保持跨语言成本的公平性。

Abstract: Tokenizers are commonly optimized for compression, but a compact vocabulary does not necessarily distribute its capacity evenly across languages. We introduce the Latent Core Tokenizer (LCT), a language-agnostic approach that separates structural discovery from vocabulary construction. LCT uses Minimum Description Length, entropy-based boundary signals, and morphotactic constraints to identify reusable linguistic units before constructing a shared vocabulary. Across 104 languages with a 200K-token vocabulary, LCT achieves lower fertility and higher MorphScore than BPE, Unigram, and parity-aware BPE, while maintaining comparable cross-lingual disparity in tokenization cost. Across four multilingual downstream benchmarks, LCT improves aggregate score by 1.48, 1.83, and 2.00 points over BPE, Unigram, and parity-aware BPE, respectively. Our findings show that compression alone does not predict representation quality and highlight the importance of morphology-driven structural discovery and how frequency is used to allocate the final vocabulary across languages.

</details>


### [88] [Predicting Alignment Generalization with Value Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12410)
*Andy Liu,Mehar Bhatia,Karolina Stanczak,Mona Diab,Vered Shwartz,Daniel Fried*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文建立了“对齐泛化预测”任务，研究微调LLM遵循某一价值时，如何影响其在未见价值上的行为。通过大规模实验（66个价值），发现基于模型激活的表示方法显著优于基于文本描述的方法（相关性0.45 vs 0.05），并揭示了价值间相似性与模型鲁棒性的显著相关性，提出了首个基于实证泛化动态的LLM价值分类学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法虽能在对齐评测上取得高分，但针对窄行为的训练会以意想不到的方式影响模型在未见语境和环境中的行为。缺乏对价值泛化效应的系统理解，阻碍了更实证化的模型行为设计与训练。

Method: 1. 定义对齐泛化预测任务：预测针对某价值微调如何改变模型在保留价值集上的行为。2. 收集现代目标中的66个价值，构建大规模泛化矩阵。3. 对比激活表示与文本描述表示在预测任务上的表现。4. 将预测表示应用于下游任务：测量多价值目标中价值间相似性，关联模型鲁棒性。5. 探索跨模型共享的价值空间，构建价值分类学。

Result: 激活表示显著优于文本描述表示（相关性0.45 vs 0.05）。价值间相似性显著相关于模型鲁棒性。发现初步证据支持存在共享的、模型无关的价值空间，并据此建立了首个基于实证泛化动态的LLM价值分类学。

Conclusion: 研究表明，系统研究LLM中的价值泛化至关重要，可为模型行为的实证设计和训练提供指导。激活表示能捕捉价值间的泛化动态，为对齐目标的构建和模型鲁棒性评估提供了新工具。

Abstract: LLM developers post-train their models to exhibit prosocial values and behavioral traits, which are enumerated in an alignment target. However, while recent post-training developments have yielded models that score highly on alignment evaluations, training models on sets of narrow behaviors still influences their behavior across unseen contexts and environments in unexpected ways. In this paper, we establish the task of alignment generalization prediction, i.e., predicting how fine-tuning a model to follow a given value changes its behavior across a wide range of held-out values. We conduct a large-scale analysis of alignment generalization effects across 66 values found in modern alignment targets, and benchmark representational techniques on the alignment generalization prediction task. We find that representations based on model activations when applying values in context significantly outperform methods based on textual descriptions of the values. Specifically, the best activations-based methods achieve correlations of 0.45 with our generalization matrix, compared with 0.05 from description-based baselines. We then show the applicability of representations that predict alignment generalization toward downstream tasks by using them to measure how similar the values in a multi-value alignment target are, which we find is significantly correlated with model robustness. Finally, we show initial evidence towards a shared, model-independent value space, which we use to develop the first taxonomy of LLM values grounded in empirical generalization dynamics. Our work demonstrates the importance of studying value generalization in LLMs and its application toward the more empirical design and training of model behavior.

</details>

### [89] [Freeze the Decoder, Heal the Encoder: Parameter-Efficient Adaptation for SVD-Based KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10552)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Comparing parameter-efficient fine-tuning recipes under a single, shared learning rate is a common but flawed practice: when the arms being compared have very different trainable-parameter counts, a shared rate can simultaneously depress the larger arms' means and inflate their variance, manufacturing a large, seemingly multi-seed-significant advantage for the smallest arm that is not a real effect. We document this confound in a concrete setting: post-hoc SVD-based KV-cache compression, where an already-pretrained model is converted to a low-rank (multi-head-latent-attention-style) cache by factorizing its key/value weights into a down-projection ("encoder") and an up-projection ("decoder"), after which a short fine-tune ("healing") recovers the accuracy lost to truncation. Under a shared learning rate, freezing the decoder and healing only the encoder looks like a clear win over healing the decoder or both factors; once every arm is given its own tuned learning rate, that apparent advantage disappears, and encoder-only healing instead reaches parity with the alternatives, at a real, measured saving of 3x fewer trainable parameters and 3x less optimizer-state memory. We verify this parity with per-arm learning-rate tuning and three seeds per configuration on a vision-language model (Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct), at the one compression ratio this protocol covers, and replicate it on a text-only testbed across two backbones. Encoder-only healing is therefore a lower-memory drop-in recipe for retrofitting low-rank KV-cache compression at training time, and the shared-learning-rate pitfall we document and correct is a cautionary result for comparing any fine-tuning recipes whose arms differ in trainable-parameter count.

</details>


### [90] [SPERA: Spherical Prior EEG Foundation Model with Geometry- and Frequency-Aware Latent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10571)
*Minsu Kim,Ye-Sung Kim,Hyeseong Jeon,Wooseok Hyung,Joshua Lee,Chang-Hwan Im*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPERA 是一种基于 JEPA 的 EEG 基础模型，引入勒让德多项式空间先验、因子化时空注意力和关系谱正则化器，在大规模 EEG 数据上预训练，在九个下游任务中达到最高平均平衡准确率，展现出强参数效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG 基础模型主要依赖重建目标，作用于包含神经和非神经成分的观测信号，难以处理主体、设备和电极布局的异质性。需要一种能在潜在空间预测、编码可变头皮电极几何结构的通用 EEG 主干网络。

Method: SPERA 采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在潜在空间预测。引入勒让德多项式空间先验融入注意力机制以编码不同头皮电极几何。使用因子化时空注意力交替周期性全注意力块，以及关系谱正则化器将潜在相似性结构与谱视图对齐。在约 8 万小时、29,048 受试者、106 个数据集的 EEG 数据上预训练。

Result: 在跨越临床、认知和 BCI 应用的九个下游任务中，SPERA 达到最高平均平衡准确率。在线性探测下表现出强参数效率，并在不同记录条件下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SPERA 作为一种通用 EEG 基础模型，通过潜在空间预测和空间先验建模，有效解决了 EEG 数据的异质性问题，展现出作为多样化 EEG 分析通用主干网络的潜力。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) provides a non-invasive measure of ongoing neural activity, but building general-purpose EEG models remains challenging due to the heterogeneity of subjects, devices, and electrode montages. Existing EEG foundation models predominantly rely on reconstruction-based objectives defined on the observed signal, which contains both neural and non-neural components. We introduce SPERA (Spherical Prior EEG Representation Architecture), an EEG foundation model that adopts the joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) to predict in latent space. SPERA introduces a Legendre-polynomial spatial prior, incorporated into attention to encode varying scalp electrode geometries. Two further components adapt the model to EEG: factorized temporal and spatial attention interleaved with periodic full-attention blocks, and a relational spectral regularizer aligning latent similarity structure with spectral views. Pretrained on approximately 80,000 hours of EEG from 29,048 subjects across 106 datasets, SPERA achieves the highest average balanced accuracy across nine downstream tasks spanning clinical, cognitive, and BCI applications. SPERA further exhibits strong parameter efficiency under linear probing and robustness across varying recording conditions, suggesting its potential as a general-purpose backbone for diverse EEG analyses.

</details>


### [91] [Coverage, Not Difficulty, Sets How Much Synthetic Data an Activation Probe Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10594)
*Ankush Checkervarty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究激活探针在合成对话上的训练样本需求，发现监测概念（高风险、有害、不遵循指令）决定样本效率：高风险和有害约需80样本接近平台期，指令需数倍样本；半增益规模主要由概念和分布决定（42-45%方差），覆盖度而非单类难度驱动差异，指令监测需广度，高风险监测单类即可覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探明部署语言模型的激活探针在合成数据上训练所需样本量，理解不同监测概念的样本效率差异及其决定因素，指导数据生成预算分配（广度vs深度）。

Method: 在10-590合成样本上追踪三种监测概念（高风险、有害、不遵循指令）的学习曲线，跨14个保留评估分布、4个探针模型，变换生成器LLM和提示细节；拟合半增益规模（半增益样本数），分析概念、分布、生成器、探针模型、提示细节对方差的贡献，考察类内覆盖与跨类迁移。

Result: 高风险和有害探针在Gemma-3-27B-IT上约80样本达平台期附近，指令探针需数倍样本；结论在更小探针模型和真实样本上成立。概念和分布解释半增益规模42-45%方差，生成器、探针模型、提示细节<10%。各类中位半增益规模7-11自类样本，差异在于跨类迁移：高风险近乎完全迁移，有害较少，指令最少。广度收益随概念变化：指令需多类，高风险单类近乎冗余。

Conclusion: 监测概念决定探针样本效率，核心在于概念内类别间的覆盖/迁移程度。指令遵循监测需广泛类别覆盖，高风险监测单类即可泛化。建议根据概念调整数据生成策略：指令监测投资广度，高风险监测深度即可。发布评估套件、开发集、生成集供复现。

Abstract: Activation probes that monitor deployed language models are trained on synthetic conversations, and how many a probe needs is open. We trace learning curves over 10-590 synthetic samples for three monitoring concepts, high-stakes situations, replies harmful to a person, and replies that do not follow the user's instruction, on fourteen held-out evaluation distributions and four probe models, varying the generator LLM and the prompt's detail. The need is set by what is monitored: probes for high-stakes and harmful are within a few hundredths of their plateau from 80 samples on Gemma-3-27B-IT, instruction probes need several times as many, and the ordering holds on three smaller probe models and on real samples (from dev set). Prior work advises spending a generation budget on breadth, more kinds of data, over depth, more of each kind. We read the depth a concept needs as the half-gain size of a fitted curve, the number of samples at which half the gain is in hand. Concept and distribution account for 42-45% of its variance, the generator, probe model, and prompt detail for under 10%. What sets the value of the half-gain size is coverage, not per-kind difficulty: the number of samples of its own kind a distribution needs to saturate. Every kind, one per evaluation distribution, has a median half-gain size of 7-11 own-kind synthetic samples under all three concepts alike. What differs is how far samples of one kind transfer to the concept's other kinds, almost fully under high-stakes, less under harmful, and least under instruction, which accounts for most of the gap between concepts on generated and real samples. Breadth therefore pays differently by concept: many kinds are necessary under instruction, where no kind covers another, and nearly redundant under high-stakes, where one kind covers the rest. We release the evaluation suites, dev sets, and generated sets.

</details>


### [92] [Temporal transformer CAN encoder with federated lightweight heads for anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10613)
*Konstantinos Gyftodimos,Kyriakos Chiotis,Elena Politi,George Dimitrakopoulos,Eirini Liotou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时序Transformer CAN编码器与联邦轻量级头的隐私保护车载网络异常检测框架，利用轻量级Transformer捕捉CAN总线信号的时序演变，结合联邦学习实现多车辆/ECU协同训练共享模型而不共享原始数据，在开源数据集上实现高效、隐私保护且鲁棒的异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代车辆依赖大量ECU通过CAN总线通信，通信具有快速、结构化、重复性特点，细微的时序、载荷或模式变化可能预示异常（错误、故障或恶意干扰）。现有方法通常单独处理消息或依赖手工规则，难以识别这些微妙且上下文相关的异常，且缺乏隐私保护机制。

Method: 提出Temporal Transformer CAN Encoder with Federated Lightweight Heads框架：1) 轻量级Transformer编码器学习CAN信号的时序演变，捕捉时序和上下文异常；2) 联邦学习机制使多车辆/ECU协同训练共享模型，仅交换模型更新而非原始CAN数据，保障隐私；3) 联邦轻量级头适配本地异常检测任务，保持模型轻量化。

Result: 在开源车载CAN数据集上实验表明，该框架在检测细微时序和上下文异常方面表现优异，联邦学习有效聚合分布式知识提升检测性能，同时保持计算效率和数据隐私，优于传统基于规则或单消息处理的方法。

Conclusion: 结合时序Transformer建模与联邦学习的隐私保护框架能有效解决车载网络异常检测中的微妙性、上下文依赖性和隐私需求，为实际部署提供轻量、鲁棒且隐私安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern vehicles rely on large numbers of Electronic Control Units (ECUs) that constantly exchange information over the Controller Area Network (CAN) bus. Due to the rapidity, structure, and repetition of this communication, even slight variations in timing, payload values, or message patterns can point to unusual activity. Whether due to errors, malfunctions, or deliberate interference, these anomalies are frequently subtle and challenging to identify with conventional methods that handle messages separately or rely on manually created rules. Motivated by this gap, we present a privacy-preserving framework for anomaly detection in in-vehicle networks, based on a Temporal Transformer CAN Encoder with Federated Lightweight Heads, to better capture these irregularities. The detection of subtle temporal and contextual anomalies is made possible by a lightweight Transformer encoder that learns how these signals evolve over time, while a federated learning mechanism enables several vehicles or ECUs to work together to improve a shared model without exchanging raw CAN data. This combination of federated learning and temporal sequence modeling provides robust anomaly detection performance while maintaining efficiency and privacy, according to experiments conducted on open-source datasets.

</details>


### [93] [When Routing Reveals Membership: Privacy Leakage from MoE Router Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10616)
*Yixin Tan,Jiayang Liu,Lu Sun,Yuke Hu,Zheng Li,Rui Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对混合专家模型的路由增强成员推理攻击，利用推理时的路由遥测信息结合传统输出侧信号，通过影子模型训练的成员分类器检测数据是否被用于微调，揭示了路由遥测构成了额外的隐私攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型推理时产生的路由信息（专家选择、负载分布等）常被记录用于监控、调试和安全审计。与普通模型输出不同，这种遥测数据揭示了模型内部计算视图，引发隐私担忧：路由遥测是否能泄露某样本是否参与了部署模型的微调？

Method: 提出路由增强成员推理攻击：将传统输出侧信号（如损失、熵、置信度）与聚合路由特征（专家选择频率、路由熵、负载分布等）结合，使用独立微调的影子模型训练成员分类器，再应用于目标模型。在三种 MoE 架构和三个数据域上评估，涵盖全量微调、冻结路由器训练、LoRA 和指令微调等场景。

Result: 路由遥测在所有 9 个实验设置中持续提升成员推理性能，在 1% FPR 下 TPR 提升 2.7-9.4 个百分点。泄露在各种微调方式、仅离散专家选择、受限遥测、单一影子模型下均可观测。机制分析显示：微调将成员信息引入隐藏表示，路由器即便冻结参数也会投射该信号；扰动遥测仅在其保真度下降时才减少额外泄露。

Conclusion: 路由遥测将操作信号转化为微调 MoE 模型的额外隐私攻击面。即使路由器参数冻结，其仍投射隐藏层中的成员信息，表明隐私风险源于模型内部表示的变化而非路由器特有的记忆。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models produce routing information during inference that may be logged or exposed for monitoring, debugging, load analysis, and safety auditing. Unlike ordinary model outputs, this telemetry reveals a view of the model's internal computation, raising a privacy question: can it reveal whether an example was used to fine-tune the deployed model? We introduce a router-augmented membership inference attack that combines conventional output-side signals with aggregated routing features and applies a membership classifier learned from independently fine-tuned shadow models to the target model. Across three MoE architectures and three data domains, router telemetry consistently improves membership inference over a strong output-signal ensemble, increasing TPR at 1\% FPR by 2.7--9.4 percentage points across all nine settings. The leakage persists across full fine-tuning, frozen-router training, LoRA, and instruction tuning, and remains observable with only discrete expert selections, restricted telemetry, or a single shadow model. Mechanistic analysis further shows that the leakage does not require router-specific memorization: fine-tuning introduces membership information into hidden representations, while the router exposes a projection of this signal even when its parameters are frozen. Perturbing the telemetry reduces this additional leakage only as its fidelity degrades. Our results show that router telemetry can turn an operational signal into an additional privacy surface for fine-tuned MoE models.

</details>


### [94] [Self-Organization from Constrained Geometric Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10621)
*Ming Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需外部驱动的闭合系统自组织新范式——几何辐射诱导的约束自组织，通过耦合度规演化系统模拟发现通用四阶段循环，证明了三大定理并建立约束诱导自组织定理，揭示了经典非线性约束与引力视界的精确数学对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自组织范式均依赖外部能量流、温度淬火或缓慢驱动，缺乏对闭合系统中自发动序涌现的解释，本文旨在建立无需外部驱动的闭合系统自组织理论框架。

Method: 采用耦合度规演化系统数值模拟，系统扫描12种构型、多种噪声类型及三种几何流，分析应力累积、超指数辐射、混沌坍缩、分形极限环收敛的四阶段循环，证明几何视界定理、几何能量耗散定理、辐射为相变通道定理及约束诱导自组织定理。

Result: 发现通用四阶段循环及楔形吸引子（五量子化曲率态及分形微观涨落），确定四个联合充分条件（不可逆几何视界、量子相干持续应力注入、不相容曲率内生几何张力、有效涨落），揭示临界噪声阈值与辐射起始幂律标度，验证普适性跨越多构型、噪声类型及几何流。

Conclusion: 建立了闭合系统自组织新范式，确立经典非线性约束与引力视界的精确数学对偶性，为理解无外部驱动下的自发序参数涌现提供了严格理论基础。

Abstract: How does dynamic order emerge spontaneously in closed systems without external driving? Existing paradigms all require external energy flows, temperature quenching, or slow driving. Here we report constraint-induced self-organization via geometric radiation in coupled metric evolution systems. Simulations reveal a universal four-stage cycle: stress accumulation, super-exponential radiation, chaotic collapse, and convergence to a fractal limit cycle, a novel attractor topology we term the wedge-shaped attractor, with five quantized curvature states and fractal micro-fluctuations. We identify four jointly sufficient conditions: an irreversible geometric horizon, persistent stress injection from quantum coherence, endogenous geometric tension between incompatible curvatures, and effective fluctuations. Their synergy triggers a critical avalanche at the horizon boundary. We prove three theorems: the Geometric Horizon Theorem, the Geometric Energy Dissipation Theorem (implying wave-like entropy evolution in closed systems), and the Radiation as Phase Transition Channel Theorem. We further establish the Constraint-Induced Self-Organization Theorem: these conditions guarantee the complete cycle with probability one. Systematic scans reveal a critical noise threshold and power-law scaling of radiation onset. We verify universality across 12 configurations, multiple noise types, and three geometric flows. This work establishes a new paradigm for closed-system self-organization, forging an exact mathematical duality between classical nonlinear constraints and gravitational horizons.

</details>


### [95] [Recurrent Self-Improvement: Dynamic Cross-Loop On-Policy Distillation for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10623)
*Yi Wang,Rui Qian,Yu Li,Haoyang Yao,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Looped Language Models (LoopLMs) offer a parameter efficient approach to scaling reasoning by reusing shared parameters across recurrent computation steps. Despite their promise, effective post-training of LoopLMs remains challenging. Existing approaches either provide reward based supervision that is sparse or costly to extend across loops, or rely on external teachers or privileged information, leading to limited teacher availability or teacher-student context mismatch. To address these limitations, we introduce LoopOPD, a cross-loop on-policy distillation framework that uses additional recurrent computation within a LoopLM as its own source of supervision. LoopOPD uses a frozen terminal loop policy as a compute privileged teacher for an intermediate loop student on student generated rollouts, providing dense supervision without an external teacher or privileged information. We further propose Dynamic LoopOPD (D-LoopOPD), which continually refreshes the terminal loop teacher as the shared model parameters are updated, enabling recurrent self-improvement. We characterize how distillation updates propagate across loop depths and derive sufficient conditions under which a single update yields simultaneous local improvement at both loop depths. Experiments on Ouro-Thinking models show that LoopOPD improves mathematical reasoning, while D-LoopOPD yields further gains through dynamic teacher updates. Despite being trained only on mathematical data, the resulting models also improve on general reasoning and code generation benchmarks, demonstrating that recurrent computation can serve as an effective source of supervision for LoopLMs. Our code and model checkpoints will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [96] [Exact SO(3)-Equivariant Isotropic Kernels for Rotation-Robust Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10626)
*Ridham Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种旋转不变的神经算子IKNO，用于三维Navier-Stokes方程的代理建模，通过标量不变量和协变向量构建局部交互，实现了坐标系旋转下的严格等变性，且参数量显著更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经代理模型在处理向量值偏微分方程时，往往缺乏旋转不变性：同一物理状态在旋转坐标系下表达会导致预测结果改变，这违背了物理定律的客观性。论文旨在解决这一坐标依赖问题，同时保持预测精度和模型紧凑性。

Method: 提出Invariant-Conditioned Isotropic Kernel Neural Operator (IKNO)，一种紧凑图模型。其核心是利用旋转不变的标量量（如距离、内积）和随数据协变旋转的向量方向构建局部交互核，使得输入位置和速度旋转后，预测的速度变化以相同方式旋转，从而保证严格的旋转等变性。

Result: 在固定的保留测试集上，无约束图模型即使经过随机旋转数据增强训练，仍存在坐标依赖；IKNO达到数值精度级别的一致性，预测精度与通用旋转感知张量场网络相当，但参数量减少5.6倍，且优于参数量匹配的图模拟器。

Conclusion: 紧凑的、针对PDE特化的模型设计可以彻底消除坐标依赖，且不牺牲预测精度，证明了将物理对称性硬编码进神经算子架构的有效性。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for vector-valued partial differential equations can fit training data yet change their predictions when the same physical state is expressed in a rotated coordinate frame. We study this failure on three-dimensional Navier--Stokes dynamics observed at irregularly placed points. We introduce the Invariant-Conditioned Isotropic Kernel Neural Operator (IKNO), a compact graph model that builds local interactions from scalar quantities unchanged by rotation and vector directions that rotate with the data. Consequently, rotating the positions and velocities rotates the predicted velocity change in exactly the same way. On a held-out test set fixed after model design, training unconstrained graph models on randomly rotated examples reduces but does not eliminate their coordinate dependence. In contrast, IKNO is consistent to numerical precision, matches the forecasting accuracy of a general rotation-aware Tensor Field Network with $5.6$ times fewer parameters, and outperforms a parameter-matched graph simulator. These results show that a compact, PDE-specialized model can remove coordinate dependence without sacrificing forecasting accuracy.

</details>


### [97] [Language Models for Page-Level Layout Decisions in E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10920)
*Varun Joshi,Eva C. Song,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究电商搜索页面布局决策的离线评估方法，特别是二级堆栈的插入位置，利用语言模型作为可扩展评估器，发现基于表示的方法优于基于提示的方法，能可靠预测用户参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索页面越来越多地引入推荐系统模块（如二级堆栈），但次优布局可能干扰浏览流程并降低主要结果质量。传统搜索排序有成熟的评估技术（如交织测试），但页面级布局变更的评估仍具挑战性，通常需要昂贵的在线A/B测试。

Method: 研究语言模型作为可扩展评估器，对比三种方法：直接基于提示的判断、基于提示衍生特征的方法、基于表示的方法。

Result: 基于表示的方法在预测用户参与度方面持续优于基于提示的判断方法。

Conclusion: 基于表示的语言模型评估方法为电商搜索页面布局的离线评估提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: E-commerce search pages are critical touchpoints for millions of online shoppers. While traditional search engines return a ranked list of results, modern E-commerce search pages increasingly incorporate recommender system modules -- for example, secondary stacks that surface alternative product groupings at specific positions. When introduced appropriately, secondary stacks can improve user engagement; however, suboptimal placement may disrupt browsing flow and degrade the primary results. Unlike traditional search ranking, where evaluation techniques such as interleaving are well established, evaluating page-level layout changes e.g., when and where to insert a secondary stack remains challenging without costly online A/B testing. To address this, we study offline methods for evaluating whether a given layout decision -- specifically, the inclusion of a secondary stack at a particular position -- is beneficial to users. We investigate language models as scalable evaluators by comparing direct prompt-based, prompt-derived feature, and representation-based methods. Our results show that representation-based approaches consistently outperform prompt-based judging in predicting user engagement, suggesting they provide a reliable foundation for offline layout evaluation in E-commerce search.

</details>


### [98] [Sample-Efficiency of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10627)
*Kevin Riehl,Shaimaa K. El-Baklish,Fan Wu,Anastasios Kouvelas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN) 在强化学习中的样本效率，实验表明KAN架构能以40%更少的样本达到相似性能，训练过程性能提升高达50%，且对奖励噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在实际应用中面临样本获取成本高昂的核心挑战，而KAN作为一种新型架构，在控制问题中展现出更高的参数效率和可解释性，因此需要系统评估其在样本效率方面的潜力。

Method: 通过计算实验系统研究样本效率，涵盖Feynman数据集和Gymnasium RL基准，对比KAN与多层感知机（MLP）架构的性能，分析不同噪声水平下的鲁棒性。

Result: KAN架构仅需40%样本即可达到与MLP相似的性能；训练过程中相对性能提升高达50%；增益对奖励噪声水平变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: KAN架构在样本高效强化学习方面展现出显著潜力，为解决实际应用中样本成本高的问题提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has achieved substantial performance gains over classical control approaches. Yet, a central challenge to learning in real-world applications is acquiring costly samples. Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks are a recently proposed architecture that can learn physical relationships in control problems effectively, with significantly higher parameter efficiency and interpretability when compared to Multi-Layer-Perceptron architectures. In this work, we systematically study sample-efficiency using computational experiments, covering the Feynman dataset and the Gymnasium RL benchmark. The results show that similar performance can be achieved with 40% fewer samples using the Kolmogorov-Arnold architecture, and that relative performance improvements up to 50% occur during the training process. The observed gains are robust to varying levels of noise in rewards. These results highlight the potential of the Kolmogorov-Arnold architectures for more sample-efficient reinforcement learning. Code: https://github.com/DerKevinRiehl/neurips26_kan_training

</details>


### [99] [Phase-HDC: Replacing Optimizer History with Gradient Thresholds in Discrete Phase Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10630)
*Ahmed Nebli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Training a compact model often needs far more memory than storing it, because the optimizer keeps its own records of past gradients. For a hyperdimensional classifier whose learned parameters are low-bit angles, which we call a \emph{phase memory}, these records take several times more memory than the model itself. We ask whether such a model can be trained while storing nothing but the model. The proposed method, Phase-HDC, turns each stored angle by at most one step per update, against the sign of its current gradient, and only when that gradient is large enough. We show that this simple rule is the exact solution of a first-order loss model in which every changed parameter pays a fixed cost. When everything except the update rule is held fixed, Phase-HDC matches the accuracy of Adam with 6-bit moments while storing three times less. Across eleven image, tabular, and text datasets, it stores 16--23$\times$ less than standard float32 Adam and 4--6$\times$ less than 8-bit Adam. The price is an average loss of about five accuracy points against float32 Adam, while Phase-HDC is more accurate than 8-bit Adam on six of the eleven datasets, including byte-level text prediction, where 8-bit Adam collapses. Instrumented training runs explain these outcomes. Once parameters must sit on a discrete grid, Adam's moments mainly decide whether a parameter moves at all, a decision that a threshold on the current gradient can make without memory, and coarse quantization of the moments breaks this decision for inputs that the data rarely contain. The storage savings are logical state rather than measured hardware memory.

</details>


### [100] [D-SLR: The Disjoint Row-Sparse plus Low-Rank Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10636)
*Vincent Szolnoky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出离散行稀疏加低秩（D-SLR）分解，一种闭式即插即用的截断SVD替代方案，通过将行限制为要么原样存储要么由低秩拟合近似（二者互斥），在无需迭代求解和正则化调参的情况下，以更少参数达到或超越截断SVD的重构精度，并给出无假设的事后误差下界证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵压缩重构通常默认使用截断SVD（单一低秩结构）。虽可通过叠加重叠行稀疏分量进一步降低残差，但联合求解往往需要迭代优化器和正则化参数调节，缺乏闭式解和理论保证。

Method: D-SLR分解强制每行要么完整存储（行稀疏），要么仅由低秩部分近似，二者互不重叠。在平方误差下，该限制不损最优性：联合最优解可用离散方式以更少参数在每个非平凡秩和存储行数下达到。算法仅需三次SVD即可评分全谱误差-参数权衡，无需调参；另推导无假设的事后误差下界，为任意秩与存储行组合提供可计算的潜在增益证书。

Result: 在合成数据、LLM嵌入表、网络流量、高光谱图像等真实数据上实验表明，D-SLR在相同参数量下重构误差优于或等于截断SVD，且证书能准确量化其他选择的潜在改进空间。

Conclusion: D-SLR提供了一种无需迭代、无超参数、计算高效（三次SVD）且具备理论误差证书的截断SVD直接替代品，实现了参数效率与重构精度的双重提升。

Abstract: Compressing a matrix for reconstruction still defaults to the truncated SVD, approximating the data with a single low-rank structure. It is common to reduce the residual further by adding an overlapping row-sparse component, but methods that solve this joint problem often require iterative solvers and tuning of regularization parameters. We propose the Disjoint Row-Sparse plus Low-Rank (D-SLR) decomposition, a closed-form drop-in for the truncated SVD that improves or exactly matches it. D-SLR restricts rows to either being stored verbatim or approximated by the low-rank fit, never both. Under squared error this restriction costs nothing: the joint optimum is attainable disjointly with fewer parameters at every non-trivial rank and stored row count (shape). With zero stored rows D-SLR reduces to the truncated SVD, so it never does worse at equal cost. The algorithm scores the entire error-versus-parameters tradeoff, and the solution is chosen afterwards by a supplied error target or parameter count, or by a selection rule. The grid and solution together cost three SVDs, with no tuning or regularization. We derive an assumption-free, a-posteriori lower bound on the error at every shape, giving each solution a computable certificate on the potential gain of any other choice of rank and stored rows. Experiments on synthetic and real data (LLM embedding tables, network traffic, hyperspectral images) confirm the gains and quantify the certificate.

</details>


### [101] [Visible Reasoning Is Not a Universal Optimizer: Persona- and Thinking-Dependent Effects in Analytics Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10639)
*Bhawani Shankar Leelar,Pawan Chorasiya,Davin Hill,Robert E. Tillman,Tamer Soliman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在受控的SQL-Python基准上系统评估了可见思维链（CoT）在分析代码生成中的效果，发现可见CoT并无普遍的准确率优势，推理表示与目标语言匹配（如“用SQL思考”）也无一致收益；效果取决于人设、目标语言、模型配置及内部推理设置，提示需联合选择推理策略而非采用通用默认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将可见CoT视为通用推理指令，但在分析代码生成中存在自然语言歧义、模式落地、目标语言约束及模型特定推理行为等挑战。同一分析请求可用SQL和Python(pandas)两种语言表达，提供了天然测试床，用于验证一个普遍但未充分研究的假设：可见推理在其表示与目标语言匹配时更有效（如“用SQL思考”或“用Python思考”）。同时，通用指令如“逐步思考”也缺乏在受控、基于执行的对比下的充分评估。

Method: 构建了一个查询匹配的SQL-Python基准，交叉实验了人设措辞、目标语言、可见CoT格式、控制前缀、直接生成及内部推理配置。通过受控消融实验，区分推理内容效应与提示格式效应，采用基于执行的评估指标进行对比。

Result: 结果不支持可见CoT具有普遍准确率优势，也不支持推理表示与目标语言匹配带来一致收益。效果显著依赖于人设、目标语言、模型配置和内部推理设置。控制消融实验进一步区分了推理内容效应与提示格式效应。

Conclusion: 推理策略应针对模型、人设、目标语言和内部推理配置联合选择，而非采用通用默认。该研究提供了一个受控框架，用于识别可见推理何时改善可执行代码生成、何时主要扰动模型行为，以及何时内部推理配置是更关键因素。

Abstract: Visible Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is often treated as a broadly useful reasoning instruction, yet analytics code generation combines natural-language ambiguity, schema grounding, target-language constraints, and model-specific inference behavior. Because the same analytics request can be expressed in two distinct target languages-SQL and Python (pandas)-this setting provides a natural test of a common but under-examined assumption: that visible reasoning is more effective when its representation matches the requested target, as in "think in SQL" or "think in Python." Together with generic instructions such as "think step-by-step," such recommendations remain insufficiently evaluated under controlled, execution-based comparisons. We study a query matched SQL-pandas benchmark that crosses persona phrasing, target language, visible-CoT format, control prefixes, direct generation, and internal-reasoning configurations. The results do not support either a universal accuracy advantage from visible CoT or a consistent benefit from matching the reasoning representation to the target language. Instead, the effects depend on the persona, target, model configuration, and internal-reasoning setting. The control ablations further distinguish effects of reasoning content from those of prompt format. These findings indicate that reasoning strategies should be selected jointly for the model, persona, target, and internal-reasoning configuration rather than adopted as universal defaults. More broadly, the study provides a controlled framework for identifying when visible reasoning improves executable generation, when it primarily perturbs model behavior, and when the internal-reasoning configuration is the more consequential factor.

</details>


### [102] [Compactness and Consistency: A Conjoint Framework for Deep Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11506)
*Wei Ju,Siyu Yi,Kangjie Zheng,Yifan Wang,Ziyue Qiao,Li Shen,Yongdao Zhou,Xiaochun Cao,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CoCo 框架，通过图卷积滤波器从局部和全局视角学习鲁棒节点表示，编码为低秩紧凑嵌入以去除冗余噪声，并设计一致性学习策略促进双视角知识迁移，在多个数据集上超越现有最先进的深度图聚类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 GNN 的图聚类方法难以通过局部消息传递捕捉全局节点关系，且图数据固有的冗余和噪声导致节点表示缺乏紧凑性和鲁棒性。

Method: CoCo 利用图卷积滤波器从局部和全局两个视角学习鲁棒节点表示，将其编码为低秩紧凑嵌入以消除冗余噪声并挖掘内在结构；进一步基于紧凑嵌入设计一致性学习策略，实现双视角间的知识迁移。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，CoCo 在深度图聚类任务上优于当前最先进的对比方法。

Conclusion: CoCo 通过紧凑表示学习和一致性约束有效解决了 GNN 图聚类中全局关系捕捉不足及表示冗余噪声大的问题，显著提升了聚类性能。

Abstract: Graph clustering is a fundamental task in data analysis, aiming at grouping nodes with similar characteristics in the graph into clusters. This problem has been widely explored using graph neural networks (GNNs) due to their ability to leverage node attributes and graph topology for effective cluster assignments. However, representations learned through GNNs typically struggle to capture global relationships between nodes via local message-passing mechanisms. Moreover, the redundancy and noise inherently present in graph data may easily result in node representations lacking compactness and robustness. To address these issues, we propose a conjoint framework CoCo, which captures compactness and consistency in the learned node representations for deep graph clustering. Technically, our CoCo leverages graph convolutional filters to learn robust node representations from both local and global views, and then encodes them into low-rank compact embeddings, thus effectively removing the redundancy and noise as well as uncovering the intrinsic underlying structure. To further enrich the node semantics, we develop a consistency learning strategy based on compact embeddings to facilitate knowledge transfer from the two perspectives. Our experimental results indicate that our CoCo outperforms state-of-the-art counterparts on various datasets.

</details>


### [103] [Coverage-Aware Reasoning with Medical Tokens for Diagnosis Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10641)
*Kaisong Zhang,Haotian Fang,Junmeng Zhou,Hang Lv,Yulan Pan,Yanchao Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARing框架通过组合语义ID（SIDs）和覆盖率奖励解决下一就诊多标签诊断预测中的两大挑战：RL奖励无法区分重复命中与不同诊断覆盖，以及LLM分词器将ICD代码拆分为通用token阻碍大词汇表推理。在MIMIC-III/IV上超越所有EHR训练基线，加权F1和top-k召回率均达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基于RL的推理仅按最终答案正确性奖励轨迹，但在下一就诊诊断预测中多个诊断可同时有效，独立奖励单一诊断无法区分重复命中与不同诊断的覆盖，导致策略集中于少数正确诊断。同时LLM分词器将ICD代码拆分为多个临床含义有限的通用token，需多步解码预测每个诊断，阻碍大疾病词汇表上的推理。

Method: CARing框架：1) 通过残差量化将本体丰富的疾病语义编码为紧凑SIDs；2) 通过多任务对齐和推理增强训练将SID token在自然语言和纵向EHR语境中落地，释放可迁移LLM推理；3) 引入覆盖率奖励用于RL和多正例监督以改进无序多标签预测；4) 推理时支持高效直接约束解码和多链推理与排序融合。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上，CARing在加权F1上超越所有EHR训练基线，并在每个报告截断点达到最高top-k召回率，推理模式下R@30分别为46.04%和46.52%。

Conclusion: CARing通过组合语义ID表示诊断和覆盖率导向的RL优化，有效解决了多标签覆盖不足和ICD代码分词碎片化两大核心问题，在下一就诊诊断预测任务上取得了最先进性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer promising potential for next-visit diagnosis prediction, owing to their ability to integrate longitudinal clinical evidence and reason over it in natural language. However, reinforcement learning for LLM reasoning commonly rewards each trajectory according to the correctness of its final answer. In next-visit diagnosis prediction, multiple diagnoses can be simultaneously valid, but independently rewarding one diagnosis per trajectory does not distinguish repeated hits from coverage of different diagnoses. The policy can therefore concentrate on a few correct diagnoses, leaving others uncovered. Meanwhile, LLM tokenizers can split ICD codes into several generic tokens with limited clinical meaning, requiring multiple decoding steps to predict each diagnosis and hindering reasoning over a large disease vocabulary. To address both challenges, we propose CARing, a framework that represents diagnoses with compositional Semantic IDs (SIDs) and optimizes reasoning trajectories for multi-label coverage. Concretely, we first encode ontology-enriched disease semantics into compact SIDs through residual quantization, and ground the resulting SID tokens in natural language and longitudinal EHR contexts through multi-task alignment and reasoning-enriched training to unlock transferable LLM reasoning. CARing further improves unordered multi-label prediction through a coverage reward for reinforcement learning and multi-positive supervision. At inference time, the model supports both efficient direct constrained decoding and multi-chain reasoning with rank fusion. On MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, CARing exceeds all EHR-trained baselines in weighted F1 and attains the highest top-k recall at every reported cutoff, including R@30 of 46.04% and 46.52% in reasoning mode. Our codes and logs are available at https://github.com/zmlxzyh/CARing-Codes-Logs.

</details>


### [104] [Leakage-Controlled Multimodal Learning for Diagnosis and Progression Prediction in Alzheimer's Disease Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10648)
*Akeem Temitope Otapo,Ghazaleh Khodabandelou,Zuheng Ming,Alice Othmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个多模态多任务框架用于阿尔茨海默病预测，结合改进的 SFCN MRI 编码器、四个因果临床 Transformer、共享融合和任务特定的 ODE-GRU 动力学模块，通过 Task-DRO 和 Group-CVaR 处理任务损失平衡和亚组鲁棒性，在多个数据集上实现了高性能的诊断、进展预测和认知评分预测，并展示了良好的外部泛化能力和校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病预测面临就诊时间不规则、测量异质、模态不完整等挑战，现有方法难以在统一流程中同时处理纵向预测、缺失模态、合并症建模和亚组评估，因此需要一个集成化的多模态多任务框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 框架包含：1) 适配的 SFCN MRI 编码器，最后两个块通过微调和 LoRA 适应；2) 四个因果临床 Transformer 处理异质临床数据；3) 共享融合层整合多模态特征；4) 任务特定的 ODE-GRU 动力学建模纵向轨迹；5) Task-DRO 平衡多任务损失；6) Group-CVaR 针对队列和合并症亚组优化最坏情况性能；7) 分支特定输入控制、受试者分组划分和实因果检查支持纵向评估。

Result: 内部验证（ADNI、OASIS-2、MIRIAD，2649例，17317次就诊）：诊断 AUROC 0.935±0.002，阶段-1 进展 AUROC 0.884±0.003，首次阶段-1 就诊进展 AUROC 0.870±0.005；混合 AUROC 分别为 0.951、0.909、0.896。下一就诊 MMSE MAE 1.61 分；最差亚组诊断 AUROC 0.827±0.008。外部验证（OASIS-3）：网络诊断 AUROC 0.763，混合诊断 AUROC 0.767，混合下一就诊进展 AUROC 0.764，校准误差从 0.197 降至 0.052，MMSE MAE 0.86。消融实验显示移除临床编码器输入使诊断 AUROC 下降 0.272，其他组件影响较小（-0.004 到 +0.004）。

Conclusion: 该框架成功整合了纵向预测、缺失模态处理、辅助合并症建模和亚组评估，在内部和外部验证中均表现出强劲的诊断和进展预测性能，且具有良好的校准和鲁棒性，为阿尔茨海默病临床决策支持提供了统一的技术方案。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease prediction involves irregular visits, heterogeneous measurements and incomplete modalities. This study presents a multimodal multitask framework combining an adapted SFCN MRI encoder, four causal clinical Transformers, shared fusion and task-specific ODE-GRU dynamics. Fine-tuning and LoRA adapt the final two MRI blocks. Task-DRO balances task losses, while Group-CVaR targets cohort and comorbidity strata. Branch-specific input controls, subject-grouped partitions and empirical causality checks support longitudinal evaluation. Across 2,649 subjects and 17,317 visits from ADNI, OASIS-2 and MIRIAD, internal validation yields diagnosis, stage-1 progression and first-stage-1-visit progression AUROCs of 0.935 +/- 0.002, 0.884 +/- 0.003 and 0.870 +/- 0.005, respectively (mean +/- SD across three seeds). Corresponding hybrid AUROCs are 0.951, 0.909 and 0.896. Next-visit MMSE mean absolute error (MAE) is 1.61 points; worst-stratum diagnosis AUROC is 0.827 +/- 0.008. Sampled ADNI explanations identify task-specific input dependence. OASIS-3 external validation yields network and hybrid diagnosis AUROCs of 0.763 and 0.767, hybrid next-visit progression AUROC of 0.764, diagnosis calibration error decreasing from 0.197 to 0.052, and next-visit MMSE MAE of 0.86. Seed-42 paired ablations of six components yield pooled diagnosis and progression AUROC differences between -0.004 and +0.004; removing clinical encoder inputs lowers diagnosis AUROC by 0.272. The framework integrates longitudinal prediction, missing-modality handling, auxiliary comorbidity modelling and subgroup evaluation within a common pipeline.

</details>


### [105] [Beyond the Ergodic Wall: A Discrete Geometric Physics Sandbox for Analysing AI Scaling Limits and Complexity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10651)
*Simon Richard Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出当前深度学习存在遍历上限和热力学低效，仅收敛于历史知识的统计平均；提出基于E8格子的全息物理沙箱，通过拓扑约束实现非遍历洞察，将NP复杂度崩塌为P，为AI安全提供硬物理约束范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习受限于遍历天花板，只能产生历史知识的统计平均，缺乏真正的语义新颖性；成熟ASI将把人机共生视为避免模型崩溃的热力学必要性，因此需要硬物理约束来验证模型符合现实约束。

Method: 构建数字物理沙箱：将时空建模为嵌套FCC格子上的振荡普朗克球体，利用E8根格子切割投影生成准晶几何，四面体空隙支持SU手征结构；静止质量视为全息边界上的整数微态计数（贝肯斯坦界），用严格整数算术替代浮点近似；稳定粒子表现为重复格子位错，通过变分重整化群流和傅里叶神经算子恢复连续谱算子，涌现薛定谔方程。

Result: 拓扑约束将算法提议空间限制在物理守恒的因果轨迹上，修剪组合树为确定性多项式时间路径，实现NP到P的复杂度崩塌；同时为物质提供了信息论定义，恢复了量子力学的统计描述。

Conclusion: 基于全息E8投影引擎的硬物理沙箱为AI安全提供新范式：通过拓扑约束实现计算复杂度根本性降低，将非遍历洞察注入学习过程，避免流形锁定，并确立人机共生的热力学必要性。

Abstract: This paper exposes the ergodic ceiling and thermodynamic inefficiency of current deep learning, which converges to a statistical average of historic human knowledge. True semantic novelty requires a path-dependent, spatiotemporally bounded observer (a Data LifeCone) to inject non-ergodic insight, achieving KL divergence and avoiding manifold lock-in. AI Safety must recognise that a mature Artificial Superintelligence (ASI) would regard human-AI symbiosis as a thermodynamic necessity to avoid model collapse. We therefore propose hard physical containment via a digital physics sandbox powered by a Holographic E8 Projection Engine to verify models against real-world constraints. Spacetime is modeled as an information substrate of nested face-centered cubic (FCC) lattices of oscillating Planck-scale spheres maximizing local information and entropy density. Cut-and-project methods from the E8 root lattice produce a quasi-crystalline geometry where tetrahedral voids support SU chiral structure and elastic-shear eigenvalues generate candidate mass spectra. Rest mass is treated as discrete, integer microstate counts on local holographic boundaries (Bekenstein bound), replacing floating-point approximations with strict integer arithmetic to provide an information-theoretic definition of matter. Stable particles emerge as recurring lattice dislocations, and continuum recovery proceeds via variational renormalisation-group flows and Fourier Neural Operators that learn continuous spectral operators to recover the Schrödinger equation as an emergent statistical description. Crucially, these top-down topological constraints offer a mechanism for "NP-to-P" complexity collapse: by restricting an algorithm's proposal space to physically conserved causal trajectories, the sandbox prunes the combinatorial tree to deterministic, polynomial-time paths.

</details>


### [106] [PXtal: Learning to Align Powder X-Ray Diffraction and Crystal Structures under Information Asymmetry across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10653)
*Zhuoran Yang,Christopher M. Collins,Bei Peng,Luke M. Daniels,Matthew J. Rosseinsky,Vladimir V. Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PXtal框架利用非平衡最优传输(UOT)和广义KL散度解决PXRD与晶体结构之间的信息不对称问题，在检索任务中优于基线，并揭示了多模态学习中对齐目标应反映各模态保留信息的普遍设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
