Afterwards, samples are organized into episodes by assigning distinct channel roles as target variates, past-only covariates, and known-future covariates, ensuring that the model is optimized on predictable variates using available exogenous information. Technically, Timer-M1 further adapts gated two-dimensional Transformer blocks that dynamically allocate cross-variate attention across layers. Across three large-scale forecasting benchmarks, Timer-M1 ranks first on both FEV and TIME and second on GIFT-Eval among most recent time series foundation models. These results support effective primitive-based pretraining as a route to robust general forecasting technique across domains and task settings.

</details>


### [211] [Uncovering and Fixing Collider Bias in Bayesian PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11737)
*Michael Obermayr,Robert Peharz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Bayesian physics-informed neural networks (B-PINNs) are a popular framework for parameter and state inference from sparse or noisy observations. They are commonly formulated via a collider structure, in which physical and trajectory parameters are assumed to be a priori independent and become coupled through virtual likelihoods on differential-equation residuals that enforce physical consistency. We show that this modeling choice can induce severe systematic bias in the posterior over physical parameters: even when the prior is favorably centered on the ground-truth parameters, the resulting posterior can drift away and concentrate far from them. As a remedy, we advocate a hierarchical chain model in which physics generates trajectories, which in turn generate observations. The chain model does not suffer from this posterior bias, but it poses a harder, so-called doubly intractable, inference problem due to a physics-dependent normalization constant. This challenge can be resolved by discretizing the underlying stochastic dynamics, after which the chain posterior can be sampled exactly with particle MCMC. We identify two distinct mechanisms characterizing the collider bias, derive analytical approximations of their magnitudes, and establish diagnostic criteria for predicting when standard B-PINNs remain reliable. Experiments confirm the predicted bias and show that the chain formulation successfully avoids it.

</details>


### [212] [SparseDecoding: Decoding-Aware Pruning for Accurate and Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12327)
*Qitong Wang,Xinwei Niu,Mingluo Su,Shanwei Zhao,Shiai Zhu,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SparseDecoding 是一个面向解码的剪枝框架，通过生成时的激活分布校准 Hessian 并优化 SpMV 内核，实现高达 1.48 倍的端到端解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Hessian 的剪枝方法使用自然序列校准，但解码时使用自生成 tokens，导致分布偏移，激活分布偏离，剪枝性能下降；且现有方法主要优化 SpMM，而解码主要由 SpMV 主导，加速有限。

Method: 算法层面：在密集模型自回归生成阶段（不含 prefill）收集逐层激活构建校准矩阵，使剪枝目标与解码激活对齐。系统层面：开发优化的 N:M 稀疏矩阵-向量内核，采用位掩码索引和固定步长遍历。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B、Llama-3.3-70B、Qwen3-14B/32B 上，长文本生成基准上持续优于固定文本校准方法，在 A100 GPU 上实现高达 1.48 倍端到端解码加速。

Conclusion: SparseDecoding 通过解码感知的激活校准和专用 SpMV 内核，有效解决了分布偏移和 SpMV 加速不足的问题，显著提升了 LLM 推理解码阶段的效率与性能。

Abstract: The memory-bound nature of the decoding stage of large language model (LLM) inference incurs significant latency. Layer-wise training-free network pruning approaches guided by the Hessian have been a prominent solution to this problem, as pruning reduces the number of nonzero parameters read from memory during decoding. Nevertheless, typical methods in this line compute the Hessian using pre-collected natural sequences, whereas the model is fed self-generated tokens during decoding, creating a distribution shift between the two sequences. The Hessian calculated on the natural sequence is different from that calculated on the generated sequence. We observe that this discrepancy causes the activation distribution during generation to deviate from that used for pruning, further hurting the pruned model performance. Moreover, most existing LLM pruning methods that bring actual speedup primarily target the sparse matrix-matrix (SpMM) multiplication, providing limited support for the sparse matrix-vector (SpMV) operations, which dominate decoding. To solve these problems, we introduce SparseDecoding, a principled decoding-aware pruning framework tailored for accurate and efficient LLM decoding. Specifically, at the algorithmic axis, SparseDecoding constructs calibration matrices from layer-wise activations collected during the dense-model autoregressive generation, excluding prefill, thereby aligning the pruning objective with the decoding activations. At the system axis, we develop an optimized N:M sparse matrix-vector kernel with bitmask indexing and fixed-step traversal. Substantial empirical results on representative LLMs (Llama-3.1-8B, Llama-3.3-70B, Qwen3-14B / 32B) demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms standard fixed-text calibration on the long-form generation benchmarks while achieving up to 1.48x end-to-end wall-clock decoding speedup on A100 GPUs.

</details>


### [213] [Correlational Training of Morphological Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11740)
*Konstantinos Fotopoulos,Petros Maragos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于形态学神经网络的新型权重更新方法，受启发于乘法权重更新（MWU）方案，将形态学感知机视为对数空间中的专家建议学习问题，采用基于相关性的奖励机制，在九个基准测试中有八个取得改进，最高提升32.84个百分点，并显著降低运行间变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络通常使用一阶方法和反向传播训练，但对于权重雅可比稀疏、参数梯度较差的形态学层，这种方法是否最优尚不清楚。

Method: 提出基于乘法权重更新（MWU）的新权重更新方法，将每个形态学感知机视为对数空间中的专家建议学习问题，使用基于相关性的奖励，偏向于与期望输出变化对齐的输入，无论是否有强梯度信号到达其权重。

Result: 在九个基准测试中，相关性训练在八个上取得改进，最高提升32.84个百分点，并显著减少运行间的变异性。

Conclusion: 该方法为形态学神经网络提供了一种有效的替代训练策略，能够克服稀疏梯度问题，提高性能并增强训练稳定性。

Abstract: Neural networks are typically trained using first-order methods and back-propagation. It is unclear whether this approach is optimal for morphological layers whose weight Jacobians are sparse and whose resulting parameter gradients can be poor. In this work, we propose a novel weight update method for morphological neural networks inspired from the Multiplicative Weights Update (MWU) scheme. We view each morphological perceptron as an instance of the learning from experts' advice problem in logarithmic space, and use a correlation-based reward that favors inputs aligned with the desired output change, regardless of whether a strong gradient signal has reached their weight. We empirically evaluate our approach by training fully connected layers both as stand-alone models and as parts of larger transformer networks. Across nine benchmarks, correlational training yields improvements on eight, by up to 32.84 percentage points, while substantially reducing run-to-run variability.

</details>


### [214] [TraceRelay: Attention-Aligned Recurrence over Rolling Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11743)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present TraceRelay, an attention-aligned recurrent architecture that distributes persistent representations over a rolling sequence of low-dimensional traces. Local right looking attention forms increments from lower-layer representations; delivery is delayed until all attended inputs are in the causal past. A fixed additive phase recurrence accumulates the delayed increments, and left-looking attention reads the resulting residual augmented stream. A stride-wise prefix sum supports parallel prefill and bounded-buffer continuation. We study 36 small-model runs on Equal Repeats, bounded Dyck closing-type prediction, and causal Most-Freq generation, using three seeds per setting. At trained length 256, Equal Repeats models with recurrent phase inheritance reach 98.81-99.69% accuracy versus 50.73-51.63% for separately trained variants without inheritance, despite the latter receiving more updates. Accuracy drops sharply at lengths beyond the training range. At the longest evaluated lengths, models with more dimensions in the middle layer's recurrent traces perform better on Dyck (76.34% versus 55.49% close accuracy at length 4096), whereas models with fewer trace dimensions perform better on five-symbol Most-Freq (70.74% versus 55.60% exact generation at length 1024). These contrasting cases motivate further study of how the size of recurrent representations should be chosen for different tasks, without establishing a general rule across tasks or model configurations.

</details>


### [215] [The Ball and the Box: Two Geometries of Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11744)
*Xiaoyu Li,Lequan Lin,Dai Shi,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经表示中超现象下计算布尔门所需的维度，针对单阈值层、高斯随机字典和均匀随机稀疏布尔输入，推导了两种误差准则下的尖锐维度阈值：期望错误计数趋零与所有输出高概率正确。发现共享读取解释了两者的差距：罕见实例会同时产生大量错误。期望计数阈值呈球几何，联合可靠性呈盒几何。优化共享读出权重和偏置给出了合取、析取和多数门的显式阈值，且成对合取的临界行为与精确模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络在超现象（表示特征数超过维度）下如何可靠地计算布尔逻辑门，理解不同误差准则对所需维度的影响，揭示共享读取机制导致的错误聚集现象。

Method: 理论分析单阈值层配合高斯随机字典和均匀随机稀疏布尔输入，推导两种误差准则下的维度阈值：期望错误计数趋零和所有输出高概率正确。利用几何视角（球几何与盒几何）刻画阈值差异，优化共享读出权重和偏置获得显式阈值，并对成对合取进行临界行为分析。

Result: 得出两个尖锐维度阈值：期望错误计数趋零需要更高维度，而所有输出高概率正确所需维度较低；共享读取导致罕见实例产生大量错误，解释了阈值差距。期望计数阈值对应球几何，联合可靠性对应盒几何。为合取、析取、多数门给出了显式阈值公式，成对合取的临界转变与精确模拟吻合。

Conclusion: 在超现象下计算布尔门时，误差准则的选择显著影响所需维度。共享读取机制导致错误聚集，使得期望错误计数趋零比全输出高概率正确需要更多维度。几何视角揭示了两种准则的本质差异，优化读出参数可获得具体门类型的维度阈值，理论预测与模拟一致。

Abstract: Neural representations can encode more features than they have dimensions, a phenomenon known as superposition. We study the dimension needed to compute Boolean gates from such representations. For a single threshold layer with a Gaussian random dictionary and uniformly random sparse Boolean inputs, we derive sharp dimension thresholds under two error criteria. A vanishing expected error count can require more dimensions than correctness of every output with high probability. Shared reads explain the gap: rare realizations can produce many errors at once. The expected-count threshold has ball geometry, while joint reliability has box geometry when a gate is evaluated on every feature tuple. Optimizing shared readout weights and biases gives explicit thresholds for conjunction, disjunction, and majority. For pairwise conjunction, the analysis also describes the transition near the threshold, in agreement with exact simulations.

</details>


### [216] [SR-TTA: Spatial-Redundancy Test-Time Adaptation for Interference-Robust Respiration Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11755)
*Jingyuan Liu,Zheng Chang,Haoqiu Xiong,Zhuangzhuang Cui,Sofie Pollin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无标签测试时自适应的空间冗余方法(SR-TTA)，用于在6G无细胞大规模MIMO基站上实现呼吸感知，有效解决了同频带运动干扰导致的传统波束成形失效问题，将呼吸率误差从5.8降至0.8 bpm，并在真实测试床上将失败率从47%降至7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络旨在将感知作为原生服务，复用通信基础设施。现有的手工设计融合方法在良性条件下接近最优，但在强同频带运动干扰（频率落在呼吸频带内）下会失效。需要一种能够通过空间零点抑制干扰、并能在部署时通过无标签自适应弥补与预言机差距的方法。

Method: 提出学习型复权重波束成形器进行空间零点干扰抑制，并设计空间冗余测试时自适应(SR-TTA)：在带外频谱否决约束下，最大化随机天线子集间的一致性。该流水线映射到O-RAN架构：O-DU测距门控、自适应xApp和校准rApp。采用一次跨受试者校准结合SR-TTA。

Result: 仿真同频带干扰下，呼吸率误差从5.8降至0.8 bpm；真实测试床录音中，一次跨受试者校准加SR-TTA将失败率从47%降至7%，性能与使用无标签测试时自适应的手工组合器持平。频率和方差准则无法分离同频带干扰与呼吸，而SR-TTA保持了良性条件下的性能。

Conclusion: SR-TTA通过利用空间冗余和带外频谱约束，实现了无标签的测试时自适应，有效解决了同频带干扰下的呼吸感知难题。该方法可部署于O-RAN架构，为6G原生感知服务提供了实用的技术路径。

Abstract: Future 6G networks aim to expose sensing as a native service by reusing communication infrastructure. We study respiration sensing on a cell-free massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) base station, where a 64-antenna channel must be fused into a breathing waveform. The state-of-the-art hand-crafted fusion is near-optimal in benign conditions. It collapses, however, under strong in-band motion interference, whose frequency falls inside the respiration band. We show that a learned complex-weight beamformer recovers respiration by spatial nulling, and that the remaining gap to a per-recording oracle can be closed at deployment by label-free test-time adaptation. Crucially, we identify which label-free signal makes this work. Frequency- and variance-based criteria cannot separate an in-band interferer from breathing. Our spatial-redundancy test-time adaptation (SR-TTA), which maximizes consistency across random antenna subsets under an out-of-band spectral veto, preserves benign performance in our tests. The respiration-rate error drops from 5.8 to 0.8 breaths per minute (bpm) under simulated in-band interference, and the pipeline maps onto the Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) architecture as O-RAN distributed-unit (O-DU) range-gating, an adaptation xApp, and a calibration rApp. On real testbed recordings, a one-time cross-subject calibration plus SR-TTA reduces failures from 47% to 7%, drawing level with the hand-crafted combiner using label-free test-time adaptation.

</details>


### [217] [RAGenome: Scaling Retrieval-Based Genomic Language Models to Long Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11761)
*Frederikke Isa Marin,Panagiotis Antoniadis,Dionysia Danai Brilli,Andreas Bjerregaard,Rachael DeVries,Yan Li,Ole Winther,Wouter Boomsma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAGenome是首个基于检索的基因组语言模型（gLM），将预训练上下文长度扩展至现有MSA-based gLM的100倍，基于100种脊椎动物全基因组比对训练，显著提升长距离建模能力（基因发现性能从0.45提升至0.60），同时在进化相关任务（如致病变异优先级排序）上保持竞争力，以极低训练成本统一了进化建模与长距离建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基因组语言模型需要极大计算资源且在部分下游任务仍落后于传统方法；基于MSA的预训练虽高效但受限于短上下文，仅能处理短程任务（如变异效应预测），无法捕捉跨物种进化关系与种内长距离相互作用。

Method: 提出RAGenome——基于检索的gLM架构，利用检索机制将预训练上下文扩展至现有MSA-based gLM的100倍（长距离建模），在100种脊椎动物全基因组比对数据上训练，融合跨物种进化信息与种内长距离基因组交互，构建统一、灵活、可扩展的框架。

Result: 长距离能力大幅提升：基因发现性能从0.45提升至0.60；在纯进化任务（致病变异优先级排序）上保持竞争力；训练成本仅为传统gLM的一小部分，实现了性能与效率的平衡。

Conclusion: RAGenome通过检索增强实现了进化建模与长距离基因组建模的统一，提供了一个高效、可扩展的单一框架，以极低成本达到竞争性gLM性能，为大规模基因组语言建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: The genome holds the blueprint that governs the biological properties of the cell. Consequently, advancing our knowledge of genomic function is crucial both for a broader understanding of biology and for continued biomedical advances. The success of large language models on natural language and protein sequences has motivated similar efforts on genomic data. However, standard genomic language models (gLMs) often require extremely large computational resources and still fall behind traditional methods on some downstream tasks. Recently, MSA-based pretraining has been proposed as an efficient alternative, but existing models are limited to short input contexts, restricting their use to short-range tasks, such as variant effect prediction. In this work, we present RAGenome, the first retrieval-based gLM that scales pretraining to longer contexts (100$\times$ longer than existing MSA-based gLMs), allowing it to capture both across-species evolutionary relationships and within-species longer-range interactions. Trained on whole-genome alignments from 100 vertebrates, RAGenome substantially improves the long-range capabilities of MSA-based gLMs, raising gene finding performance from 0.45 to 0.60, while remaining competitive on purely evolutionary-based tasks like prioritizing pathogenic variants. RAGenome provides competitive gLM performance at a fraction of the training cost, unifying evolutionary modeling and long-range capabilities within a single, flexible, scalable framework. Code is available at https://github.com/PanosAntoniadis/RAGenome.

</details>


### [218] [Discovering Global False Negatives On the Fly for Self-supervised Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20612)
*Vicente Balmaseda,Bokun Wang,Ching-Long Lin,Tianbao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出GloFND方法，解决自监督对比学习中的假阴性问题，通过优化为每个锚点自动学习阈值，在全局数据集层面检测假阴性，且计算成本与数据集大小无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自监督对比学习中，负样本对通常由锚点图像和从整个数据集中抽取的样本构成（排除锚点本身）。这种方式可能产生语义相似的负样本对（即假阴性），导致它们的嵌入被错误地推远，损害表示学习质量。现有方法主要在mini-batch内局部检测假阴性，无法覆盖全局数据集。

Method: 提出GloFND（Global False Negative Detection），一种基于优化的方法，在训练过程中动态为每个锚点数据学习阈值以识别其假阴性。核心创新在于：1）全局检测：跨整个数据集而非仅在mini-batch内识别假阴性；2）计算高效：每次迭代的计算成本与数据集大小无关。

Result: 在图像和图文数据上的实验结果证明了该方法的有效性。代码已开源：https://github.com/vibalcam/GloFND

Conclusion: GloFND通过全局视角的假阴性检测和自适应阈值学习，有效缓解了对比学习中的假阴性问题，且保持了计算效率，为自监督表示学习提供了改进方案。

Abstract: In self-supervised contrastive learning, negative pairs are typically constructed using an anchor image and a sample drawn from the entire dataset, excluding the anchor. However, this approach can result in the creation of negative pairs with similar semantics, referred to as "false negatives", leading to their embeddings being falsely pushed apart. To address this issue, we introduce GloFND, an optimization-based approach that automatically learns on the fly the threshold for each anchor data to identify its false negatives during training. In contrast to previous methods for false negative discovery, our approach globally detects false negatives across the entire dataset rather than locally within the mini-batch. Moreover, its per-iteration computation cost remains independent of the dataset size. Experimental results on image and image-text data demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/vibalcam/GloFND.

</details>


### [219] [In-Ride Alcohol-Impairment Detection in E-Scooterists with False-Alarm Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11783)
*Marco Capuccini,Rahul Rajendra Pai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于车载传感器（惯性测量和油门数据）的实时酒后骑行检测方法，具有可证明的虚假报警率上限，实验表明其在较高血液酒精浓度下检出率达91%，中位检测时间仅25秒，且嵌入式实现满足实时性要求，为共享电动滑板车提供了一种无需赛前测试或夜间禁令的干预方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享电动滑板车已成为广泛采用的城市交通方式，但酒精中毒是导致严重事故的突出因素。现有对策仅限于单点反应测试和夜间禁令，会暂停服务并给清醒用户带来负担。需要一种能在骑行过程中实时评估骑手状态、并可控制虚假报警率的在线检测方法。

Method: 利用车载惯性传感器和油门测量数据，设计一个在线检测器，在骑行过程中累积证据判断骑手是否受酒精影响。该检测器提供可证明的虚假报警率上限。通过141次骑行实验（25名参与者，清醒及两个目标血液酒精浓度水平）验证，并与基线和消融实验对比。同时实现嵌入式部署以满足实时性要求。

Result: 在虚假报警率上限0.023下，检测器对较高酒精浓度骑行的检出率为91%，中位检测时间25秒；对较低浓度检出率50%，中位检测时间27秒。基线和消融方法要么超出虚假报警率上限，要么丧失检测性能，甚至延迟报警。嵌入式实现满足实时要求，可在车载端直接执行缓解措施，无需数据上传。

Conclusion: 该工作为尽早干预酒后骑行奠定了基础，避免了清醒用户承担赛前测试负担或夜间服务暂停，同时让运营商可在用户体验与虚假报警率之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Shared e-scooter services have become a widely adopted urban transport mode. While most users ride responsibly, alcohol intoxication stands out among the factors contributing to severe crashes. Nonetheless, countermeasures remain limited to single-point reaction tests and night bans that suspend the service altogether. This paper proposes a new approach in which onboard sensors evaluate the rider as the trip unfolds, raising an alarm as soon as enough evidence of impairment has accumulated. Specifically, we introduce a detector that operates on inertial and throttle measurements, with a provable bound on the rate of false alarms. Experiments on sensor data from 141 rides, in which 25 participants rode while sober and at two target blood alcohol concentration levels, confirm that the bound holds, whereas baselines and ablations either exceed it or lose detection performance, and in some cases delay the alarm. At a bound of 0.023, the detector identifies 91% of the rides performed at the higher concentration and 50% of those at the lower one, with median detection times of 25 and 27 seconds, respectively. We further show that an embedded implementation meets the real-time requirement, making mitigation actions feasible onboard, without requiring data to leave the vehicle. Overall, this work lays the ground for interventions that reach impaired riders as soon as possible, sparing the sober ones the burden of a pre-ride test or the suspension of the service at night, while letting operators budget false alarms against user experience.

</details>


### [220] [Long Text to Predictive Features: LLM-Guided Blockwise Feature Engineering via Executable Program Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12390)
*Ziming Dai,Dabiao Ma,Ziheng Guo,Jack Dong,Zimu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-BlockFE，一种LLM引导的离线特征构建框架，将长文本转化为可执行特征程序，避免在线推理调用LLM，通过块级回滚机制和交错搜索轨迹提升搜索效率，在多个数据集和实际部署中显著提升AUC和KS指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业风控系统主要依赖结构化数据模型，但非结构化长文本中蕴含大量有价值信息；人工特征工程耗时费力，而实时调用大语言模型（LLM）难以满足实际部署的延迟与成本要求，因此需要一种离线构建特征程序、在线高效推理的方法。

Method: LLM-BlockFE通过增量追加不可变代码块构建特征程序，并利用下游模型评估候选特征；针对传统贪心搜索易陷入局部最优，引入基于深度校准信用分配的块级回滚机制，并交错推进多条独立搜索轨迹，通过共享固定探索方向描述减少冗余探索；搜索完成后冻结程序部署，用于在线提取结构化特征供下游预测模型使用。

Result: 在两个公开和两个私有数据集上，LLM-BlockFE相比各数据集最强基线实现绝对AUC提升0.0069至0.0358；上线后在五个已部署金融风控应用中监测到绝对KS提升0.02至1.56个百分点，优于现有手工设计策略。

Conclusion: LLM-BlockFE有效解决了长文本特征提取的工程化难题，兼顾离线构建与在线高效推理，显著提升风控模型性能，已在实际业务中验证其价值。

Abstract: Industrial risk-control systems typically rely on structured-data models for efficient prediction, yet substantial valuable information remains embedded in unstructured long text. Extracting this information through manual feature engineering is labor-intensive, while requiring a large language model (LLM) to process every real-time input may not meet practical deployment requirements. To address this challenge, we propose LLM-BlockFE, an LLM-guided offline feature construction framework that converts long text into executable feature programs, thereby avoiding LLM calls during online inference. LLM-BlockFE constructs feature programs by incrementally appending immutable code blocks and evaluates candidate features using a downstream model. To address the tendency of conventional greedy search to become trapped in suboptimal solutions, our method introduces a block-level rollback mechanism based on depth-calibrated credit allocation and advances multiple independent search trajectories in an interleaved manner, reducing redundant exploration by sharing fixed descriptions of each trajectory's exploration direction. After the search, the resulting programs are frozen and deployed to extract structured features for downstream prediction models. Across two public and two private datasets, LLM-BlockFE achieves absolute AUC improvements of 0.0069 to 0.0358 over the strongest baseline on each dataset in the full-dataset comparison. Post-launch monitoring across five deployed financial risk-control applications shows absolute KS improvements of 0.02 to 1.56 percentage points over the existing manually designed strategy.

</details>


### [221] [Compile the Table: Query-Calibrated Operator Compression for Tabular In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11784)
*Xu Zhao,Jiaming Zhao,Bin Zhao,Yong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QCOC（查询校准算子压缩）方法，用于表格上下文学习（ICL）中的上下文示例压缩。QCOC通过一次性编译完整KV缓存为紧凑的共享内存原型，结合锚定闭式解校准原型值，实现了跨查询的高效复用，在保持接近全上下文精度的同时大幅提升推理吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格ICL中当前的上下文示例压缩方法面临精度-吞吐权衡：固定子集牺牲精度，查询特定检索限制缓存复用和批处理，降低吞吐。需要一种能跨查询复用紧凑内存、同时保持高精度的压缩方案。

Method: QCOC利用上下文示例的可交换性和重复使用特性，一次性将完整KV缓存编译为跨查询共享的紧凑内存。核心步骤：将示例状态聚类为联合KV原型，保留每簇重数和原始示例总数，通过锚定闭式解根据上下文示例产生的注意力查询向量校准原型值。原型压缩带来加速，值拟合保持精度。

Result: 在64个OpenML-CC18数据集上，QCOC在两种保留数量下均达到对比压缩/检索方法中最高平均精度。在7个长表的12个配置中，压缩方法中10个排名第一，平均仅比全上下文低0.23个百分点。将8192示例压缩至512内存槽实现10.5倍缓存压缩；排除一次性编译后，单核CPU在线服务1000查询比动态检索基线快达508倍，比全上下文推理快1.98倍。

Conclusion: QCOC实现了跨查询的紧凑内存复用和高效推理，同时保持了接近全上下文的精度，有效解决了表格ICL中上下文压缩的精度-吞吐权衡问题。

Abstract: Tabular in-context learning (ICL) has emerged as a training-free and accurate paradigm for tabular prediction, but current approaches to compressing its in-context examples face an accuracy-throughput tradeoff: fixed subsets can sacrifice accuracy, while query-specific retrieval limits cache reuse and batching across queries, reducing throughput. We propose QCOC (Query-Calibrated Operator Compression), which exploits the exchangeability and repeated use of in-context examples by compiling their full KV cache once into compact memory shared across subsequent queries. Instead of retaining raw examples, QCOC clusters their states into joint-KV prototypes, preserves per-cluster multiplicities and the original example count, and calibrates prototype values against attention query vectors produced by the in-context examples through an anchored closed-form solution. Prototype compression drives the speedup, while value fitting helps preserve accuracy. On 64 held-out OpenML-CC18 datasets, QCOC achieves the highest mean accuracy among the compared compression and retrieval methods at both retained counts. Across 12 configurations on seven long tables, it ranks first among compressed methods in ten and averages 0.23 percentage points below full context. Compressing 8,192 in-context examples to 512 memory slots yields a 10.5x cache compression ratio; excluding one-time compilation, in a single-core CPU online-serving comparison over 1,000 queries, QCOC is up to 508x faster than dynamic retrieval baselines and 1.98x faster than full-context inference. These results show that QCOC enables compact-memory reuse and efficient inference across queries while retaining accuracy close to full context.

</details>


### [222] [PRAXIS: Learning Dynamics of Self-Improving Models with Symbolic Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11803)
*Venkat Margapuri,Mustafa Teber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRAXIS，一个协同进化框架，将生成器、学习器和符号档案建模为相互作用的动力学过程，为自改进学习系统提供了理论保证和实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改进学习系统通过自适应数据选择、优化和辅助符号组件，导致目标函数非平稳，超出了标准学习假设的范围，需要一个能够建模和控制这种协同进化动态的理论框架。

Method: PRAXIS框架包含三个核心组件：生成器、学习器、符号档案，三者作为相互作用的动力学过程协同进化。理论贡献包括：(1) KL约束的生成器更新和受控档案权重移动限制单步目标漂移；(2) 档案更新在次高斯噪声下，相对于任何固定比较器，抑制累积效用优势持久的程序；(3) 随机梯度下降达到平均平稳性保证，其退化由累积目标漂移决定。

Result: 在视觉鲁棒性、关系图推理和算法图推理三个任务上的实验显示：生成器稳定化、学习器损失下降、档案浓度集中，均与理论机制一致。

Conclusion: PRAXIS为自改进学习系统提供了一个理论奠基的协同进化框架，通过可证明的目标漂移界和实证验证，展示了生成器-学习器-档案三方协同动态的有效性。

Abstract: Self-improving learning systems adapt data selection, optimization, and auxiliary symbolic components, inducing nonstationary objectives outside standard learning assumptions. We introduce \textsc{PRAXIS}, a co-evolutionary framework that models generators, learners, and symbolic archives as interacting dynamical processes. We prove that KL-constrained generator updates and controlled archive-weight movement bound one-step objective drift, that archive updates suppress a program relative to any fixed comparator with a persistent cumulative utility advantage under sub-Gaussian noise, and that stochastic gradient descent achieves an average-stationarity guarantee whose degradation is governed by cumulative objective drift. Experiments across visual robustness, relational graph reasoning, and algorithmic graph reasoning exhibit generator stabilization, decreasing learner loss, and archive concentration consistent with these theoretical mechanisms.

</details>


### [223] [Self-Supervised Speech Representations for Cross-Speaker Dysarthria Detection During Awake Craniotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11825)
*Kanthila Chinmayi,Abdallah Nassib,Misy Harrison,Panheleux Celine,Saliou Vanessa,Seizeur Romuald,Dardenne Guillaume*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了一个用于区分清醒开颅术中构音障碍语音与正常语音的流水线，发现语音表示比分类器选择对跨说话人性能影响更大，患者特定的语音分离和强预训练表示比增加分类器复杂度更重要，并量化了患者特定术前校准可能带来的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在清醒开颅术中检测术中语言障碍对于保护语言功能至关重要，但自动化检测面临手术室录音声学干扰大、临床相关语音事件稀少、可用队列小且跨说话人异质性高等挑战。

Method: 提出的流水线包含：说话人日志分离患者语音、结合手工声学描述符与多层 wav2vec 2.0 嵌入的多视图表示、说话人条件归一化与基于可迁移性的特征选择以提升跨说话人鲁棒性、以及由梯度提升一级分类器和神经网络二级分类器组成的级联分类器。采用留一说话人出交叉验证进行严格的说话人无关评估。

Result: 跨说话人性能受语音表示影响远大于分类器选择：三个分类器的 AUC 差异不超过 4.7%，而用多层自监督表示替代传统声学描述符使 AUC 提升 18.2%-26.1%。日志条件特征提取和级联分类器提供了额外的持续增益。

Conclusion: 在低资源术中场景中，可靠的患者特定语音分离和强预训练表示比增加分类器复杂度更关键。研究还量化了患者特定术前校准可能实现的性能增益。}

Abstract: Detecting intra-operative speech impairment during awake craniotomy is essential for preserving language function. However, automated detection remains challenging because operating-room recordings contain substantial acoustic interference, clinically relevant speech events are rare, and available cohorts are small and heterogeneous across speakers. This study presents a systematic component-wise evaluation of a pipeline for distinguishing dysarthric from no-trouble speech in the DATABRASE corpus of awake-craniotomy recordings. The pipeline incorporates speaker diarization to isolate patient speech, a multi-view representation combining handcrafted acoustic descriptors with multilayer wav2vec 2.0 embeddings, speaker-conditional normalization and transferability-based feature selection to improve cross-speaker robustness, and a cascaded classifier comprising a gradient-boosted first stage and a neural second stage. Evaluation was conducted under strict speaker-independent conditions using leave-one-speaker-out cross-validation. The results show that cross-speaker performance is influenced more strongly by the speech representation than by classifier choice. The AUCs of three classifiers differed by no more than 4.7%, whereas replacing conventional acoustic descriptors with the multilayer self-supervised representation produced AUC improvements of 18.2%-26.1%. Diarization-conditioned feature extraction and the proposed classifier cascade provided additional consistent gains. These findings indicate that reliable patient-specific speech isolation and strong pretrained representations are more important than increased classifier complexity in low-resource intra-operative settings. They also quantify the potential performance gains that may be achieved through patient-specific preoperative calibration.

</details>


### [224] [Recovery Guarantees for Posterior Sampling of One-Bit Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11834)
*Jing Ma,Yujia Wu,Zhaoqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究噪声一比特压缩感知的样本复杂度，利用近似覆盖数刻画先验分布复杂度，证明后验采样在测量数与近似覆盖数对数成比例（含一比特分离间隙因子）时可高概率精准恢复，且对先验失配鲁棒；建立下界表明上界在主要先验相关项上近似最优；提出基于扩散先验的即插即用后验采样算法并在FFHQ和ImageNet上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一比特压缩感知在极低比特率下硬件友好，但噪声下的样本复杂度理论尚不完善，尤其缺乏结合先验分布复杂度的刻画及对学习先验失配鲁棒性的分析。

Method: 通过近似覆盖数刻画先验分布的有效分布复杂度，证明后验采样的上界；利用Wasserstein距离分析近似先验的鲁棒性；基于Fano不等式建立任意可靠方法的下界；设计基于扩散模型的即插即用后验采样算法近似理想后验采样过程。

Result: 测量数随近似覆盖数对数增长（乘以一比特分离间隙因子）即可高概率精准恢复；上界对先验失配鲁棒，只要学习先验在Wasserstein距离上足够接近真实分布；下界表明上界在主要先验相关项上近似最优；实验验证了算法在FFHQ和ImageNet上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为噪声一比特压缩感知提供了近似最优的样本复杂度理论，揭示了先验分布复杂度（近似覆盖数）的核心作用，证明了后验采样对先验失配的鲁棒性，提出的扩散先验即插即用算法在实际图像数据集上表现优异。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of noisy one-bit compressed sensing for signals drawn from a prior distribution. By characterizing the effective distributional complexity of the prior via its approximate covering number, we prove that posterior sampling achieves accurate recovery with high probability when the number of measurements scales with the logarithm of the approximate covering number, up to a one-bit separation gap factor. This upper bound is robust to learned prior mismatch. Specifically, we show that posterior sampling with an approximate prior remains reliable, provided that the learned prior distribution is sufficiently close to the true signal distribution in Wasserstein distance. In addition, we establish a sample complexity lower bound for any reliable method of noisy one-bit compressed sensing, showing that our upper bound is nearly matched in its main prior dependent term. To approximate the ideal posterior sampling process for real world scenarios, we instantiate posterior sampling through a plug-and-play algorithm with diffusion priors. Experiments on the FFHQ and ImageNet datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed approach.

</details>


### [225] [DADP: Dynamic Activity-Dependent Pruning, A Reverse Hebbian-Inspired Structural Pruning Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11853)
*Bhushan Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为动态活动依赖剪枝（DADP）的生物启发式结构可塑性机制，该方法在训练过程中通过累积预突触激活与后突触误差梯度的乘积来衡量连接重要性，利用单一全局阈值动态分配稀疏度，在多种架构上达到或优于现有剪枝方法，在ResNet-18上以99%稀疏度保持73.67%准确率（稠密基线76.06%），并通过矩阵香农熵和有效秩验证了在极端稀疏下保持潜在特征多样性且无表示崩塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经网络严重过参数化，导致训练和推理时的计算和内存开销巨大。现有剪枝方法依赖事后幅度阈值或静态初始化启发式，往往需要人工设定每层稀疏目标或昂贵的重新训练周期，缺乏动态、自适应的结构稀疏分配机制。

Method: DADP在训练过程中实时测量连接重要性：累积预突触激活值与后突触误差梯度的乘积。采用单一全局阈值而非固定层级预算，动态跨网络深度分配稀疏度，自然诱导神经元级和通道级剪枝。该机制受生物结构可塑性启发，无需预设每层稀疏率，实现端到端的动态稀疏训练。

Result: 在MLP、VGG-16、ResNet-18、BiLSTM-CRF和MiniBERT等多种架构上，DADP匹配或优于Magnitude、SNIP和RigL等基线方法。在ResNet-18上以99%稀疏度保持73.67%准确率（稠密基线76.06%）。矩阵香农熵和有效秩测量证实，DADP在极端稀疏下保留潜在特征多样性，避免了表示崩塌。

Conclusion: DADP提供了一种生物学合理、计算高效的动态剪枝框架，通过单一全局阈值实现跨层自适应稀疏分配，在极端稀疏条件下保持模型性能和特征多样性，为大规模神经网络的高效训练和部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern neural networks are heavily over-parameterized. This redundancy incurs substantial compute and memory overhead during training and inference. Existing pruning methods rely on post-hoc magnitude thresholds or static initialization heuristics. Consequently, they often require manual per-layer sparsity targets or expensive retraining cycles. We propose Dynamic Activity-Dependent Pruning (DADP), a biologically inspired structural plasticity mechanism. During training, DADP measures connection importance via the accumulated product of pre-synaptic activations and post-synaptic error gradients. Using a single global threshold instead of fixed layer budgets, DADP dynamically allocates sparsity across network depth while naturally inducing neuron- and channel-level pruning. Across MLP, VGG-16, ResNet-18, BiLSTM-CRF, and MiniBERT architectures, DADP matches or outperforms Magnitude, SNIP and RigL, retaining 73.67% accuracy (dense baseline: 76.06%) at 99% sparsity on ResNet-18. Finally, matrix-based Shannon entropy and effective rank measurements confirm that DADP preserves latent feature diversity at extreme sparsities without representation collapse.

</details>


### [226] [Understanding Latent-Dimension Scaling in Dynamical-System Learning through Spectral Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11866)
*Itsushi Sakata,Yuta Miyauchi,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度学习中表示大小对动力学自回归预测的影响，利用Koopman算子谱结构分析学习到的时间演化，提出谱残差损失优于潜在预测损失，在六个混沌系统上验证了谱残差模型能更一致地降低滚动预测误差，且特征值集中在低残差区域，支持Koopman谱可靠性解释误差随维度增加而减小的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似理论表明增加表示大小有益于深度学习，但这一优势是否延伸到自回归预测的动力学学习中尚不明确。现有方法在一步误差下降时仍可能产生虚假特征对，导致长期预测不可靠。需要一种能量化Koopman谱可靠性的指标来指导模型训练。

Method: 构建共享Koopman自编码器，分别采用潜在预测损失（潜在坐标一步预测误差）和谱残差损失（候选特征对的相对残差）进行交替训练。利用相对残差检测虚假特征对，理论证明有界Koopman算子在学习字典空间上的最小残差随空间逼近可观测空间而逐点收敛。在六个混沌系统上对比两种损失及四个基线族。

Result: 两种模型均随维度增大降低中位窗口滚动误差。谱残差模型在所有系统和维度上中位数均低于潜在预测模型，且下降幅度更大。其误差在四个系统中随维度单调下降，而潜在预测模型仅一个。对比基线，谱残差模型平均有效预测时间几乎总是最长。最大维度下，谱残差特征值集中在低残差区，潜在预测特征值也出现在高残差区。

Conclusion: Koopman谱可靠性（由相对残差量化）能解释滚动误差随表示维度增加而更一致地减小。谱残差损失比潜在预测损失更有效地引导特征值向可靠区域聚集，从而显著提升长期预测性能。

Abstract: In deep learning, approximation theory motivates increasing representation size. We ask whether this benefit extends to dynamics learning through autoregressive prediction. We analyze the learned time evolution through the eigenstructure of Koopman operators, using relative residuals to detect spurious eigenpairs arising even as one-step error falls. For bounded Koopman operators, we show that minimal residuals over learned dictionary spaces converge pointwise to their full-space counterparts as these spaces approximate the observable space in $L^2$. Our hypothesis is that Koopman spectral reliability helps explain how consistently rollout error decreases with increasing dimension. We compare two models of a shared Koopman autoencoder trained alternately for reconstruction and latent evolution, using latent-prediction loss (one-step prediction errors in latent coordinates) or spectral-residual loss (relative residuals of candidate eigenpairs). Across six chaotic systems, both models reduced median windowed rollout error from smallest to largest dimension. The spectral-residual model achieved lower medians than the latent-prediction model for all systems and dimensions, and its median fell by a larger factor in every system. Its median decreased monotonically with dimension in four systems, against one for latent prediction. Against four baseline families, its mean valid prediction times were nearly always longer. At the largest dimension under two-stage training, we compared eigenvalue positions with each learned dictionary's residual contours. Spectral-residual eigenvalues concentrated in low-residual regions, whereas latent-prediction eigenvalues also appeared in high-residual regions, consistent with the hypothesis.

</details>


### [227] [Automated Assembly Instruction Generation from CAD Models Using Grounded Large Language Models: A Human-in-the-Loop Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11896)
*Aaron Dsouza,Mohammed Azeez Khan,Ashutosh Mishra,Arshaan Khan,Neha K. Nair,Amar Kumar Behera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CAD的装配指令生成框架，将STEP装配模型映射为类型化ProductGraph，通过确定性拓扑排序推导装配顺序，结合图上下文生成自然语言步骤，附加可视化文档，并引入规则与模型辅助检查，最终经人工审核后导出手册。案例研究验证了六部件装配的端到端可行性，保留了装配顺序、数量、材料和扭矩等工程信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前装配文档仍主要靠人工解读CAD模型编写，尽管结构化产品数据和大语言模型均已可用，但CAD解读、装配序列规划、指令撰写和人工监管等研究长期割裂，缺乏端到端的工程约束生成体系。

Method: 框架包含五大模块：1) 将STEP装配转为类型化ProductGraph中间表示；2) 基于图结构进行确定性拓扑排序得出装配优先顺序；3) 以选定图上下文为条件逐步实现自然语言生成；4) 为每步附加可视化文档；5) 应用规则检查与模型辅助检查，PDF导出需人工解决所有质量标记后方可释放。

Result: 在六部件基准装配案例中，流水线完整保留了报告的装配顺序，并将数量、材料、扭矩等工程属性准确带入导出手册页，验证了端到端可行性；但泛化能力与几何验证仍为开放实证问题。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出了一种分层架构，将工程状态、确定性推理、落地语言实现、验证机制与人工发布解耦，为CAD驱动的装配指令自动化生成提供了系统性范式。

Abstract: Assembly documentation is a downstream manufacturing artifact that is still usually authored by interpreting CAD models by hand. Structured product data and large language models are both available, yet studies of CAD interpretation, assembly sequence planning, instruction writing, and human oversight have largely proceeded separately. This paper formulates CAD-grounded assembly instruction generation: the production of natural-language assembly procedures constrained by structured engineering information extracted from CAD models. The proposed framework maps a STEP assembly to a typed ProductGraph intermediate representation, derives a precedence order by deterministic topological sorting, realizes each step as language conditioned only on selected graph context, attaches per-step visual documentation, and applies rule-based and model-assisted checks. PDF export remains disabled until a human reviewer resolves every quality flag. The case study establishes endto-end feasibility on a built-in six-part reference assembly: the pipeline preserves a reported assembly order and carries quantity, material, and torque into an exported manual page. Generalization and geometric validation remain open empirical questions. The contribution is an architecture that separates engineering state, deterministic reasoning, grounded language realization, verification, and human release.

</details>


### [228] [Puffin: Probabilistic Learning of Spatial Detail From Coarse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11914)
*Chaitanya Jobanputra,Sebastian Vollmer,Gerrit Großmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Puffin框架，一种用于统计离散化的概率方法，利用高分辨率卫星嵌入作为协变量将粗分辨率社会经济变量提升至高分辨率，通过聚合感知似然训练，无需精细标签即可生成与观测聚合一致且带有校准不确定性的精细估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率社会经济变量对城市规划、公共卫生、灾害响应和资源分配等应用至关重要，但实际中这些变量通常仅在粗空间分辨率下可观测。现有方法难以在无精细标签情况下生成一致且带不确定性的高分辨率估计。

Method: Puffin采用概率框架进行统计离散化：1) 使用高分辨率卫星嵌入作为协变量；2) 学习每个精细子区域的概率分布而非单一值；3) 通过聚合感知似然训练；4) 推理时条件于观测的区域总量，将其分配到子区域。

Result: 在德国和美国的人口普查、就业和选举数据上评估，涵盖人口、就业岗位等计数变量。研究了统计离散化在不同区域、国家和目标变量上的成功与失效条件。

Conclusion: Puffin实现了无需精细标签训练即可生成与观测聚合一致、带校准不确定性的高分辨率社会经济估计，为统计离散化提供了有效的概率框架，并揭示了其适用边界条件。

Abstract: High-resolution socioeconomic variables are important for applications such as urban planning, public health, disaster response, and resource allocation. In practice, however, these variables are often observed only at a coarse spatial resolution. We introduce Puffin, a probabilistic framework for statistical disaggregation that raises the resolution of coarse totals using high-resolution satellite embeddings as covariates. Instead of predicting a single value for each fine-resolution subregion, Puffin learns a probability distribution and is trained through an aggregation-aware likelihood. At inference, Puffin conditions these predictions on the observed regional total and splits it among the subregions. The resulting fine-scale estimates are consistent with the observed aggregate and come with calibrated uncertainty, without requiring fine-resolution labels for training. We evaluate Puffin on German and US census, employment, and election data across population, jobs, and other count variables, and study when statistical disaggregation succeeds or fails across regions, countries, and targets.

</details>


### [229] [Interval-valued SHAP in Tree-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11953)
*Chenrui Zhu,Vu-Linh Nguyen,Marie-Hélène Masson,Sébastien Destercke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于不精确狄利克雷模型（IDM）的方法，通过区间值Shapley值分析决策树和随机森林中Shapley值的鲁棒性，并推广至Banzhaf值，实验展示其在去除无信息特征偏见中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shapley值虽是流行的特征归因解释方法，且树模型已有高效计算算法，但已知Shapley值对微小扰动高度不鲁棒，缺乏系统的鲁棒性分析工具。

Method: 引入不精确狄利克雷模型（IDM），向树叶子节点随机添加少量未标注实例，基于悲观原则和平均原则定义区间值Shapley值，推导理论结果实现高效计算，并推广至Banzhaf值。

Result: 提出的区间值Shapley值能有效量化鲁棒性，案例研究和实验展示其行为特征，并成功应用于消除无信息特征的偏见。

Conclusion: 基于IDM的区间值Shapley值为树模型特征归因鲁棒性提供了有效分析框架，兼具理论保证与计算效率，可直接推广至其他归因指标。

Abstract: Shapley values are among the most popular feature-attribution explanations. Efficient approaches for computing/estimating Shapley values for tree-based models, which are state-of-the-art for tabular data sets, have been developed. However, it is known that Shapley values can be (highly) unrobust due to small and realistic changes. In this paper, we propose an imprecise Dirichlet model (IDM) based method to analyze the robustness of Shapley values in decision trees and random forests. Technically, it is done by quantifying and analyzing the interval-valued Shapley values when a few unannotated instances are randomly introduced to the leaves of the trees. The interval-valued Shapley values can be defined following common principles in handling incomplete data: the pessimistic and averaging principles. We derive various theoretical results that lead to efficient computation of the interval-valued Shapley values. We also show that the proposed method can be straightforwardly generalized to the case of Banzhaf values. We then present various case studies and experiments to illustrate the behaviour of the proposed interval-valued Shapley values and their applications in debiasing uninformative features.

</details>


### [230] [Stochastic Grouping Conformal Prediction for Effective Subgroup Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11957)
*Meihui Zhong,Wenxin Tai,Ting Zhong,Fan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一种随机分组共形预测方法(SGCP)，通过学习随机分组映射让样本从校准行为相似的其他样本中汲取校准信息，实现子群可靠的不确定性量化，在保持标准覆盖保证的同时减少子群覆盖差距并控制预测集大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测仅提供总体层面的覆盖保证，在临床重要子群间存在覆盖差异；预定义分组校准需要敏感属性且面临最差组瓶颈，保护最难子群会膨胀所有预测集，增加决策者认知负担。

Method: 提出随机分组共形预测(SGCP)，学习随机分组映射，使每个样本能从校准行为相似的其他样本中获取校准信息，形成局部得分分布，提升子群体可靠性；理论证明保留标准覆盖保证。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准实验中，SGCP持续缩小子群覆盖差距，同时实现比现有基线更小或相当的预测集大小。

Conclusion: SGCP为子群可靠的不确定性量化提供了一个有效框架，解决了标准共形预测的子群覆盖差异问题，避免了预定义分组的敏感属性依赖和最差组瓶颈，在保持理论保证的同时提升了实际性能。

Abstract: Conformal prediction offers a distribution-free coverage guarantee, making it especially attractive for clinical applications. Standard conformal prediction, however, provides such guarantees only at the population level, and its prediction sets can exhibit coverage disparities across clinically important subgroups. A natural remedy is to calibrate within predefined groups. However, this can require access to sensitive subgroup attributes and is prone to a worst-group bottleneck: protecting the most difficult subgroup can inflate prediction sets for all, increasing cognitive burden on decision makers. To this end, we propose Stochastic Grouping Conformal Prediction (SGCP), a conformal framework for subgroup-reliable uncertainty quantification. It learns a stochastic grouping map that allows each sample to draw calibration information from others with similar calibration behavior, yielding a local score law that boosts reliability across subpopulations. We prove that SGCP retains the standard coverage guarantee. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that it consistently reduces subgroup coverage gaps while achieving smaller or comparable prediction set sizes relative to existing baselines.

</details>


### [231] [Example-driven Parametrisations for Bayesian Shape Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11984)
*Gabriel Diaz-Aylwin,Joseph Neighbor,Abiel Malkani Talwar,Rui-Yang Zhang,Henry B. Moss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用PCA学习形状变形的参数化，构建线性可解释的搜索空间，用于贝叶斯优化，提升样本效率并突破手工参数化的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化适用于昂贵且不可微的形状设计目标，但需要紧凑且富有表达力的搜索空间参数化。手工设计参数化复杂、依赖领域知识，且常产生隐式不可行区域、人为边界和耦合无序坐标。

Method: 从现有设计集合中学习参数化，对形状间的变形应用主成分分析（PCA），得到线性、可解释的搜索空间，主成分数量可显式权衡表达力与维度。

Result: 在翼型、机翼和射频腔体等二维几何至三维气动/电磁任务中，展示了更高的样本效率，并能探索超出手工基线范围的设计空间。

Conclusion: 学习式参数化方法有效解决了手工参数化的局限，为贝叶斯优化在复杂形状设计中提供了更高效、灵活的搜索空间。

Abstract: Bayesian optimisation is the natural tool for shape design when objectives are expensive and non-differentiable, but it needs a compact yet expressive parameterisation of the search space. Hand-crafting one is a complex endeavour requiring domain expertise, and often yields implicit infeasible regions, artificial bounds, and coupled, unordered coordinates. We instead learn the parameterisation from a collection of existing designs, applying principal component analysis to the deformations between shapes. The result is a linear, interpretable search space in which the number of components explicitly trades expressivity against dimensionality. Across aerofoils, wings, and radio-frequency cavities, spanning 2D geometry to 3D aerodynamics and electromagnetics, we show improved sample efficiency and the ability to explore beyond the confines of hand-crafted baselines.

</details>


### [232] [The Polytopal Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12004)
*A. Emilie J. Wedenborg,Anders V. Nørskov,Teresa Dorszewski,Kristoffer Wickstrøm,Morten Mørup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多面体神经网络（PNNs），通过强制多面体结构直接用于信息处理，实现逐层可解释的表示学习，在保持性能的同时提供透明的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法往往牺牲结构保真度、依赖预设语料库或事后解释，缺乏原理化、内在可解释的深度学习框架。

Method: PNNs 引入基于多面体的层级结构，利用学习到的语料库表示和分摊单纯形推理进行扩展，观测值通过与层特定侧面的对齐显式描述，同时提供通往向量量化（VQ）训练的直接路径。

Result: 实验表明：多面体约束在潜在空间保留有意义结构且性能损失极小；在无监督学习中压缩表示优于 VQ；并为 VQ 深度学习训练提供了高性能新范式。

Conclusion: 深度网络可强制学习可解释的多面体表示，为构建高透明度 AI 系统提供了原理化路径，且几乎不牺牲性能。

Abstract: Understanding how deep neural networks process information remains a central challenge. Existing interpretability methods often compromise structural fidelity, rely on prespecified corpora, or explain models post-hoc. We propose Polytopal Neural Networks (PNNs), a framework that extracts distinct layer-wise aspects by enforcing a polytope-based structure that is used directly in subsequent information processing. We scale our approach using learned corpus representations and an amortized simplex inference procedure and highlight how the framework also gives a direct route to vector quantized (VQ) training. In PNNs, observations are explicitly described by their alignment with layer-specific aspects. Empirical results show that imposing polytopal constraints on neural network representations preserves meaningful structures in the latent space with minimal degradation in performance, favorable compressed representations when compared to VQ representations in unsupervised learning, while also providing a performant new approach to VQ deep learning training. Our findings suggest that deep networks can enforce interpretable polytope-based representations, offering a principled path toward more transparent AI systems with minimal performance compromise.

</details>


### [233] [Test-Time Compute for Tabular Foundation Models: Mechanisms, Gains, and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12005)
*Kanghui Ning,Marin Biloš,James T. Wilson,Yilang Zhang,Kashif Rasul,Dongjin Song,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了测试时计算对表格基础模型(TFMs)预测性能的提升，重点考察适应、聚合和上下文构建三个维度。提出DiagScale仅训练0.003-0.03%参数即可达到全量微调效果；贪心选择策略在96个配置池中相对默认预测器降低2.4%误差，而均匀平均反而增加误差；注意力引导检索在大表上有效并突破上下文内存限制。适应和聚合结合能进一步提升但计算成本大增，需根据计算预算权衡选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型(TFMs)在推理阶段如何有效利用额外计算资源提升预测性能尚不明确。本文旨在系统评估测试时计算沿适应、聚合、上下文构建三轴的提升效果，为实际部署提供计算-性能权衡指导。

Method: 1. 适应: 提出DiagScale，一种对角查询-键相似度更新方法，仅训练极少量参数(0.003-0.03%)。2. 聚合: 研究预测池组成与选择策略，对比TabPFN-3默认平均、均匀平均、贪心选择等策略，在96个预处理配置池上实验。3. 上下文构建: 测试注意力引导检索及上下文扩展方法，评估其在大规模表和超内存限制场景下的效果。实验基于TabArena基准及OpenML大规模表格，覆盖三个独立预训练骨干。

Result: 1. DiagScale在三个预训练骨干上达到与全量微调相当的增益。2. 聚合方面：TabPFN-3默认平均预处理变体已接近饱和；96配置池中贪心选择相对默认降低2.4%误差，均匀平均反而增加误差。3. 上下文构建：注意力引导检索在部分大表上改进预测并支持超内存源池；上下文扩展方法无一致提升。4. 适应与聚合结合有进一步增益但计算量大幅增加。

Conclusion: 适应(如DiagScale)和选择性聚合(如贪心选择)能带来稳健的基准级性能提升；上下文构建收益更依赖任务和数据规模。适应与聚合结合虽有增益但计算开销大，实际部署需根据计算预算权衡策略选择。代码已开源。

Abstract: Which forms of test-time compute improve the predictions of strong pretrained tabular foundation models (TFMs)? We systematically study this along three axes: adaptation, aggregation, and context construction. Our evaluation spans modern TFMs across the TabArena benchmark, supplemented by experiments on wide and large-scale tables from OpenML. For adaptation, we introduce DiagScale, a diagonal query-key similarity update. It trains only 0.003-0.03% of model parameters and achieves gains comparable to full fine-tuning across three independently pretrained backbones. For aggregation, both pool composition and selection strategy matter. TabPFN-3 already averages predictions from different preprocessing variants of the same data, and adding more such predictions yields diminishing returns. With a broader pool of 96 configurations, greedy selection reduces error by 2.4% relative to the default predictor, but uniform averaging increases error. For context construction, attention-guided retrieval improves TabPFN-3's predictions on some large tables and supports source pools beyond the full context memory limit. The context expansion methods we test yield no consistent improvement. Taken together, our results suggest that adaptation and selective aggregation yield consistent benchmark-level gains. The benefits of context construction depend more on the task and data regime. Adaptation and aggregation over the same backbone yield further gains when combined, but require substantially more computation than default inference. These trade-offs motivate choosing strategies according to the available computation budget. Code is available at https://github.com/kanghui-learning/test-time-compute-for-tabular-foundation-models.

</details>


### [234] [CausalDreamer: Learning Predictive World Models with Latent Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12016)
*Prince Jha,Nils Lukas,Kun Zhang,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalDreamer 通过将冻结的视频分词器潜在表示重新编码为沿可控性和奖励相关性两个轴分解的四组表示，改进了世界模型在控制任务中的表现，在已见任务和被操纵变体上显著优于基线，但对全新环境泛化能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式世界模型（如 Dreamer 4）使用重建目标训练的视频分词器，其潜在表示无法显式区分可控/不可控、奖励相关/无关信息，限制了模型在控制和规划中的效果。

Method: 保持分词器冻结，将其潜在表示重新编码为四组分解表示：两个可控组接收动作输入，两个不可控组不接收；通过从两个奖励相关组预测奖励来学习奖励相关性。随后微调预训练动力学模型以预测该分解表示。

Result: 在 20 个 MMBench2 任务上：10 个已见任务中 CausalDreamer 归一化得分 0.199 对比基线 0.175（高 14%）；6 个被操纵变体中得分 0.307 对比 0.246（高 25%）；4 个全新环境中两者均未显著超越随机策略。分析显示分解表示能将背景变化等奖励无关因素从奖励相关组中分离。

Conclusion: CausalDreamer 通过因果分解表示增强了世界模型对可控性和奖励相关性的建模，提升了在分布内和分布外（但结构相似）任务上的规划性能，但对完全陌生环境的泛化仍是挑战。

Abstract: World models for control must capture which aspects of the environment respond to the agent's actions and which are relevant to reward. Generative world models such as Dreamer 4 consist of a video tokenizer, which encodes each frame into a latent, and a dynamics model, which is pretrained to predict future latents from past latents and actions. Yet the tokenizer is trained with a reconstruction objective, without action or reward supervision, so its latent provides no explicit mechanism to separate controllable, uncontrollable, reward-relevant, and reward-irrelevant information. We propose \textit{CausalDreamer}, which keeps the tokenizer frozen and re-encodes its latent into a factored representation of four groups along two axes: controllability, where only the two controllable groups receive the action, and reward relevance, learned by predicting the reward from the two reward-relevant groups. The pretrained dynamics model is then fine-tuned to predict the factored representation. We evaluate \textit{CausalDreamer} and the pretrained world model it starts from with model-predictive planning on 20 MMBench2 tasks: 10 clean tasks seen during training and 10 unseen tasks, of which 6 are manipulated variants of clean tasks with a changed background, object, or maze layout, and 4 are new environments. We normalize returns so that a policy taking uniformly random actions scores 0 and an expert scores 1. \textit{CausalDreamer} achieves a 14\% higher normalized score than the pretrained world model on the clean tasks (0.199 vs.\ 0.175) and a 25\% higher score on the manipulated variants (0.307 vs.\ 0.246), while neither model scores meaningfully above the random policy in the new environments. Additionally, our analysis shows that the factored representation separates reward-irrelevant changes, such as a changed background, from its reward-relevant groups.

</details>


### [235] [MPGE: A Multi-Perspective Graph Explainer for Molecular Classification Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12039)
*Mahtab Sarvmaili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多视角图解释器（MPGE），统一事实支持、反事实敏感性和示例容忍度三种视角，为冻结分类器提供紧凑的保留预测的解释，并在多个分子数据集上评估其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络（GNNs）虽能预测分子性质，但预测准确性无法解释图信息如何支持单个决策；现有解释方法难以同时揭示哪些变化能反转决策、哪些修改模型可容忍，因此需要统一事实、反事实和示例三种视角的解释框架。

Method: 提出MPGE框架，包含三个视角：事实视角（原型PT）寻找保持标签和置信度的紧凑保留边集；反事实视角（CF）寻找有界的改变预测的删除；示例视角（EXE）寻找非平凡的有界删除且保持标签和置信度。采用共享约束公式连接预测行为、紧凑性和编辑成本，分离目标生成三视角。扩展CF-GNNExplainer用于图分类，学习对称边排序并验证离散候选，记录失败搜索。使用BBBP片段后端返回RDKit清洗的分子。在MUTAG、Mutagenicity、AIDS、COX2_MD、BBBP数据集上评估，指标包括语义覆盖、条件质量、稳定性、运行时间。

Result: 成功事实掩码平均保留8.6%-15.5%输入边；有界反事实覆盖率4.8%-67.6%；示例保持覆盖率98.9%-100.0%。探索性控制揭示硬投影和保留节点信息的影响。定量比较和分子可视化表征模型支持、敏感性和容忍性，但不将其视为已验证的化学机制。

Conclusion: MPGE通过统一三种解释视角，有效揭示了GNN决策的支持、敏感和容忍特性，为分子图分类提供了可解释性工具，但生成的解释不应直接等同于化学机制验证。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) predict molecular properties from chemical graph data, but predictive accuracy does not explain how graph information supports an individual decision. A compact prediction-preserving rationale does not necessarily reveal which changes reverse the decision or which modifications the model tolerates. We propose the Multi-Perspective Graph Explainer (MPGE), unifying factual support, counterfactual sensitivity, and exemplar tolerance for a frozen classifier. The factual view, originally termed prototype (PT), seeks a compact retained edge set with the same label and required confidence. Counterfactual (CF) explanations seek bounded prediction-changing deletions; exemplar (EXE) explanations seek non-trivial bounded deletions that preserve the label and confidence. A shared constrained formulation connects prediction behavior, compactness, and edit cost, while separate objectives generate the three views. Our graph-classification extension of CF-GNNExplainer learns symmetric edge rankings and verifies discrete candidates, recording unsuccessful searches. A separate BBBP fragment backend returns RDKit-sanitized molecules. We evaluate the primary GCN implementation on MUTAG, Mutagenicity, AIDS, COX2_MD, and BBBP using semantic coverage, conditional quality, stability, and runtime. Successful factual masks retained 8.6%--15.5% of input edges on average across datasets; bounded counterfactual coverage was 4.8%--67.6%, and exemplar preservation coverage was 98.9%--100.0%. Exploratory controls reveal the influence of hard projection and retained node information. Quantitative comparisons and molecular visualizations characterize model support, sensitivity, and tolerance without treating them as validated chemical mechanisms.

</details>


### [236] [Exploiting Gradients in Bayesian Inference of Expensive Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12076)
*Šimon Soldát,Václav Šmídl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将模拟器输出关于输入参数的梯度信息引入高斯过程代理模型，以加速基于贝叶斯优化的仿真推理。实验表明，利用梯度信息能显著提高收敛速度；反向模式自动微分下推理效率增益在考虑额外计算成本后依然保持，而前向模式则不划算。结论：梯度增强代理模型主要在参数数量超过输出维度（反向模式高效）的问题中有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于微分方程的模拟器在科学工程中广泛应用，仿真推理常需在有限仿真预算下通过贝叶斯优化主动学习高斯过程代理来最大化信息获取。尽管模拟器输出对输入参数的梯度日益易得，但鲜被用于推理。梯度可提供额外信号指导主动学习，对昂贵模拟器尤为重要。

Method: 将模拟器输出关于输入参数的梯度信息融入高斯过程代理模型，构建梯度增强代理，并结合贝叶斯优化主动学习框架，在有限仿真预算下进行仿真推理。对比反向模式与前向模式自动微分的计算开销与推理加速效果。

Result: 梯度信息显著提升收敛速度。反向模式微分下，推理效率增益在扣除额外计算成本后仍为正；前向模式微分下，加速收益无法抵消计算开销。梯度增强代理在参数维度高于输出维度（反向模式高效）的问题中最具优势。

Conclusion: 在仿真预算受限且模拟器昂贵的场景下，引入梯度信息的高斯过程代理能有效加速贝叶斯优化推理。实际部署时应优先采用反向模式自动微分，且适用于参数数量大于输出维度的问题。

Abstract: Simulators based on differential equations are ubiquitous in science and engineering. They are often used in simulation-based inference to evaluate the posterior distribution of the input parameters based on real-world observations of the simulator outputs. However, inference becomes challenging when individual simulator evaluations are computationally expensive. In such cases, a Bayesian optimization-based active learning approach with Gaussian process surrogate models has been used to maximize the information obtained from a limited simulation budget. Recently, gradients of simulator outputs with respect to input parameters have become increasingly available, yet they are rarely exploited for inference. Even though we only need to learn the simulator input-output relationship, gradient information can provide an additional valuable signal to guide the active learning procedure. This is of particular interest in the case of expensive simulators, when sample efficiency is crucial.
  In this paper, we demonstrate how incorporating gradient information into the Gaussian process surrogate accelerates Bayesian optimization-based inference under a limited simulation budget. Our results show significant improvement in convergence speed from using gradient information. For reverse-mode differentiation, the inference efficiency gains are maintained when accounting for the additional computational cost. In contrast, for forward-mode differentiation, the inference speed-up does not outweigh the computational costs. These results indicate that gradient-enhanced surrogates are beneficial primarily in problems where the number of parameters exceeds the output dimensionality, where reverse-mode differentiation is efficient.

</details>


### [237] [SCORE: Spectral Correlation Estimation for Multivariate Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12096)
*Christopher Bülte,Emil Partow,Astha Gupta,Pascal Esser,Gitta Kutyniok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCORE 是一种可扩展框架，用于高维结构化高斯目标的神经网络预测建模，结合评分规则训练与谱空间协方差近似，实现线性存储和 O(d log d) 计算复杂度，在时间序列预测、单目深度估计和空间天气预测任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高维结构化高斯目标的神经网络预测建模，需要一种高效、数值稳定且表达力强的协方差矩阵近似方法。

Method: 将学习任务分解为边缘分布学习和结构化相关矩阵学习；利用闭式高斯核评分进行训练，该评分即使在退化协方差下仍有定义且梯度有界；在谱空间学习表达力强的协方差近似；提出两层目标函数，理论上证明在总体水平上恢复真实边缘分布并将目标相关矩阵投影到可表示类上，有限样本 PAC 界显示两阶段误差加性进入。

Result: 在时间序列预测、单目深度估计和空间天气预测等任务上，SCORE 在较低计算成本下取得性能提升；理论分析给出总体一致性和有限样本泛化界。

Conclusion: SCORE 提供了一个可扩展、表达力强且数值稳定的框架，适用于高维高斯预测建模，兼具理论保证和实证效果。

Abstract: Neural network-based predictive modeling with high-dimensional structured Gaussian targets requires an efficient and numerically stable, yet expressive approximation of the covariance matrix. We propose SCORE: a scalable framework, combining scoring rule training with an expressive covariance approximation learned in spectral space. For $d$-dimensional data, the learning task is decomposed into learning the marginal distributions and learning a structured correlation matrix, which enables dense dependencies with linear storage and $\mathcal{O}(d\log d)$ cost. We utilize the closed form Gaussian kernel score for training, which remains defined even for degenerate covariances and admits bounded gradients during optimization. We characterize kernel scores under invertible transforms and prove exact invariance under unitary transforms. At population level, our two-level objective recovers the true marginals and projects the target correlation onto the representable class; finite-sample PAC bounds show that the errors of the two stages enter additively. We evaluate our model on a variety of tasks with a commonly assumed Gaussian domain: Time-series forecasting, monocular depth estimation, and spatial weather prediction, showing improved performance at lower computational cost.

</details>


### [238] [Few-Step Generation via Data-Space Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12102)
*Shanchuan Lin,Yansong Peng,Fu-Yun Wang,Haoqi Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“数据空间迭代”的少步生成框架，移除了流匹配中需要的固定时间步离散化，直接在数据空间中迭代优化生成样本，并在ImageNet 256x256类条件生成任务上超越了标准离散化基线，匹配或优于通过时间表搜索选择的变体，且无需针对特定时间表进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配虽然可训练高质量生成模型，但采样需要大量网络评估。蒸馏可降低至单步或少步，但单步生成牺牲质量，少步生成成为实际选择。现有少步方法沿概率流迭代，需固定手动选择的时间步离散化，这通常基于启发式、调优昂贵，且当不同样本或空间位置的细化难度不同时显得受限。

Method: 提出数据空间迭代框架：从噪声出发，共享生成器直接在数据空间中迭代优化其预测，每次迭代都训练以产生其容量允许的最佳样本。该公式与分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）仅需微小改动即可集成，可在匹配训练设置下对比不同迭代方法。

Result: 在类条件ImageNet 256x256上，数据空间迭代优于标准离散化基线，匹配或优于通过时间表搜索选择的变体，且无需针对特定时间表进行训练。

Conclusion: 数据空间迭代为快速生成提供了一种简单有效的替代方案，避免了离散化流空间迭代的固定时间步限制，实现了更灵活且性能更优的少步生成。

Abstract: Flow matching has emerged as a scalable paradigm for training high-quality generative models, but sampling from the learned probability flow requires many network evaluations. Distillation can reduce this cost to one or a few evaluations; however, one-step generation often sacrifices quality, making few-step generation the practical operating regime. Existing few-step methods perform their iterative computation along the probability flow and therefore require a fixed, manually chosen timestep discretization. This discretization is often chosen heuristically and is expensive to tune; it may also be restrictive when refinement difficulty differs across samples or spatial locations. We introduce data-space iteration, a few-step generation framework that removes flow discretization altogether. Starting from noise, a shared generator directly refines its prediction in data space, with every iteration trained to produce the best sample permitted by its capacity. Our formulation integrates with distribution matching distillation (DMD) with minimal changes, enabling a controlled comparison between iteration methods under matched training settings. On class-conditional ImageNet 256x256, data-space iteration outperforms standard discretization baselines and matches or improves upon variants selected through schedule search, without requiring schedule-specific training. These results show that data-space iteration provides a simple and effective alternative to discretized flow-space iteration for fast generation.

</details>


### [239] [A Geometric Approach to Soft Actor-Critic with Zonotopes for Locomotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12113)
*Panagiotis Roditis,Panagiotis P. Filntisis,Petros Maragos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeZo-SAC提出了一种基于几何表示的自适应悲观评论家聚合方法，通过区间多面体建模评论家不确定性，动态调整悲观偏移和评论家组合策略，在MuJoCo运动任务上达到最优或竞争性性能，同时显著降低过估计偏差和执行器工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线策略Actor-Critic方法（如SAC）通过取双评论家最小值来控制过估计偏差，但这种固定的聚合规则无法根据评论家分歧程度自适应调整悲观程度，导致次优性能。

Method: 每个评论家除标量价值外预测生成器定义区间多面体；沿采样方向探测区间多面体获得几何宽度作为悲观偏移，并计算评论家分歧度；用log-sum-exp聚合分歧度控制评论家组合：从宽度加权平均向最小值过渡；推理时仅使用未修改的SAC Actor。

Result: 在四个MuJoCo-v5运动基准上，GeZo-SAC在Ant-v5和Hopper-v5达到最高平均回报，其余任务保持竞争力；实现最低平均执行器工作量和单位距离动作努力；在所有环境中过估计频率接近零。

Conclusion: GeZo-SAC通过几何表示实现状态动作相关的自适应悲观主义，有效缓解过估计偏差，提高样本效率和策略质量，且推理无额外开销。

Abstract: Off-policy actor--critic methods control overestimation bias by taking the minimum of two critics. This uses the same aggregation rule everywhere, regardless of how the critics disagree. We propose \textbf{GeZo-SAC}, which uses auxiliary geometric representations to adapt critic pessimism to the state and action. Alongside its scalar value, each critic predicts a set of generators defining a zonotope. Probing this zonotope along sampled directions provides a geometric width, "subtracted from each critic value as a pessimistic offset, and a measure of disagreement between the two critics, aggregated with log-sum-exp. This disagreement controls how the critics are combined, moving from a width-weighted average toward the usual minimum as disagreement increases. At inference, the deployed policy is an unmodified SAC actor, since the generators are used only on the critic side during training.Across four MuJoCo-v5 locomotion benchmarks and six off-policy baselines, GeZo-SAC achieves the highest mean return on Ant-v5 and Hopper-v5 and remains competitive with other methods on the remaining tasks. Our analysis further shows that GeZo-SAC achieves the lowest average actuator work and action effort per metre among the evaluated methods, while maintaining near-zero measured overestimation frequency across all four environments.

</details>


### [240] [Credal Machine Learning for Risk-Averse Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12115)
*Timo Löhr,Paul Hofman,Maximilian Muschalik,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于置信集(credal sets)的风险厌恶学习方法，通过建模认知不确定性来实现可靠的CVaR最小化，在分类、分布偁移和强化学习任务中有效避免灾难性决策，同时保持较高的期望性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的风险厌恶学习方法通过最小化CVaR来防范最坏情况，但其有效性受限于学习者对真实损失分布的认知不确定性。由于无法准确知道真实的CVaR，单纯最小化经验CVaR可能导致不可靠的风险厌恶行为。需要一种能够显式建模并量化这种认知不确定性的方法。

Method: 提出了一种双组件方法：(1) 一个高效的学习器，输出预测形式为置信集（概率分布集合），显式表示对真实损失分布的认知不确定性；(2) 一个新颖的决策规则，将每个置信集映射为单一预测分布以进行CVaR最小化。该方法将表征不确定性的置信集学习与下游风险厌恶决策相结合。

Result: 在分类任务、分布偏移场景和强化学习实验中，该方法始终能够可靠地避免灾难性决策，同时在期望性能上仅损失很少。相比基线方法，在保持风险厌恶有效性的同时，没有显著牺牲平均性能。

Conclusion: 通过置信集显式建模认知不确定性，结合新型决策规则进行CVaR最小化，可以实现既可靠又高效的风险厌恶学习。该框架为在不确定环境下进行安全关键型机器学习决策提供了理论依据和实用方法。

Abstract: In many machine learning applications, it is necessary to guard against worst-case scenarios and predictions that could result in substantial losses. In principle, this can be achieved by training risk-averse predictive models that minimize loss functions such as conditional value-at-risk (CVaR), rather than relying on models that perform well on average. In practice, however, the effectiveness of this approach to risk aversion is undermined by the learner's uncertainty regarding the true loss distribution and, consequently, the true CVaR. To achieve reliable risk-aversion, we propose a method in which this (epistemic) uncertainty is represented in terms of credal sets, i.e., sets of probability distributions. More specifically, we develop an efficient yet reliable learner that produces predictions in the form of credal sets and combine it with a novel decision rule that maps each credal set to a single predictive distribution for CVaR minimization. Across classification, under distribution shift, and in reinforcement learning, our approach reliably avoids catastrophic decisions, while sacrificing little in expected performance.

</details>


### [241] [Using Weisfeiler-Leman Features for Algorithm Selection in Constraint Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12119)
*Alessio Pellegrino,Jacopo Mauro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图转换和Weisfeiler-Lehman (WL) 图核的自动化特征提取方法，用于约束规划中的算法选择。引入了切割基表示 (WLc) 来建模结构分区，在 2023-2025 MiniZinc Challenge 实例上评估，实验表明 WLc 特征在 SVM 上优于 fzn2feat，在随机森林和 MLP 上表现接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统算法选择依赖人工设计的实例级统计特征，难以捕捉问题的深层结构。现有基于图神经网络 (GNN) 的方法虽能捕捉结构，但需要训练且受限于 1-WL 测试的表达能力。作者旨在开发一种无需训练、能自动提取鲁棒结构表示的替代方案。

Method: 将约束规划实例转换为图，应用 Weisfeiler-Lehman 图核提取结构特征。核心创新是提出切割基表示 (WLc)，通过建模图的结构分区来增强预测信号。使用支持向量机 (SVM)、随机森林 (RF) 和多层感知机 (MLP) 作为下游分类器进行算法选择。

Result: 在 MiniZinc Challenge 2023-2025 实例上，针对最大化 Borda 计数分数和最大化预测准确率两个任务：WLc 特征配合 SVM 显著优于基线 fzn2feat；配合 RF 和 MLP 时，性能与基线接近，无显著差距。

Conclusion: 基于 WL 图核的切割表示 (WLc) 能有效捕捉约束规划实例的结构特征，为算法选择提供了强有力的自动化特征工程方案。该方法无需训练 GNN 即可达到有竞争力的性能，特别是在 SVM 等线性分类器上表现优异，验证了结构分区建模的价值。

Abstract: Algorithm Selection is essential for efficient Constraint Programming. Over the years, many algorithm selectors based on machine learning methods have been successfully applied, yet traditional feature extraction methods often rely on manually decided instance-level statistics that fail to capture the underlying problem structure.
  In this paper we aim to bridge this gap by introducing a novel, automated feature extraction methodology that integrates graph conversion and Weisfeiler-Lehman graph kernels to generate robust structural representations of problem instances. The 1-WL test bounds the graph-distinguishing power of standard message-passing Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), and suitable GNN architectures match this bound \citep{Xuetal2018}. WL-based features offer an alternative that does not require training a GNN. Our primary contribution is a cut-based representation (\texttt{WLc}) designed to model structural partitions and provide a more nuanced predictive signal. We evaluate our approach on instances from the 2023--2025 MiniZinc Challenges across two tasks: maximizing Borda count scores and maximizing predictive accuracy. Experimental results across Support Vector Machines, Random Forests, and Multi-Layer Perceptrons demonstrate that cut-based features outperform \texttt{fzn2feat} with SVMs, while results with RFs and MLPs are closer.

</details>


### [242] [Large-Scale Benchmarking of Quantum Neural Network Configurations for Financial Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12148)
*Jack Waller,Xing Liang,Dimitrios Makris,Rajagopal Nilavalan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对量子神经网络(QNN)在金融时间序列预测中的组件配置进行大规模系统比较评估，使用GBP/USD汇率为例，网格搜索1368种配置，发现门选择和排列比参数量更关键，纠缠是全电路系统级属性，最佳QNN配置R²达0.985超越BiLSTM，真实量子硬件噪声评估显示门错误和退相干是部署障碍，门选择和电路深度决定噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习特别是QNNs发展迅速，但系统比较主要集中在分类任务，回归问题尤其是金融时间序列预测较少关注，因此需要大规模系统评估QNN组件配置对预测性能的影响。

Method: 采用网格搜索遍历编码方法、ansatz设计、量子比特数、层深度、代价函数，共1368种不同模型配置，在预测精度、计算成本、收敛行为上评估；并在IQM Emerald真实量子设备上评估硬件噪声影响。

Result: 发现门选择和排列比原始参数量更关键；纠缠是全电路系统级属性而非仅ansatz级别；最佳QNN配置R²得分0.985，优于经典BiLSTM基线；真实硬件执行显示门错误和退相干是实际部署重大障碍，门选择和电路深度是硬件噪声鲁棒性关键决定因素。

Conclusion: 研究为QNN架构设计提供实用指导，建立了近期量子设备上QNN噪声敏感性的基准表征。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning, and quantum neural networks (QNNs) in particular, are advancing fields with growing potential. Although systematic comparisons of QNN configurations have been explored primarily for classification tasks, comparatively little attention has been given to regression problems, particularly financial time series forecasting. This study presents a large-scale systematic comparative evaluation of QNN component configurations for financial time series forecasting, using the GBP/USD spot exchange rate as a case study. A grid search across encoding methods, ansatz designs, qubit counts, layer depths, and cost functions yields 1,368 distinct model configurations, each evaluated in terms of prediction accuracy, computational cost, and convergence behaviour. The results reveal unique insights into how the choice of methods influences performance, such as that gate selection and arrangement are more critical to model success than raw parameter count, and that entanglement is a system-level property of the full circuit rather than solely at the ansatz level. The best-performing QNN configuration achieves an $R^2$ score of 0.985, outperforming a classical BiLSTM baseline. Additionally, the impact of real quantum hardware noise is assessed through execution on the IQM Emerald device, revealing that gate errors and decoherence represent a significant barrier to practical deployment, with gate selection and circuit depth identified as key determinants of hardware noise resilience. Overall, the findings provide practical architectural guidance for QNN design and establish a baseline characterisation of QNN noise sensitivity on near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [243] [Bayesian Optimisation under State-Preservation Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12150)
*Gabriel Diaz-Aylwin,Vignesh Gopakumar,Omkar Myatra,David Moulton,Lorenzo Zanisi,David S. Leslie,Henry B. Moss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性化约束响应的方法，将状态空间约束拉回设计空间形成椭ipsoid，用于解决工程设计中的状态保持约束问题，并在托卡马克磁约束聚变装置的偏滤器优化中验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多工程设计问题中，目标和约束依赖于由设计参数决定的PDE求解状态。现有受限贝叶斯优化难以处理这类问题中高度各向异性的可行集，需要一种能有效处理状态保持约束的新方法。

Method: 核心思想是预计算线性化约束响应在容差范围内的控制集合，将状态空间约束线性化拉回设计空间形成椭ipsoid，从中高效采样分布良好的候选点；在线细化线性响应映射并重建椭ipsoid。

Result: 在托卡马克偏滤器优化（等离子体边界保持）这一关键应用上端到端验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过线性化约束响应将状态保持约束转化为设计空间中的椭ipsoid采样问题，有效解决了高度各向异性可行集带来的优化难题，为复杂工程设计中的状态约束优化提供了实用框架。

Abstract: In many engineering design problems, the objective and constraints depend on the state: the solution of a PDE determined by the design parameters. We consider improving a design while holding selected state observables near trusted values, which we call state preservation constraints. Constrained Bayesian optimisation handles these with a learnt feasibility model, but struggles with this problem's highly anisotropic feasible set. Our central idea is to pre-compute the set of controls whose linearised constraint response stays within tolerance, thereby pulling back the state-space constraint into design space. This linearisation defines an ellipsoid from which we can efficiently draw a large number of well-spread candidates. The underlying linear response map is refined online, and the ellipsoid is rebuilt accordingly. We demonstrate the method end-to-end on our key application - Tokamak divertor optimisation under plasma-boundary preservation.

</details>


### [244] [Toward Optimal Regret in Adversarial MDPs with Stochastic Hard Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12153)
*Qian Zuo,Francesco Emanuele Stradi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带有对抗性损失和随机硬约束的分幕约束马尔可夫决策过程。从已知严格可行策略（裕度 d）出发，旨在在每幕满足期望成本约束的同时获得最优遗憾。作者提出 MA-OPS 算法，结合对 Slater 裕度的乐观搜索与对所选策略的悲观评估，实现遗憾 Õ(√T/ρ + 1/(dρ))，并给出匹配下界，证明对 T、d、ρ 的依赖在对数因子内最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作 Stradi et al. (2025) 通过混合规则获得遗憾 Õ(√T/min{d,d²})，并给出下界 Ω(√T/ρ)，其中 ρ 是离线问题的 Slater 裕度，可能远大于 d。两者存在裕度依赖的差距，亟需缩小差距并达到最优的裕度依赖。

Method: 提出 MA-OPS 算法：1) 乐观搜索 Slater 裕度 ρ，寻找更大的可行裕度；2) 对所选策略进行悲观评估，安全地学习具有大可行裕度的策略；3) 利用该策略在满足每幕约束的前提下最小化遗憾。

Result: MA-OPS 达到遗憾 Õ(√T/ρ + 1/(dρ))，优于先前的 Õ(√T/min{d,d²})。同时给出匹配下界，证明对 T、d、ρ 的遗憾依赖在对数因子内最优。

Conclusion: 本文通过乐观-悲观结合的方法，成功消除了先前算法中对 d 的次优依赖，实现了以离线 Slater 裕度 ρ 为主导的最优遗憾界，彻底解决了该设定下的遗憾最优性问题。

Abstract: We study episodic constrained Markov decision processes with adversarial losses under stochastic hard constraints. Specifically, starting from a known strictly feasible policy with margin $d$, we seek to obtain optimal regret while satisfying the expected cost constraints in every episode. In this setting, Stradi et al. (2025) show that a carefully designed mixing rule attains regret of order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T}/\min\{d,d^2\})$. Interestingly, they also provide a lower bound of order $Ω(\sqrt{T}/ρ)$ for the same setting, where $ρ$ is the Slater margin of the offline problem and can be much larger than $d$. In this work, we build on their approach to obtain optimal regret dependence on these margins. Specifically, we propose MA-OPS, an algorithm that combines an optimistic search for the Slater margin with a pessimistic evaluation of the selected policies to safely learn a policy with a large feasibility margin. This policy is then used to minimize regret while satisfying the constraints at every episode. In particular, we show that MA-OPS attains regret $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T}/ρ+ 1/(dρ))$. Finally, we provide a matching lower bound, showing that the dependence on $T$, $d$, $ρ$ in the regret bound is optimal up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [245] [Scalable Hierarchical Graph Generation via Soft Community Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12163)
*Ahmet Tüzen,Helge Langseth,Kjetil Nørvåg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Schema 通过将参考图递归分解为软社区层次结构，将大规模属性图生成分为三个独立阶段：基于软成员身份合成节点属性、从局部结构上下文生成社区内边、通过桥接节点建模社区间连接。该方法避免了构建完整邻接矩阵，在四个真实属性图上比其他生成属性的模型更好地恢复了局部与长程结构的平衡，仅复现少量参考边，保持下游任务准确性不人为提高，并在高达 1000 万节点的图上展示了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成大规模属性图需要同时复现拓扑结构、联合生成属性与结构并保持可扩展性。现实世界中许多图作为单一大图存在，生成模型必须从单一图泛化而没有独立样本。现有方法难以平衡结构保真度、属性生成、记忆化风险和可扩展性。

Method: 1) 递归分解参考图为软社区层次，每个节点获得成员分布；2) 三阶段独立训练生成：(a) 条件于软成员身份合成节点属性，(b) 基于局部结构上下文生成社区内边，(c) 通过成员质量分布跨多社区的桥接节点建模社区间连接；3) 无阶段构建完整邻接矩阵，每阶段在社区大小界定的子图上操作；4) 提出涵盖结构保真度、记忆化、下游效用和可扩展性的评估协议。

Result: 在四个真实属性图上，Schema 比任何其他生成属性的模型更接近地恢复局部与长程结构平衡，仅复现参考边的一小部分。保持下游准确性等于参考图水平而不人为提高。匹配其结构保真度的基线模型会记忆参考图；下游准确性更高的基线要么超过参考准确性（不真实），要么在更大图上无法完成。在六个额外图（高达 1000 万节点）上验证了可扩展性。

Conclusion: Schema 通过软社区分解和分阶段生成，实现了大规模属性图的高保真生成，有效平衡了结构复现、属性联合生成、抗记忆化和可扩展性，为单一大图的生成建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generating large attributed graphs requires reproducing the topology, generating attributes jointly with the structure, and remaining scalable. Many real-world graphs exist as a single large graph, so a generative model has to generalize from the one graph it is fit on, without independent samples. We present Schema, which recursively decomposes a reference graph into a hierarchy of soft communities, assigning each node a membership distribution. Generation is then split into three stages, each trained independently: (1) synthesizing node attributes conditioned on soft memberships, (2) generating intra-community edges from local structural context, and (3) modeling inter-community connections over bridge nodes whose membership mass is distributed across several communities. No stage forms the full adjacency matrix, and each stage operates on a subgraph bounded by the community size. We also introduce an evaluation protocol that covers structural fidelity, memorization, downstream utility, and scalability. On four real-world attributed graphs, Schema recovers the balance between local and long-range structure more closely than any other model that generates attributes, while reproducing only a small fraction of the reference edges. It retains the downstream accuracy of the reference graph without raising it artificially above that level. Baselines that match its structural fidelity memorize the reference, while those with higher downstream accuracy either exceed the reference accuracy or fail to complete on the larger graphs. We measure scalability on six additional graphs with up to 10 million nodes.

</details>


### [246] [Is Real-World Training Data Necessary for Generalist Graph Anomaly Detection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12167)
*Yujing Liu,Yixin Liu,Yue Tan,Xiaofeng Cao,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Heng Tao Shen,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AG-FORGE 自动合成异常图数据，并设计 TS-GGAD 拓扑-语义协同的通用图异常检测模型，配合课程学习策略，在 14 个真实数据集上显著超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用图异常检测（Generalist GAD）需要大量异常图数据训练基础模型，但真实异常图稀缺且标注成本高，现有合成数据方法容量有限，难以进一步提升模型性能。

Method: 1) AG-FORGE：自动化异常图生成框架，合成多样化的拓扑与语义异常图。2) TS-GGAD：拓扑-语义协同的通用 GAD 模型，同时捕捉拓扑结构与节点语义的异常证据。3) 面向大规模合成训练的课程学习策略，逐步增加样本难度以释放模型容量。

Result: 在 14 个真实数据集上，使用 AG-FORGE 生成的合成数据训练的 TS-GGAD，性能显著优于最先进的通用及专用 GAD 方法。

Conclusion: 合成数据驱动的训练可匹敌真实数据，且通过拓扑-语义协同建模与课程学习，能进一步突破性能边界，为通用图异常检测提供了可扩展的数据与模型解决方案。

Abstract: Generalist graph anomaly detection (GAD) aims to build a foundation model that detects anomalies on arbitrary unseen graphs without retraining or fine-tuning. Sufficient data are essential for foundation model training, yet generalist GAD still faces a data shortage, as real-world anomalous graphs are scarce and costly to collect and annotate. To fill this gap, we propose AG-FORGE, an Anomalous Graph generation Forge for automatic synthesis of anomalous graphs, exploring the feasibility of synthetic data-driven training for generalist GAD.
