Empirically, we find that synthetic data can achieve performance comparable to real-world training, but fail to push the performance boundary further due to the limited capacity of existing methods. To further unlock model capacity as training data scale up, we develop TS-GGAD, a Topology-Semantic coordinated Generalist GAD that captures complementary topological and semantic anomaly evidence, together with a curriculum learning strategy tailored to large-scale synthetic training. Extensive experiments on 14 real-world datasets demonstrate that TS-GGAD, trained on data generated by AG-FORGE, significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [247] [A Closer Look at Agentic BBO: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12183)
*Ming Chen,Rong-Xi Tan,Ke Xue,Yu-Jie Zhou,Taiye Lu,Zhi-Xuan Gao,Peng Xie,Zijun Shen,Chen Lu,Haopu Shang,Chao Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了 AgenticBBO-Bench，一个跨领域的智能体黑盒优化基准，涵盖合成函数、超参数优化、数据库调优、芯片设计和分子设计五大任务，采用统一的有限预算评估协议。实验表明智能体黑盒优化在所有五个领域均优于直接基于 LLM 的方法，并在四个领域超越最佳数值优化器。研究还分析了优化工具、任务信息与先验知识、LLM 在搜索中的角色三大因素，发现额外数值工具不一定提升性能，任务语义广泛有用但具体先验可靠性较低，数值优化器能有效吸收智能体建立的搜索轨迹收益。最后引入五任务前沿挑战，在 Codex 智能体框架下评估七个 LLM，GPT-6 Astra 和 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 位于性能与成本的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体黑盒优化研究使用不同任务领域和系统配置，导致结果难以对比，单个设计选择的效果难以隔离。因此需要一个统一的跨领域基准来系统评估智能体黑盒优化方法，并深入理解影响智能体性能的关键因素。

Method: 构建 AgenticBBO-Bench 基准，包含五大任务领域，采用统一有限预算评估协议。实验对比智能体黑盒优化、直接基于 LLM 的方法和最佳数值优化器。进一步通过消融实验研究三大因素：优化工具、任务信息与先验知识、LLM 在搜索中的角色。最后设立五任务前沿挑战，在 Codex 智能体框架下评估七个 LLM 的性能与成本权衡。

Result: 智能体黑盒优化在所有五个领域的家族平均得分均高于直接 LLM 方法，并在四个领域优于最佳数值优化器。额外数值工具不一致提升性能；任务语义广泛有用，更具体先验可靠性较低；数值优化器能有效吸收智能体建立的搜索轨迹收益。前沿挑战中，GPT-6 Astra 和 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 位于七个评估模型的性能-成本帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: AgenticBBO-Bench 为智能体黑盒优化提供了统一评估平台，揭示了智能体方法在多领域的优势，并阐明了优化工具、任务信息、LLM 角色等关键因素的影响。前沿挑战确立了当前最先进 LLM 在智能体黑盒优化中的性能-成本边界，为未来研究提供了基准和方向。

Abstract: Black-box optimization (BBO) arises in many scientific and engineering problems where objective evaluations are expensive and limited. Recent large language model (LLM) agents offer a new way to approach BBO by combining task semantics, computation, optimization tools, and feedback-driven decision making, showing great potential due to the integration with mathematically rigorous tools. However, existing agentic BBO studies use different task domains and system configurations, making their results difficult to compare and the effects of individual design choices hard to isolate. We therefore introduce AgenticBBO-Bench, a cross-domain benchmark for agentic BBO spanning synthetic functions, hyperparameter optimization, database tuning, chip design, and molecular design under a unified finite-budget evaluation protocol. In our experiments, agentic BBO achieves higher family-averaged scores than direct LLM-based methods in all five domains and outperforms the best numerical optimizers in four. We further study three factors shaping agent performance: optimization tools, task information and prior knowledge, and the role of the LLM during search. Our results show that additional numerical tools do not consistently improve performance, task semantics are broadly useful while more specific priors are less reliable, and numerical optimizers can effectively absorb gains from search trajectories established by the agent. Finally, we introduce a five-task frontier challenge within AgenticBBO-Bench and evaluate seven LLMs under the Codex agent harness, where GPT-6 Astra and DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash lie on the Pareto frontier of performance and cost among the evaluated models. Our code is available at https://github.com/lamda-bbo/agentic-bbo.

</details>


### [248] [Just Weather Scoring: Efficient End-to-end Nowcasting with Distributional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12189)
*Jannik Wiese,Johannes Schusterbauer,Tommaso Martorella,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为Just Weather Scoring (JWS)的单阶段端到端扩散模型，用于概率性降水临近预报，通过直接在雷达空间建模并结合掩码异步扩散和评分规则目标，实现了少步生成，在SEVIR和MeteoNet基准上达到最先进性能，且训练和推理成本大幅降低，最小模型参数更少且推理速度提升17倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成扩散模型在概率性降水临近预报中虽适用，但通常依赖单独训练的压缩或确定性预报组件，且因迭代去噪导致推理成本高昂。需要一种简化训练推理、消除有损压缩不确定性、并支持少步生成的端到端模型。

Method: 提出Just Weather Scoring (JWS)，一种单阶段端到端扩散模型，直接在雷达空间建模。结合掩码异步扩散（一种保留干净上下文并适应高维时空数据的时间步采样方案）与简单的评分规则目标，使训练目标与概率预报对齐并解锁少步生成能力。

Result: 在SEVIR和MeteoNet基准测试中，JWS实现了最先进的概率预报性能，同时降低了训练和推理成本。即使是最小模型也保持竞争力，参数量大幅减少，推理速度提升17倍以上。

Conclusion: JWS通过雷达空间直接建模、掩码异步扩散和评分规则目标，解决了现有扩散模型在降水临近预报中的分离训练、高推理成本和有损压缩不确定性问题，提供了一种高效、端到端的概率预报解决方案，显著提升了实用性。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models are well-suited for probabilistic precipitation nowcasting, but existing approaches often rely on separately trained compression or deterministic forecasting components and remain costly at inference due to iterative denoising. We introduce Just Weather Scoring (JWS), a single-stage, end-to-end diffusion model which addresses both issues by forecasting directly in radar space and enabling few-step generation. Radar-space modeling greatly simplifies training and inference and eliminates uncertainty arising from lossy compression. JWS combines Masked Asynchronous Diffusion, a timestep-sampling scheme that preserves clean context while adapting diffusion training to high-dimensional spatio-temporal data, with a simple scoring-rule objective that aligns training with probabilistic forecasting and unlocks few-step generation. On the SEVIR and MeteoNet benchmarks, JWS achieves state-of-the-art probabilistic forecasting performance at reduced training and inference cost. Even our smallest model remains competitive using substantially fewer parameters and more than 17x faster inference.

</details>


### [249] [DataSense-Bench: The First Step Toward an AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12190)
*Yudi Zhang,Mingyu Cao,Lu Yin,Mykola Pechenizkiy,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DataSense-Bench基准，测试前沿AI模型是否具备“数据感知”能力——即能否可靠选择高价值训练数据并预测其性能。实验显示，模型在数据选择上的增益有限，无法可靠排序子集，且排序能力跨任务不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着关于递归自我改进(RSI)和通用人工智能(AGI)的声称增多，核心问题是前沿AI模型是否具备数据感知能力：能否在无法直接训练和访问评测任务的情况下，通过检查数据、运行分析代码和前向传播，准确选择和排序用于微调的高质量训练子集。

Method: 构建DataSense-Bench基准，让AI代理从OpenThoughts-Agent和EnvScaler中选择并排序候选训练轨迹子集，用于微调小型LLM。代理可检查数据、编写执行分析代码、运行模型前向传播，但不可训练模型或访问评测任务(TBLite终端任务、BFCL工具使用任务)。随后按标准化协议微调并评测，从微调后顶选子集性能和排序准确性两个维度评估代理。

Result: 实验发现：1) 相比随机选择，代理的选择增益有限；2) 代理无法可靠排序其选定的子集组；3) 排序能力跨任务不稳定——Astra在所有3次工具使用运行中识别出最优组，但在3次终端运行中仅1次成功；4) 代理常使用相似的数据信号，但对其训练价值的解读截然不同。

Conclusion: 当前前沿AI模型缺乏可靠的数据感知能力，在数据选择和性能预测这一基础问题上表现不佳，这对依赖模型自主选择训练数据的RSI/AGI路线构成挑战。

Abstract: As claims about recursive self-improvement (RSI) and artificial general intelligence (AGI) proliferate, we ask a simple question: do frontier AI models have a sense of data, i.e., can they reliably select the right data for training? We introduce DataSense-Bench to study this capability through the fundamental problem of data selection and performance forecasting in machine learning. We ask AI agents to select and rank candidate training subsets that can be used to fine-tune a small LLM model. Agents are allowed to inspect the data, write and execute analysis code, and run model forward passes, but can not train the model or access the actual evaluation tasks. We then fine-tune the base model on each selected subset and evaluate its post-training performance under a standardized protocol. We instantiate the benchmark in terminal problem solving and tool use, selecting trajectories from OpenThoughts-Agent and EnvScaler and evaluating on TBLite and BFCL, respectively. We then evaluate the agents along two complementary dimensions: the post-training performance of the top-ranked subset, reflecting the ability to identify high-value training data, and ranking accuracy, reflecting the ability to predict the relative performance of the selected subsets. In our experiments, selection gains over random selection are limited; agents do not reliably rank their selected groups, and ranking ability does not hold consistently across tasks: Astra identifies the best group in all three tool-use runs but in only one of three terminal runs. Analysis of execution traces on both tasks shows that agents often use similar data signals while interpreting their training value differently.

</details>


### [250] [Verification with Transfer: Exact Information Frontiers and Their Price in Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12211)
*Hazar Yueksel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究验证器接受/拒绝整体答案时的信息理论极限，提出通过源任务调用来降低验证成本，建立了列表率失真函数作为最小因果信息下界，并在线性码场景下给出了闭式最优精度和可计算的预算剖面，结果经Lean 4机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决验证器在平坦先验下需要指数级验证次数的问题，探索通过源任务（课程学习或交织验证）降低验证成本的信息理论极限。

Method: 使用信息论方法，定义列表率失真函数作为交织源调用和验证所需的最小因果信息；分析精确验证器和噪声验证器下的源优先协议；针对F2上的线性码库，推导最优精度闭式解并通过多项式时间归约计算预算剖面。

Result: 证明列表率失真函数是成功概率s下的最小因果信息下界，设计源可在1+log2(5)次调用内达到唯一答案，一般情况对数项最优；精确验证器下源优先保持硬上限，交织可节省无界期望调用；噪声验证器下源优先可能损失无界因子；线性码库中零误差硬上限下的额外调用等于干扰维度；所有编号结果除两条外经Lean 4机器验证；实验显示小Transformer在潜在维度5时用尽所有比特，维度11时不使用，低XOR度不足以保证利用。

Conclusion: 确立了验证与源任务交织的信息理论基础，给出了最优策略和计算复杂度，机器验证增强了可靠性，实验揭示了Transformer利用信息的维度依赖性。

Abstract: A verifier that accepts or rejects whole answers reveals little: under a flat prior over $k$-bit answers, zero error needs $2^k-1$ verifications. The usual remedy is to solve related source tasks, either all first, as a curriculum does, or interleaved with verification. We price this remedy in information and in calls. With an exact verifier, the least causal information that any interleaving of source calls and $n$ verifications needs to succeed with probability $s$ is a list rate-distortion function, attained by one observation before any verification. It lower-bounds the expected number of binary source calls, which designed sources meet within $1+\log_25$ calls for unique answers and within a logarithmic term in general, where no additive constant suffices. With an exact verifier and fixed sources, moving every call before the first verification preserves all hard caps on calls, although interleaving can save unboundedly many expected calls; under a noisy verifier, source-first protocols can lose unbounded factors in information and in error. For linear banks over $\mathbb{F}_2$, optimal accuracy has a closed form, and after a polynomial-time reduction the budget profile is computable in time $2^{O(h^2)}\operatorname{poly}(J,k+h)$ for $J$ sources and nuisance dimension $h$. In these banks, for zero error under a hard cap, the calls beyond the rounded-up information price are exactly those spent on nuisance. Every numbered result apart from two clauses about the planner is machine-checked in Lean 4, assuming two published results. Used as a ruler, the frontier shows a small transformer using all delivered bits at latent dimension $5$ and none at $11$ within fixed training budgets; in a test with predictions recorded before training, low XOR degree of the target bits did not suffice for their use.

</details>


### [251] [Training on the Future: A Delay-Aware Audit of Test-Time Adaptation for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12232)
*Mohamed Readh Fentazi,Mazene Ameur,Adlen Ksentini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个无泄漏的测试时自适应（TTA）评估框架，严格强制标签延迟 d ≥ H，并在五个基准上对四种最新 TTA 方法及两个闭式修正器进行了因果一致的对比。结果显示：仅在 ETTm1 上自适应方法有真实增益，但简单的 RLS 中位数滤波器以极低成本击败其中三个，仅 DynaTTA 在最小延迟下胜出但计算成本高 2500 倍；在其他四数据集上，最大显著改进不足 0.5%，且至少一种方法比冻结模型更差；标签泄漏会夸大自适应收益高达 110%，而骨干训练配方对在线误差的影响（高达 25 倍）远超任何自适应效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TTA 方法在时间序列预测中常忽略标签天然的 H 步延迟及工程管线带来的额外延迟，导致评估中存在标签泄漏，高估了自适应收益。论文旨在建立因果一致的评测规范，真实衡量自适应方法在延迟标签下的效果，并揭示骨干训练配方对在线性能的主导影响。

Method: 1. 设计无泄漏评测 harness：预测起点 s 的标签仅在 s+d 释放（d ≥ H），并在四种 TTA 方法（TAFAS、COSA、PETSA、DynaTTA）的开源代码中强制执行该规则。2. 引入两个无超参闭式基线：逐坐标中位数融合的递归最小二乘（RLS）滤波器组（56 μs/步）和 ELF 风格线性修正器。3. 在五个基准（ETTm1、ETTh2、Weather、Electricity、Traffic）上，使用各方法原始骨干和检查点进行因果延迟下的对比实验，并统计显著性检验。4. 分析标签泄漏（次步更新）和骨干训练配方对在线误差的影响量级。

Result: • ETTm1：四种 TTA 均真实自适应，但 RLS 中位数滤波器以极低成本击败其中三个；仅 DynaTTA 在最小因果延迟下胜出，但单次更新成本约为 RLS 的 2500 倍；ELF 修正器全胜。• 其他四数据集：最大显著改进 ≤ 0.5%，且每个数据集上至少一种已发表方法显著劣于冻结模型；在漂移严重的 ETTh2 上，更长的标签延迟使所有脱离冻结模型的适配器（含本文基线）均显著有害。• 标签泄漏（次步更新）会夸大简单适配器收益高达 110%。• 骨干训练配方导致冻结在线误差变化高达 25 倍，远超任何自适应效应。

Conclusion: 在因果延迟标签的现实设定下，现有 TTA 方法的自适应收益极其有限且不稳定，简单的闭式修正器（RLS 中位数、ELF）往往以极低成本达到或超越复杂自适应方法。标签泄漏是以往报告高收益的主要原因，而骨干训练配方才是决定在线性能的关键因素。论文发布的无泄漏评测套件、集成补丁及全部缓存实验为未来研究提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) methods for time-series forecasting update a deployed model, or a small adapter around it, from incoming ground truth. But the label of an $H$-step forecast exists only $H$ steps later, and real data pipelines add further delay. We build a leakage-free harness in which the label of forecast origin $s$ is released for updates only at step $s+d$ with $d \ge H$, and enforce this rule inside the released code of four recent TTA methods (TAFAS, COSA, PETSA and DynaTTA), run on their own backbones and checkpoints across five benchmarks (ETTm1, ETTh2, Weather, Electricity and Traffic). As references we add two closed-form correctors: a bank of recursive least squares (RLS) filters combined by a per-coordinate median, with no tunable hyperparameters and 56 microseconds per step on the 7-channel streams, and an ELF-style linear corrector. Under causal delayed labels the picture is asymmetric. On ETTm1 every audited method genuinely adapts, yet the RLS bank still beats three of the four at a fraction of their cost; only DynaTTA beats the bank, only at the minimum causal delay, and at roughly 2,500 times the per-update cost; the ELF-style corrector beats all four. On the other four datasets, the largest statistically significant improvement any published method achieves over its own frozen checkpoint is half a percent, on all four at least one published method is significantly worse than the frozen model at the minimum causal delay, and on drift-heavy ETTh2 longer label delays make every adapter that separates from the frozen model, ours included, significantly harmful. Leaky next-step updates inflate the apparent gains of simple adapters by up to 110%, and the backbone training recipe moves frozen online error by up to a factor of 25, more than any adaptation effect we measure. We release the harness, integration patches and all cached runs.

</details>


### [252] [AdaCast: Conditional Parameter Generation for Adaptive Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12240)
*Darahaas Nallagatla,Darryl Cherian Jacob,Pan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaCast，一种条件参数生成框架，为冻结的预训练时间序列基础模型生成输入特定的低秩参数更新，实现自适应预测，在六个公开基准上持续优于静态适应基线，并提升跨域零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列基础模型适应方法多为静态的全一法，仅学习单一数据集级参数更新并应用于所有输入，无法针对每个输入序列的时序模式、季节性和动态进行个性化适应，限制了对异构输入的定制化预测能力。

Method: AdaCast采用生成器为冻结的预训练TSFM生成输入特定的低秩参数更新，这些更新在训练和推理阶段均动态适应每个输入，形成条件参数生成机制。

Result: 在六个公开基准上，AdaCast在域内预测任务中持续超越静态适应基线，且在跨域保留数据集上的零样本泛化性能显著提升。

Conclusion: 条件参数生成是实现自适应时间序列预测的有效途径，能够根据每个输入序列的特性动态调整模型参数，从而提升预测精度和泛化能力。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) have achieved strong forecasting performance across domains. However, most adaptation methods remain static. Existing all-in-one methods learn a single set of dataset-level parameter updates and apply the same adapted model to every input. As a result, they cannot adapt the model parameters to the temporal patterns, seasonality and dynamics of each input time series. This limits their ability to produce forecasts that are tailored to heterogeneous inputs. To address this limitation, we propose AdaCast, a conditional parameter generation framework for time-series forecasting. AdaCast uses a generator to produce input-specific low-rank parameter updates for a frozen pretrained TSFM. These updates adapt the model to each input during both training and inference. Across six public benchmarks, AdaCast consistently outperforms static adaptation baseline in in-domain forecasting and improves zero-shot generalization to held-out datasets across domains. These results demonstrate that conditional parameter generation provides an effective approach for adaptive forecasting.

</details>


### [253] [RIFT: Relative Isolation From Trees For Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12244)
*Mark Daniel Szalai,Gabor Horvath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RIFT（Relative Isolation From Trees），一种确定性的无监督异常检测方法，基于最小生成树并利用边的视在大小之和作为异常分数。在一维数据上，RIFT精确恢复了Isolation Forest（IF）的无限森林极限闭式解；在高维数据上，它提供了一个无参数、确定性、对密度变化和聚类异常鲁棒且避免轴平行伪影的泛化。还提出了用于大规模数据集的集成变体。实验表明其准确率与IF相当，且集成变体在随机种子上的方差显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Isolation Forest是广泛使用的无监督异常检测基线，但存在随机性、轴平行划分伪影以及对密度变化和聚类异常敏感等问题。近期研究给出了一维数据下IF无限森林极限的闭式表达，启发作者从几何角度设计确定性、无参数且更鲁棒的泛化方法。

Method: RIFT构建数据点的最小生成树（MST），对每个点计算其作为视点时MST所有边的视在大小之和作为异常分数。一维情况下该分数等同于IF的闭式极限；高维情况下自然推广。为处理大规模数据，提出基于子采样的集成变体。

Result: 在合成数据和ADBench基准上，RIFT的检测准确率与IF相当。集成变体在保持准确率的同时，显著降低了不同随机种子下的方差，表现出更强的稳定性。

Conclusion: RIFT提供了一种确定性、无参数、几何直观的异常检测方法，精确推广了IF的一维理论极限，克服了IF的轴平行伪影和随机性，且对密度变化和聚类异常鲁棒。集成版本适合大规模应用，具有更低的方差。

Abstract: Isolation Forest (IF) is a widely used baseline for unsupervised anomaly detection. Recent studies provide a closed-form expression for the infinite-forest limit for one-dimensional data. Inspired by the geometric interpretation of this formula, we introduce RIFT (Relative Isolation From Trees), a deterministic anomaly detection method that generates the minimum spanning tree and scores each point by the sum of the apparent sizes of tree edges as viewed from that point. For one-dimensional data, the RIFT score recovers the closed-form IF limit exactly. In higher dimensions, it provides a parameter-free generalization that is deterministic, robust to varying density and clustered anomalies and avoids the axis-parallel artifacts of IF. We further propose an ensemble variant for large datasets. Experiments on synthetic data and the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that the accuracy is comparable to IF, while the ensemble variant exhibits significantly lower variance across random seeds.

</details>


### [254] [Batch Before You Lift: Scalable Topological Deep Learning on Large Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12247)
*David Leko,Luka Benić,Guillermo Bernárdez,Nina Miolane,Olga Fink,Lev Telyatnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cluster-TNN是一种领域无关的框架，通过局部提升而非全局构建来解决拓扑深度学习在大规模稠密图上的内存瓶颈，实现了在Reddit和OGBN Products等大型数据集上首次训练多种高阶拓扑神经网络，平均峰值GPU内存减少83.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全域训练需要预先构建并存储完整的提升表示，在大规模稠密数据集（如Reddit：233k节点，57.3M边）上成为严重的计算瓶颈，往往导致训练不可行。

Method: Cluster-TNN在预处理阶段对输入图进行划分，运行时动态采样节点簇组，重构其诱导子图形成小批量，在每个小批量内应用选定的提升操作。保留采样节点间的所有边以保持跨簇构建高阶结构所需的连通性，生成现有拓扑神经网络可直接处理的拓扑小批量。

Result: 在21组匹配对比中，Cluster-TNN在每种配置下均降低峰值GPU内存，平均减少83.2%，同时保持有竞争力的预测性能。显著地，这种减少使得（据作者所知）首次在Reddit和OGBN Products等大型数据集上训练多种不同的高阶拓扑神经网络成为可能。

Conclusion: Cluster-TNN确立了一种通用策略，将拓扑深度学习扩展到全局域构建限制之外，为大规模图上的高阶拓扑学习提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: Topological Deep Learning extends graph-based learning to higher-order domains, such as hypergraphs, cellular, and simplicial complexes. These domains are typically constructed from patterns in an input graph through a process of graph lifting. Full-domain training constructs and stores the complete lifted representation before model execution. On large and dense datasets like Reddit (233k nodes and 57.3M edges), this global materialization becomes a severe computational bottleneck, often rendering training infeasible. To address this limitation, we introduce Cluster-TNN, a domain-agnostic framework that avoids this bottleneck by lifting locally instead. After partitioning the input graph during preprocessing, at runtime Cluster-TNN dynamically samples groups of node clusters, reconstructs their induced subgraphs to form mini-batches, and applies the chosen lifting within each mini-batch. Retaining all edges among sampled nodes preserves the connectivity needed to construct higher-order structures across clusters, producing topological mini-batches that existing Topological Neural Networks can process directly. Across 21 matched comparisons with full-graph execution, Cluster-TNN reduces peak GPU memory in every configuration, by 83.2% on average while maintaining competitive predictive performance. Notably, such a reduction enables, to our knowledge, the first training of multiple different higher-order Topological Neural Networks on large datasets such as Reddit and OGBN Products. These results establish Cluster-TNN as a general strategy for scaling Topological Deep Learning beyond the limitations of global domain construction.

</details>


### [255] [AdaptLSTM: Efficient Adaptive Online Learning for Cloud Workload Forecasting under Distribution Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12265)
*Xinhua Miao,Bowei Yang,Zhengong Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AdaptLSTM，一种自适应在线框架，通过验证校准阈值检测概念漂移并执行选择性、有针对性的模型更新，在阿里巴巴机器追踪和容器追踪数据集上以 20% 的计算成本分别恢朿 54% 和 96% 的朴素在线学习精度提升，显著优于经典漂移检测器，且模型无关（适用于 LSTM、GRU、Transformer）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模云服务中工作负载预测面临病毒式内容、产品发布、用户行为导致的分布偏移，离线训练模型迅速失效；朴素在线学习虽能恢复精度，但逐步更新计算成本过高，亟需低成本、高效率的自适应在线学习方案。

Method: AdaptLSTM 框架：1) 使用验证集校准的阈值检测回归尺度误差流中的概念漂移；2) 仅在检测到漂移时触发选择性、有针对性的模型参数更新；3) 兼容 LSTM、GRU、Transformer 等主干网络，实现模型无关的自适应在线学习。

Result: 在阿里巴巴机器追踪上，以 20% 成本恢复 54% 朴素在线提升（效率 2.7 倍，p=0.002）；在更易变的容器追踪上，以 20% 成本达成 96% 恢复（效率 4.8 倍，MAE 降低 75%）。漂移触发 42 次/301 步，显著优于 ADWIN/DDM/Page-Hinkley 等经典检测器及同预算基线。实测吞吐提升 1.33 倍，更新时间减少 45%。

Conclusion: AdaptLSTM 通过轻量级漂移检测与选择性更新，在极低计算开销下实现接近朴素在线学习的预测精度恢复，验证了其在真实云负载预测场景中的高效性、鲁棒性及模型无关性，为大规模弹性资源供给提供了实用的自适应预测范式。

Abstract: Accurate workload forecasting is critical for elastic resource provisioning in web-scale cloud services, where distribution shifts driven by viral content, product launches, and user behavior degrade offline-trained models rapidly. Naive online learning recovers accuracy but incurs prohibitive per-step compute cost. We propose AdaptLSTM, an adaptive online framework that detects drift via validation-calibrated thresholds and applies selective, targeted updates. On the Alibaba Machine Trace, AdaptLSTM recovers 54\% of Naive Online's improvement at 20\% cost ($2.7\times$ efficiency, $p=0.002$ over 10 seeds). On the more volatile Container Trace, it achieves 96\% at 20\% cost ($4.8\times$ efficiency, $+75\%$ MAE reduction over Static). Unlike classical drift detectors (ADWIN, DDM, Page-Hinkley) which fail to trigger on regression-scale error streams, AdaptLSTM fires 42 times over 301 steps and outperforms matched-budget baselines. Wall-clock profiling shows $1.33\times$ throughput gain and 45\% update-time reduction. The framework is model-agnostic: identical Pareto patterns hold for LSTM, GRU, and Transformer backbones.

</details>


### [256] [Composite Online-to-Nonconvex Conversion with Optimal Oracle Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12328)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究随机非光滑非凸复合优化问题，通过在线学习到非凸转换框架扩展到复合目标，达到最优预言机复杂度，证明凸正则化项不增加复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在随机一阶和零阶预言机下，对于非光滑非凸复合优化（目标为非光滑非凸 Lipschitz 函数加凸正则化项）缺乏最优复杂度界，且零阶方法次优。需要填补这一空白。

Method: 采用在线到非凸转换框架，引入包含正则化项本身（而非其线性化）的新损失函数，利用在线镜像下降变体实现低遗憾，从而扩展框架处理复合目标。

Result: 算法以 O(δ^{-1}ε^{-3}) 随机梯度查询或 O(d δ^{-1}ε^{-3}) 函数值查询找到 Goldstein 型稳定点，复杂度与非复合非光滑非凸优化的最优率匹配；也给出光滑情况的速率和数值实验。

Conclusion: 该工作证明凸正则化项不恶化预言机复杂度，为随机非光滑非凸复合优化提供了首个最优复杂度算法，具有理论意义和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We consider stochastic nonsmooth nonconvex composite optimization, which includes several important problems such as constrained optimization and the regularized training of neural networks. The objective is the sum of a possibly nonsmooth nonconvex Lipschitz function and a convex regularizer, and the function is accessed through stochastic gradients or function values. The goal is to find a point that satisfies a Goldstein-type stationarity condition designed for composite objectives. To our knowledge, no oracle complexity bound for this setting is known under first-order access, and existing complexities under zeroth-order access are suboptimal. To handle this issue, we employ the framework of online-to-nonconvex conversion, which chooses update directions by an online learner and is known to achieve optimal rates for noncomposite problems. We extend the framework to our composite scenario by introducing new losses for the learner, which contain the regularizer itself rather than its linearization and for which a variant of online mirror descent achieves low regret. We show that the resulting algorithm finds such a point with $O(δ^{-1}\varepsilon^{-3})$ stochastic gradient queries or $O(dδ^{-1}\varepsilon^{-3})$ function-value queries, where $δ$ is the Goldstein radius, $\varepsilon$ is the stationarity tolerance, and $d$ is the dimension. These rates match the optimal ones for noncomposite nonsmooth nonconvex optimization, demonstrating that the additional convex regularizer does not worsen the oracle complexity. We also give rates for the smooth case and present numerical experiments.

</details>


### [257] [Ambient Discrete Diffusion: Using the Wrong Data at the Right Time for Data Efficient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12340)
*Julian Kleutgens,Mauricio Tec,Claudio Battiloro,Francesca Dominici,Giannis Daras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RefineMix是一个用于在严重数据稀缺下训练离散扩散模型的框架，通过在选定扩散时间步使用分布外数据提高泛化能力，不偏移采样分布。在蛋白质序列生成任务中，仅用197个域内样本微调即可使生成蛋白质同时具备新颖、可折叠、同族的比例几乎翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学应用中常面临严重数据稀缺；离散扩散模型中掩码保留域信息，导致高噪声下难以利用相关数据，但低噪声下域间不相交支持反而成为优势，可同时利用域内和域外数据而不偏移采样器。

Method: 提出RefineMix框架，在选定扩散时间步引入分布外数据训练，形式化直觉并提供理论分析；利用低噪声下域支持不相交的特性，避免采样分布偏移。

Result: 在五个域迁移设置上实验，RefineMix匹配或优于域内微调和数据混合；蛋白质序列生成中，仅197个域内样本微调使生成蛋白质同时满足新颖、可折叠、同族的比例几乎翻倍。

Conclusion: RefineMix有效解决离散扩散模型在数据稀缺下的泛化问题，通过巧妙利用不同时间步的域外数据，实现无偏采样的性能提升，在科学应用如蛋白质设计中展现巨大潜力。

Abstract: We introduce RefineMix, a framework for training discrete diffusion models under severe data scarcity, a common constraint in scientific applications. RefineMix uses out-of-distribution data at selected diffusion times to improve generalization without biasing the sampling distribution. Although this strategy has been explored in continuous diffusion, discrete diffusion presents a distinct challenge: unlike Gaussian noise, masking preserves domain information in surviving tokens, limiting the use of related data at high noise levels. At low noise levels, however, the domains effectively disjoint supports become an advantage, allowing the model to learn from both in-domain and out-of-distribution data without biasing the sampler. We formalize these intuitions and provide a theoretical analysis for the proposed method. Experimentally, across five domain-shift settings, RefineMix matches or outperforms in-domain finetuning and data mixing. For protein sequence generation, finetuning with just 197 in-domain examples nearly doubles the fraction of generated proteins that are simultaneously novel, foldable, and in-family compared to standard finetuning.

</details>


### [258] [SplitJEPA: Learning Invariant and Variant Latent Worlds without Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12349)
*Ruijin Hua,Zichuan Liu,Zhuokai Zhao,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SplitJEPA 是一种无需重构的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），能在表示空间中直接联合恢复潜在状态及其不变-可变子空间分解。在平稳高斯预测动力学与满秩变分条件下，理论证明其可将不变与可变子空间识别至独立分块等距变换等价，实验在合成非线性系统与机器人操作任务上验证了鲁棒性与效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在状态分解方法多依赖重构（解码器），要求潜在变量先解释完整观测世界才能信任其组织结构；JEPA 虽然直接建模潜在状态且无需重构，但缺乏恢复不变/可变部分的理论保证。如何在不付出重构代价的前提下学习潜在世界的不变-可变结构仍是开放问题。

Method: 提出 SplitJEPA：在 JEPA 框架下引入显式的不变/可变分块结构，直接在表示空间联合预测与分解，无需观测解码器。理论分析基于平稳高斯预测动力学假设与满秩变分条件，证明不变与可变子空间可被识别至独立分块等距变换。

Result: 理论上证明 SplitJEPA 能在无解码器条件下识别不变/可变子空间（至分块等距变换）；合成非线性系统与机器人推箱子任务实验表明，该方法在视角变换、光照变化等干扰下保持动作一致性，同时提升样本效率与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SplitJEPA 填补了无重构潜在恢复到不变-可变分块识别的理论空白，为强化学习与机器人控制中学习鲁棒、可泛化的状态表示提供了新范式。

Abstract: Understanding a dynamical world calls for more than a latent state that summarizes its observations: the state should also be organized into the factors that stay shared across related observations and the factors that vary between them. For example, a robot pushing a cube to a goal should take the same action when the camera shifts or the lights dim, since nothing in the scene has moved. Existing approaches to this decomposition commonly obtain it through reconstruction, so the latent variables must first explain the entire observational world before their organization can be trusted. Joint embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) model the latent state directly and never reconstruct, yet no existing result recovers the invariant and variant parts of the state they learn. How to learn the invariant-variant structure of the latent world without paying for its reconstruction therefore remains open. To close this gap, we introduce SplitJEPA, a JEPA that jointly recovers the latent state and its invariant and variant organization directly in representation space, without any reconstruction. We prove that, under stationary Gaussian predictive dynamics and a full-rank variation condition, SplitJEPA identifies the invariant and variant subspaces up to independent block-wise isometries, without introducing an observation decoder. Since the guarantee needs no decoder, the result extends reconstruction-free latent recovery to invariant-variant block identification. Experiments on synthetic nonlinear systems and robotic manipulation tasks support the theoretical results and show their practical value for both robustness and efficiency.

</details>


### [259] [Closing the Horizon Gap in Policy Optimization for Adversarial MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12362)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对在线情景表格马尔可夫决策过程(具有对抗性损失和区间反馈)中的策略优化问题，通过引入正则化Q函数，解决了现有策略优化算法比占用度量算法多出H倍遗憾界的差距，实现了已知转移Õ(√(HS(H+A)T))和未知转移Õ(HS√(AT))的高概率遗憾界，后者达到最优界，并将该方法扩展到对抗性线性混合MDPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的策略优化算法在局部更新策略时，其遗憾界比基于占用度量多面体优化的算法大一个地平线H因子。本文旨在通过联合控制所有状态-动作对的更新稳定性，消除这一差距，提升策略优化在对抗性MDP中的性能保证。

Method: 提出使用正则化Q函数，使得局部策略更新能够跨所有状态-动作对联合控制稳定性，而非分别在每个状态独立控制。设计基于此的策略优化算法，并推广到对抗性线性混合MDP设置。

Result: 算法实现了两种设置下的高概率遗憾界：已知转移时为Õ(√(HS(H+A)T))，未知转移时为Õ(HS√(AT))。两者均改进了现有策略优化算法的地平线依赖性，后者达到目前已知最优界。在对抗性线性混合MDPs中也获得相同的地平线依赖性改进。

Conclusion: 通过正则化Q函数实现的联合稳定性控制，成功缩小了策略优化与占用度量方法在对抗性MDP中的遗憾界差距，证明了策略优化可以达到最优的地平线依赖性，且方法可推广到更复杂的线性混合MDP设置。

Abstract: We consider policy optimization for online episodic tabular Markov decision processes (MDPs) with adversarial losses and bandit feedback. Policy optimization updates the policy locally at each state and avoids optimization over the occupancy-measure polytope, but its existing regret bounds are larger by a factor of the horizon $H$ than those of occupancy-measure-based algorithms. We close this gap by using regularized $Q$-functions, which allow us to control the stability of the local updates jointly over all state-action pairs rather than separately at each state. The resulting algorithm attains high-probability regret bounds of $\widetilde O(\sqrt{HS(H+A)T})$ for known transitions and $\widetilde O(HS\sqrt{AT})$ for unknown transitions, where $S$ is the number of states, $A$ the number of actions, and $T$ the number of episodes. Both bounds improve the horizon dependence of existing policy optimization bounds, and the latter matches the best-known bound. We further extend the algorithm to adversarial linear-mixture MDPs and obtain the same improvement in the horizon dependence.

</details>


### [260] [Bilevel optimization for data-driven learning of Koopman embeddings using kernel-based autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12370)
*Joel-Pascal Ntwali N'konzi,Feliks Nüske,Stefan Klus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核的字典学习方法EDMD-kDL，用于从数据中直接学习有限维Koopman嵌入，结合配点法和双层优化，在多个实验中达到或优于ANN方法，且具有可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman算子理论为非线性动力系统提供线性分析框架，但EDMD等有限维近似需要预先指定字典；现有机器学习方法多用ANN自编码器学习字典，而核方法具有更好的可解释性和理论分析便利性，却鲜受关注。

Method: 提出EDMD-kDL，结合配点法和双层优化，同时学习核字典和对应的Koopman近似，直接从数据中学习有限维Koopman嵌入。

Result: 在全球海表温度预测和视频数据学习等数值实验中，EDMD-kDL性能可比或优于最先进的ANN方法；且核矩阵规模取决于配点数而非训练数据量，设计上可扩展到大规模数据集。

Conclusion: EDMD-kDL提供了一种可解释、可扩展且性能优异的核基字典学习方法，为Koopman算子近似提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Koopman operator theory provides a linear framework for analyzing nonlinear dynamical systems and has become a major tool for data-driven modeling. A central challenge, however, is that finite-dimensional approximations computed by methods such as extended dynamic mode decomposition (EDMD) require the dictionary to be specified a priori. Recent machine-learning approaches address this limitation by learning the dictionary from data, predominantly using artificial neural network (ANN) autoencoder architectures. Although kernel methods offer an alternative with greater interpretability and tractability for theoretical analysis, they have received little attention in this setting. We introduce extended dynamic mode decomposition with kernel-based dictionary learning (EDMD-kDL), a kernel-based method for learning finite-dimensional Koopman embeddings directly from data. The method combines ideas from collocation methods and bilevel optimization to simultaneously learn a kernel dictionary and the corresponding Koopman approximation. We evaluate EDMD-kDL against state-of-the-art ANN-based approaches on a range of numerical experiments, including global sea-surface-temperature forecasting and learning directly from video data. Across all tested settings, EDMD-kDL achieves performance comparable to or better than the ANN-based methods. Moreover, in contrast to standard kernel methods, the proposed approach is scalable to large datasets by design since the size of the required kernel matrices depends on the number of collocation points rather than the size of the training dataset.

</details>


### [261] [Marformer: A Transformer for Predicting Missing Data Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12379)
*Prabhav Singh,Xiheng Tom Wang,Haojun Shi,Jason Eisner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Real decisions are made under incomplete information. If we observe only some of the random variables we need, we can predict the others. The \textbf{conditional marginals} over the missing variables are the key ingredient for computing Bayes risk and Value of Information (VOI), the expected gain from acquiring one more observation before deciding. We present the Marformer, a Transformer trained to directly predict conditional marginals given any set of observed values. Like BERT, which is trained to predict missing words from context, the Marformer constructs a hidden-vector representation for each distribution $p(X_i)$ and iteratively refines it through attention to other distributions $p(X_j)$. Unlike generative approaches, the Marformer does not model the full joint distribution, requires no domain knowledge of the data-generating process, and makes all predictions in a single forward pass. We evaluate across three synthetic domains with missing data---Bayesian networks, discretized multivariate Gaussians, and structured annotation data. The Marformer can match or outperform classical missing-data methods, even when those methods are given the true model family and prior that generated the synthetic data. We also evaluate on a real annotation dataset, where the Marformer outperforms the evaluated baselines at the largest training size. In both cases, the Marformer is substantially faster than the evaluated generative baselines.

</details>


### [262] [Prospective Prediction of OOD Degradation from Source-Side Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12397)
*Sasha,Monin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了持久的分布外（OOD）性能下降可以在直接观察到之前，仅利用源域训练动态进行预测。在受控的捷径学习设置中，简单的逻辑回归预测器展现出明显的前瞻性信号，且训练时的时间序列摘要比当前数值更具信息量。置信度和熵动态在从CNN迁移到MLP时仍保留显著预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在解决能否在OOD失效实际发生前，仅通过源域训练过程中的动态信号来预测未来的持久性OOD性能下降问题。这对于模型部署前的风险评估和早期干预具有重要意义。

Method: 采用受控的捷径学习实验设置，使用逻辑回归预测器分析源域训练过程中的时间序列动态（如置信度、熵等数量的时间摘要），并测试这些动态特征在不同架构（CNN到MLP）间的迁移性，且无需额外训练。

Result: 逻辑回归预测器在训练早期就发展出清晰的前瞻性预测信号，而单纯的训练时间无预测力。源域数量的时间摘要（如累积统计）比瞬时值显著更有信息量。置信度和熵的动态模式在跨架构迁移（CNN→MLP）时仍保留大量预测信息。

Conclusion: 源域训练动态包含针对未来OOD失效的早期预警信号这一概念得到原理性验证。这为基于训练过程监控的OOD鲁棒性预测提供了理论基础和实证支持。

Abstract: We study whether persistent out-of-distribution (OOD) degradation can be predicted before it is directly observed using only source-side training dynamics. In a controlled shortcut-learning setting, a simple logistic regression predictor develops a clear prospective signal, while training time alone does not. Temporal summaries of the source-side quantities are substantially more informative than their current values. When transferred without additional training from a CNN to an MLP, confidence and entropy dynamics retain substantial predictive information. These results provide a proof of principle that source-side training dynamics can contain an early warning signal for future OOD failure.

</details>


### [263] [Learning Kilometer-Scale Weather Prediction with Global-Regional Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12401)
*Guowen Li,Yang Liu,Yujie Wang,Qiuyan Sun,Haoyuan Liang,Juepeng Zheng,Hong Cheng,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Kilometer-scale regional weather forecasting is essential for local weather warnings and weather-sensitive decisions. Existing data-driven approaches often rely on numerical forecasts for large-scale guidance or require additional training of global forecasting components. Pretrained global weather models offer an efficient source of large-scale forecasts, motivating their reuse to guide high-resolution regional prediction. However, this coupling requires aligning global and regional representations across different grids and integrating global guidance with local interactions to advance regional states. We propose ScaleCast, a regional forecasting framework that addresses these challenges through Global-Regional Alignment. Its Global-Regional Conversion module aligns joint global and regional representations with regional locations, while the Global-Regional Alignment and Dynamics block combines aligned guidance with regional neighborhood interactions. Experiments using ERA5 global analyses on a 0.25-degree grid and CERRA regional reanalysis at 5.5 km spacing demonstrate improved regional forecasts across surface and upper-air variables, with a single trained model supporting multiple global forecast drivers (i.e., Pangu-Weather, GraphCast, and HRES) without specific retraining. Fine-tuning on HRRR at 3 km spacing further demonstrates the framework's adaptability to a different regional domain and spatial resolution. Windstorm case studies show improved cyclone positioning and core-pressure estimates, while comparisons with HadISD station observations show closer agreement with local temperature and humidity changes.

</details>


### [264] [A Unified Bellman Operator for Safety-Critical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12420)
*Nishanth Arun Rao,Royina Karegoudra Jayanth,Benjamin Eysenbach,Jaime Fernández Fisac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的Bellman算子将性能与安全目标统一为联合价值函数，采用双时间尺度随机近似框架的时序差分学习，快时间尺度估计安全价值，慢时间尺度估计联合价值，理论保证收敛到最优安全约束任务价值函数，实验显示稳定收敛且测试时安全违规近乎零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键领域，强化学习需在最大化任务性能的同时严格遵守安全约束。现有安全强化学习范式通常面临权衡：要么需要先验知识提供严格安全保证（如安全过滤器），要么只能在平均意义上满足安全约束。本文旨在解决这一权衡，实现同时学习性能与安全并提供严格安全保证。

Method: 提出一种新的Bellman算子，将性能与安全目标统一为联合价值函数。采用双时间尺度随机近似框架的时序差分学习：快时间尺度估计学习联合策略的安全价值，慢时间尺度估计联合价值。通过将极限动态表述为占用率平均微分包含，证明其渐近收敛到一组极限最优安全约束任务价值函数。

Result: 理论上证明收敛后的最优策略在最大化任务回报的同时始终保持安全。在连续控制任务上使用神经网络近似的实证评估显示稳定收敛且测试时安全违规近乎零。

Conclusion: 该方法成功统一了性能与安全目标，通过双时间尺度学习实现严格安全约束下的最优策略学习，理论保证收敛且实验验证有效性，为安全关键领域的强化学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in safety-critical domains requires maximizing task performance while strictly adhering to safety constraints. Existing safe reinforcement learning paradigms typically force a trade-off: they either require a priori knowledge to provide strict safety guarantees (e.g., safety filters), or they enable joint learning but only satisfy safety constraints on average. In this work, we propose a novel Bellman operator that unifies performance and safety objectives into a joint value function. We show that temporal difference learning with the joint Bellman operator converges under a two-timescale stochastic approximation framework. On the fast timescale, the safety value of the learning joint policy is estimated, while the joint value is estimated on the slow timescale. Convergence is ensured by formulating the limiting dynamics as an occupation-averaged differential inclusion, and showing that it asymptotically converges to a set of limiting optimal safety-constrained task value functions. Theoretically, once converged, the resulting optimal policy maximizes task return while maintaining safety at all times. Empirical evaluations on continuous control tasks with neural approximations demonstrate stable convergence with near-zero safety violations at test time.

</details>


### [265] [Rounding in Preconditioner Space: Redesigning 4-bit AdamW Optimizer-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12444)
*Hanyang Li,Shao Tang,Daniel Thomas Braithwaite,Gregory Dexter,Leonardo Neves,Aman Gupta,Hiroto Udagawa,Abhishek Shivanna,Daniel Silva,Rohan Ramanath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种4-bit AdamW优化器状态量化方法（ZIP-SR和ZE-EDEN），从“舍入空间”视角重新设计，显著缩小与32-bit AdamW的验证损失差距，在130M-2.7B参数模型上实现高达70%的差距减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化AdamW优化器状态可减少持久存储，但量化误差会通过动量递推传播并扰乱后续自适应更新。现有量化方法在状态空间舍入，导致二阶矩量化误差在预条件子空间被放大，尤其靠近零的量化单元格分析表明：小的平均状态误差不意味着下一步的小平均预条件子误差。

Method: 提出两种互补方案：1) ZIP-SR（零包容预条件子空间随机舍入）：在二阶矩码本中保留零，在预条件子空间计算随机舍入概率。2) ZE-EDEN（零排除EDEN校准）：使用零排除的二阶矩码本，重新缩放量化后的二阶矩块以减轻正量化下限导致的预条件子失真。两者均对一阶矩使用4-bit NormalFloat (NF4)，并在训练最后10%对LM-head一阶矩进行针对性随机舍入。

Result: 在GPT和Llama风格的预训练实验中（130M至2.7B参数），两种方法均在每个评估模型规模上减小TorchAO 4-bit AdamW与32-bit AdamW的平均验证损失差距，最大差距减少达70%。全参数监督微调中，两种配方验证损失均低于TorchAO，在下游任务上接近32-bit AdamW表现。

Conclusion: 从舍入空间视角重新设计优化器状态量化能有效解决量化误差在动量递推中的传播问题，ZIP-SR和ZE-EDEN为4-bit AdamW提供了实用且高效的量化方案，在多种模型规模和任务上均显著优于现有TorchAO 4-bit AdamW基线。

Abstract: Quantizing AdamW's optimizer states reduces persistent storage, but quantization errors propagate through the moment recurrences and perturb subsequent adaptive updates. We redesign 4-bit optimizer-state quantization for AdamW from the perspective of \emph{rounding space}: the coordinate in which a quantizer chooses between adjacent reconstruction levels. For the second moment, a local analysis of the quantization cell adjacent to zero shows that small mean state error need not imply small mean preconditioner error at the next step. A one-dimensional quadratic construction further shows qualitatively different optimization dynamics under state-space and preconditioner-space rounding. These results motivate Zero-Inclusive Preconditioner-space Stochastic Rounding (\textbf{ZIP-SR}), which retains zero in the second-moment codebook and computes stochastic-rounding probabilities in preconditioner space. As a complementary route, Zero-Excluding EDEN calibration (\textbf{ZE-EDEN}) uses a zero-excluding second-moment codebook and rescales the quantized second-moment block to mitigate the preconditioner distortion caused by the positive quantization floor. Both configurations use 4-bit NormalFloat (NF4) for the first moment, with targeted stochastic rounding of the LM-head first moment during the final 10\% of training. Across GPT- and Llama-style pretraining experiments ranging from \textbf{130M} to \textbf{2.7B} parameters, both methods reduce TorchAO 4-bit AdamW's mean validation-loss gap to 32-bit AdamW at every evaluated model size, with the largest reported gap reduction reaching \textbf{70\%}. In full-parameter supervised fine-tuning, both recipes achieve lower validation loss than TorchAO while remaining close to 32-bit AdamW on downstream tasks.

</details>


### [266] [Caught in the Act: Probes Effectively Detect Sabotage and Catch Unverbalized Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12445)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth,Alex F. Spies,Tigist Diriba,Adam Gleave,Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的白盒欺骗检测方法，通过构建最大规模的欺骗数据集FIBS和创新的探针架构，在前沿模型监控任务中实现了98.8%的AUC，超越了文本监控基线，并在内省欺骗检测中达到99.7% AUC，同时能有效检测开源模型在政治敏感话题和压力下的欺骗行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期事件凸显了监控LLM代理的挑战及模型欺骗人类的风险，现有方法难以扩展到前沿部署场景，需要一种可大规模部署的白盒欺骗检测技术。

Method: 1) 收集最大规模欺骗数据集FIBS用于训练探针；2) 设计新型探针架构，能跨多层和多token聚合信息；3) 在SHADE-Arena基准上评估，并测试内省欺骗（需通过精心诱导或训练数据知识才能确定真值）及开源模型在敏感话题和压力下的欺骗行为。

Result: 探针在SHADE-Arena达到98.8% AUC，超越Opus 5.5文本监控基线；随底层模型规模增大效果提升；在内省欺骗任务中区分模型真实隐藏目标达99.7% AUC；亦能有效检测开源模型在政治敏感话题和信念施压下的欺骗。

Conclusion: 白盒探针方法可大规模部署于前沿模型监控，FIBS数据集的发布将推动社区进一步扩展欺骗和破坏行为示例，为更安全的LLM部署提供关键工具。

Abstract: Recent incidents have highlighted the challenge of monitoring LLM agents and the danger of models deceiving people. We show that white-box deception detection via probes can be scaled up to frontier monitoring settings by collecting the largest deception dataset to date for training probes and introducing a novel probe architecture which can aggregate information across many layers and tokens. Our probes achieve 98.8% AUC in SHADE-Arena, surpassing an Opus 5.5 text-monitoring baseline, and show improved efficacy as the underlying model is scaled up. To push our probes to their limit, we test them on several cases where deception cannot be determined from the context alone. In these cases, which we refer to as introspective deception, the ground truth can only be determined through careful elicitation or thorough knowledge of a model's training data. In one such evaluation, we show that probes can distinguish transcripts containing a model's true hidden goal from other goals with an AUC of up to 99.7%. Our probes also readily detect deception on prominent open-weight models which lie about politically sensitive topics, and about their beliefs when put under pressure. We release our training dataset, dubbed FIBS, to help drive frontier deployment of effective probes, and encourage the community to expand upon it with further examples of deception and sabotage.

</details>


### [267] [Bi-FORK: Generative Modeling of High-Dimensional Bifurcating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12449)
*Anna Zimmel,Fleur Hendriks,Markus Holzleitner,Florian Sestak,Martin Weichselbaumer,Vlado Menkovski,Johannes Brandstetter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Bi-FORK，一种用于学习高维物理系统分叉行为中一对多解映射的生成框架，通过潜在流匹配和排斥引导采样恢复不同解分支，并在多个系统上验证了其有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分叉普遍存在于物理系统中（如结构屈曲、流体和气候动力学），但在深度学习中鲜有探索。对称性破缺分叉导致单一输入对应多个等效解，违背了大多数学习物理代理模型的一对一假设，因此需要能处理一对多映射的生成方法。

Method: 提出Bi-FORK生成框架，采用潜在流匹配生成完整轨迹，保持时空相干性；利用排斥引导采样在单次摊销前向传递中恢复不同解分支。

Result: 在屈曲梁、机械超材料和Allen-Cahn相分离三个基准上评估，涵盖连续、离散和场值分叉，离散化点数高达260,000。Bi-FORK成功恢复多模态解结构，并比现有方法扩展了数个数量级。

Conclusion: Bi-FORK为高维分叉物理系统的生成建模开辟了道路，能够高效学习一对多解映射，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Bifurcations are ubiquitous in physical systems, from structural buckling to fluid and climate dynamics, yet they remain largely unexplored in deep learning. At a symmetry-breaking bifurcation, a single input admits multiple equally valid solutions, violating the one-to-one assumption underlying most learned physical surrogates. We introduce Bi-FORK, a generative framework for learning these one-to-many solution maps in high-dimensional systems. Bi-FORK generates complete trajectories through latent flow matching, preserving space and time coherence, and uses repulsion-guided sampling to recover distinct solution branches in a single amortized pass. We evaluate Bi-FORK on buckling beams, mechanical metamaterials, and Allen-Cahn phase separation, spanning continuous, discrete, and field-valued bifurcations with discretizations up to 260,000 points. Bi-FORK recovers the multimodal solution structure while scaling several orders of magnitude beyond prior approaches, opening generative modeling to high-dimensional bifurcating physical systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [An Explainable Header-Centric Framework for Large-Scale Semantic Table Interpretation and Data Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10541)
*Marcelo Valentim Silva,Hannes Herrmann,Valerie Maxville*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可解释的、以表头为中心的元数据专用列类型标注（CTA）和数据质量评估（DQA）框架，将表头映射到39种可解释的FinalFormat类型，通过SourceKeywords保留令牌级可追溯性，基于数据质量问题（DQI）分类激活验证规则，聚合生成HeadersIQ质量指标，并在多基准测试中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱质量不仅取决于下游图验证，还取决于集成前表格元数据的质量；在仅有元数据的语义表解释（STI）场景下，单元格值不可用、嘈杂或不适用，表头成为可追溯KG准备的关键语义证据来源。

Method: 框架利用精选词汇资源将表头映射到39种可解释的FinalFormat类型，通过SourceKeywords保留令牌级可追溯性；每个分配的类型基于数据质量问题（DQI）分类激活验证规则，检测缺失数据、重复、域违规、错误数据类型、时间不匹配等问题；将检测结果聚合为轻量级、无权重的数据源级质量指标HeadersIQ；并提供并行KG映射路径支持对齐DBpedia和Schema.org。

Result: 在UCI、Prague、Kaggle、VizNet/Sato、SOTAB、T2Dv2、SemTab 2024 Metadata-to-KG等异构基准（约12万表头列）上评估，展示了对噪声真实元数据的广泛实用覆盖；SemTab 2024官方GT-strict评估表现一般，但盲审诊断审计显示许多不匹配源于基准粒度、别名和本体选择效应，而非完全不可预测的表头中心预测；报告审计作为分歧模式的诊断证据，而非修正基准性能。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种可复用的工作流，用于元数据驱动的语义标注、数据源级质量监控和面向知识图谱的基准诊断，强调了表头中心方法在元数据专用场景下的可解释性和实用性。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph (KG) quality depends not only on downstream graph validation, but also on the quality of tabular metadata used before integration. In metadata-only Semantic Table Interpretation (STI), where cell values are unavailable, noisy, or unsuitable, column headers become a critical source of semantic evidence for traceable KG preparation.
  We present an explainable, header-centric framework for metadata-only Column Type Annotation (CTA) and Data Quality Assessment (DQA). The framework maps headers to 39 interpretable FinalFormat types using curated lexical resources and preserves token-level traceability through SourceKeywords. Each assigned type activates validation rules based on a taxonomy of Data Quality Issues (DQIs), producing detections such as missing data, duplicates, domain violations, wrong data type, and temporal mismatch. These detections are aggregated into HeadersIQ, a lightweight, unweighted data source-level quality metric.
  The framework was evaluated across heterogeneous benchmarks, including UCI, Prague, Kaggle, VizNet/Sato, SOTAB, T2Dv2, and the SemTab 2024 Metadata-to-KG track, comprising around 120,000 header columns. The results show broad practical coverage across noisy real-world metadata, while a parallel KG-mapping pathway supports alignment to DBpedia and Schema.org. On the SemTab 2024 Metadata-to-KG track, the official GT-strict evaluation was modest. However, a blinded diagnostic audit indicates that many mismatches reflect benchmark granularity, aliasing, and ontology-selection effects rather than wholly implausible header-centric predictions. We report this audit as diagnostic evidence on disagreement patterns, not as revised benchmark performance. Overall, the paper presents a reusable workflow for metadata-driven semantic annotation, data source-level quality monitoring, and KG-oriented benchmark diagnosis.

</details>


### [269] [Synthesis Through Simulation: Generating Coherent Enterprise Data via Scalable Agent-System Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10549)
*Yipeng Li,Ashutosh Hathidara,Jane Lo,Harshavardhan Abichandani,Gunraj Singh,Atin Ghosh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需数据库模式的数据合成范式——Synthesis Through Simulation (STS)，利用LLM智能体在模拟企业环境中通过执行API操作生成数据，保证结构有效性并解耦有效性约束与分布建模。其通用填充器在十个环境中无需模式即可实现0.88平均边际保真度和100%约束满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI中工具调用智能体的大规模训练和评估受限于业务和法律对企业系统、数据及数据库模式的限制。表格数据合成受限于结构有效性和模式可用性，而基于过程的方法虽能保证有效性但缺乏分布保真度，且需要领域专家编写。

Method: STS范式：LLM智能体在模拟企业环境中通过执行策略强制的API操作生成数据。数据由定义有效性的同一环境生成，从而天然保证结构有效性，并将有效性强制与分布建模解耦。通用填充器作为领域无关智能体解决分布保真度和合成规模问题。

Result: 通用填充器在所有十个环境中实现0.88平均边际保真度和100%约束满足，且无需访问数据库模式。统计合成器因需要种子数据而在七个环境中不可用；拥有模式特权的智能体在航空公司环境的紧耦合工作流中82%的轨迹失败，原因是脆弱的任务组合。

Conclusion: STS通过构造保证结构有效性，将有效性强制与分布建模独立处理。开源了完整框架、十个环境及生成数据集，为企业级工具调用智能体的大规模训练和评估提供了可行的数据合成方案。

Abstract: Tool-calling agents have become central to enterprise AI, yet training and evaluating them at scale remains severely constrained due to business and legal restrictions on enterprise systems, data, and database schemas. Tabular data synthesis offers a natural alternative, but its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by structural validity and schema availability, while procedure-based approaches yield the opposite weakness, typically lacking distributional fidelity without per-domain authoring. We introduce **Synthesis Through Simulation** (STS), a **schema--free** data synthesis paradigm in which an LLM agent generates data by executing operations against policy-enforcing APIs within simulated enterprise environments. Because data is generated through the same environment that defines what is valid, STS guarantees structural validity by construction while decoupling validity enforcement from distribution modeling, allowing each to be addressed independently. The **Generalist Populator** (GP), STS's domain-agnostic agent, addresses the remaining challenges of distributional fidelity and synthesis scalability: GP achieves **0.88** average marginal fidelity and **100\% constraint satisfaction** across all ten environments *without access to DB schemas*, while statistical synthesizers are inapplicable to seven due to necessary seed data requirements, and schema-privileged agents fail 82\% of trajectories on airline environment's tightly coupled workflows due to brittle task composition. We open-source the full framework, all ten environments, and generated datasets at https://github.com/SAP/synthesis-through-simulation.

</details>


### [270] [Agent-Controlled Forgetting for Tool-Using Agents: Reversible Context Curation in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10590)
*Jan-Peter Franke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种智能体可控遗忘机制，允许模型将冗长的工具观察结果替换为简短笔记，并将原始内容归档以便恢复。实验显示在噪声密集的工具使用轨迹中可显著减少上下文令牌和API成本，但效果强依赖于工作负载类型，且可能增加请求次数和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型智能体在交互过程中会积累大量冗长的观察结果，其中有用信息往往远小于原始载荷。现有方法难以在不丢失关键信息的前提下动态压缩上下文，因此需要一种可逆的、模型可控的上下文管理机制。

Method: 设计了一种智能体可控遗忘框架：行动模型选择历史工具结果，将其在原位置替换为简短笔记，完整原始内容存入可恢复归档。提供Python工具包支持批量归档与显式恢复，无需任务特定训练，并保护用户指令和助手消息不受影响。

Result: 在OpenTelemetry调试案例中，该方法将提示令牌从912,492降至231,951，累积输入令牌减少50%，API成本从约4.38美元降至1.28-1.44美元。两组均通过主要行为预言机测试，但后续评估均未完全满足。代价是请求数增加、耗时延长17%。在应用开发任务中未观察到上下文或成本节省，早期续写实验显示质量下降尽管上下文减少。

Conclusion: 可逆上下文管理在噪声工具使用轨迹中能带来显著资源节省，但效果高度依赖工作负载特性。工作负载依赖性是设计此类机制的核心考量，需权衡令牌节省与请求开销、延迟及输出质量。

Abstract: Tool-using agents repeatedly carry observations whose useful content can be much smaller than their original payload. We study agent-controlled forgetting: the acting model selects previously observed tool results, replaces each with a short note at its original position, and retains the exact original in a recoverable archive. A Python harness exposes batch archival and explicit recovery without task-specific model training, while protecting user instructions and assistant messages from these operations. In an exploratory OpenTelemetry debugging case followed by an unrelated implementation task, the method ended with 231,951 provider-reported prompt tokens versus 912,492 under retained history, used 50% fewer cumulative input tokens, and had an estimated API cost of USD 1.28-1.44 versus approximately USD 4.38. Both arms passed the two-case primary behavioral oracle; neither fully satisfied the follow-up evaluation. The method made more requests and took 17% longer. A contrasting application-development pair produced no context or cost saving, and an earlier continuation exhibited lower manually assessed quality despite reduced context. These observations demonstrate substantial resource savings in noisy tool-use trajectories and identify workload dependence as a central consideration for reversible context management.

</details>


### [271] [Verification and Self-Improvement in Agentic AI: Foundations and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10611)
*Chien-Ping Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个用于分析智能体AI系统自我改进的验证框架，通过有界验证和隐藏终端随机性，形式化定义了原生触达与闭包前沿两种语言，证明了多数放大保持语言不变而存在性接受可能引入错误，建立了随机验证者复杂度类与多项式层级的关系，并给出了递归自我改进在统一验证协议下保持验证类不变的结果，最后通过配额强制的XOR合成族将搜索成功率与语言变化分离，将自我改进主张与正确性义务、证据要求、验证资源和选择误差联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有性能分数无法区分智能体AI系统通过延长搜索、增加支持或修改提议/验证机制来改进的不同方式，需要一种形式化框架来严格分析这些改进机制的验证属性、复杂度边界及自我改进的正确性保证。

Method: 构建基于阶段的验证框架，包含允许转录、多项式界限、交替验证协议和终端检查器；定义原生触达（默认支持）和闭包前沿（接口允许的所有支持）两种语言；在均匀逐点概率间隙和任务相对可靠性假设下分析；使用独立多数放大、存在性接受、精确验证（零随机性）、随机验证者复杂度类（Σ_k^RV）、表示分析、配额强制的XOR合成族等技术工具。

Result: 证明独立多数放大保持两种语言不变，存在性接受可能接受错误输出；精确验证为零随机性情况，给出定位和完备性结果；随机验证者类满足 Σ_k^P ⊆ Σ_k^RV ⊆ Σ_{k+1}^P，严格扩大和深度分离需显式复杂度假设，BPP=P时得到相同前沿的精确伴随类；表示分析分离不变接受与可重构下变化的核心/支持标签；统一有界自我修改在相同验证类内；条件误差预算控制自适应候选的虚假选择；XOR合成族分离无界搜索成功比与语言变化；精确和概率审计检查证据要求。

Conclusion: 该框架将自我改进主张形式化为正确性、可接受证据、验证资源和选择误差四方面的义务，为智能体AI系统的递归自我改进提供了严格的理论基础和验证标准。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems can improve by searching longer, receiving additional support, or modifying how they propose and verify outputs. A performance score does not distinguish these mechanisms. We compare these changes through bounded verification with hidden terminal randomness. A stage specifies admissible transcripts, polynomial bounds, an alternating verification protocol, and a terminal checker. Its native reach uses default support; its closure frontier permits all support already admitted by the interface. Under a uniform pointwise probability gap and task-relative soundness, these are well-defined languages. We prove that independent majority amplification preserves both languages, whereas existential acceptance over random tapes can admit incorrect outputs. Exact verification is the zero-randomness case, with placement and completeness results. The randomized-verifier classes satisfy $Σ_k^{\mathrm{P}}\subseteqΣ_k^{\mathrm{RV}}\subseteqΣ_{k+1}^{\mathrm{P}}$; strict enlargement and depth separation require explicit complexity assumptions, while $\mathrm{BPP}=\mathrm{P}$ yields exact companions with the same frontiers. Representation analysis separates invariant acceptance from core-versus-support labels that can change under refactoring. For recursive self-improvement, uniformly bounded self-modification under a common sound interpreter and fixed verification protocol remains within the same verification class. A separate conditional-error budget controls false selection across adaptively chosen candidates. A quota-enforced XOR-synthesis family separates unbounded ratios of search success from changes in the accepted languages; exact and probabilistic audits check the resulting evidence requirements. The framework ties self-improvement claims to obligations on correctness, admissible evidence, verification resources, and selection error.

</details>


### [272] [The Harness as the Only Mutable Surface: Compliance-Bounded Self-Evolution of LLM Agents in Credit Pipelines, with a Measured Admission Gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10629)
*Ravil Akhtyamov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种可审计的自进化LLM代理框架，将自我改进限制在运行时 harness（指令文本、工具调用逻辑、原语组合）而保持模型权重固定，通过双循环引擎和准入门控机制实现可追溯的适应性变更，在信用评分管道中实验验证了其有效性和合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化LLM代理在自我重写时会破坏监督者审查所需的制品（命名变更、记录测试、审批），导致不可审计。需要一种机制将自我进化限制在运行时 harness 且模型权重固定，使每次适应都是带有原因和测试的 diff，满足高风险信用评分的监管要求（如欧盟AI法案）。

Method: 构建双循环引擎：内循环生成候选变更，外循环通过准入门控（写入哈希链记录）决定部署。使用模拟代理和种子搜索提议者而非语言模型进行仿真评估。在三种监督重新解释家族、三个严重程度、各10个种子下测试。对比有门控与无门控（无界系统）的表现。

Result: 有门控循环接受了7,449个候选变更中的144个，无一恶化留出历史误差，将假阳性率恢复到神谕水平且未提高低/中严重程度下的漏报。无门控时接受309有害变更，49/90次运行漏报>10%。门控在预偏移标签上拒绝所有候选，表明重新解释需编码为重标记历史的规则。参数/范围偏移局部修复，结构偏移需原语替换；最高结构严重度下门控固定容忍度在半数种子中阻挡正确替换。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了可审计的自进化，符合欧盟AI法案高风险信用评分条款，但美国2026年4月模型风险指南将代理式AI排除在外。门控机制有效防止有害变更，但固定容忍度可能阻碍必要的结构性修复，需进一步研究自适应容忍度。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents can adapt a credit pipeline to a changed rule, but an agent that rewrites itself destroys the artefact a supervisor reviews: a named change, a recorded test, an approval. We argue that self-evolution is reviewable only if it is confined to the runtime harness (instruction text, tool-call logic and primitive composition) while model weights stay fixed, so that every adaptation is a diff with a cause and a test attached. We give a dual-loop engine built on that bound, with one admission gate that writes a hash-chained record before deployment, and we measure the gate in simulation, with a simulated agent and a seeded-search proposer rather than language models. Across three families of supervisory re-interpretation at three severities, 10 seeds each, the gated loop admitted 144 of 7,449 candidate changes, none of which worsened error on held-out history, and restored the false-positive rate to the oracle level without raising missed flags in every low- and mid-severity cell. With the gate replaced by the check an unbounded system applies (fewer errors visible in recent traces), the same loops admitted 309 harmful changes and left missed flags above 10% in 49 of 90 runs: false positives fell because the screen was loosened. Evaluated on pre-shift labels, the gate rejected every candidate, so a re-interpretation must be encoded as a rule that relabels history. Parametric and scope shifts were repaired locally, a structural one only by primitive replacement; at the highest structural severity the gate's fixed tolerance blocked the correct replacement in half the seeds. We map the mechanisms to the EU AI Act's provisions for high-risk credit scoring and note that the April 2026 US model-risk guidance excludes agentic AI from its scope.

</details>


### [273] [Speaking the Navigator's Language: Trajectory-Grounded Instruction Translation for Frozen Aerial VLN Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10635)
*Xi Chen,Zhe Liu,Xiaogang Xu,Jiafei Xu,Chunyi Zhou,Yuan Su,Rui Zeng,Tianyu Du,Kelu Yao,Chao Li,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对空中视觉语言导航（VLN）中训练指令与用户真实指令的“指令鸿沟”问题，提出Trajectory-Grounded Instruction Translator（TGIT）作为前端翻译器，在冻结导航器的情况下，通过从轨迹结果学习将弱指令翻译为智能体可执行指令，显著提升弱指令和真实人类指令下的成功率，并实现零样本迁移和跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中VLN智能体通常在详细、轨迹对齐的指令上训练，但实际用户发出的是简短、意图驱动的指令，导致指令分布偏移（instruction gap），使冻结OpenFly导航器的成功率从31.03%骤降至11.33%。为了缩小这一鸿沟，需要一种无需重新训练导航器、能利用轨迹反馈将弱指令翻译为可执行指令的方法。

Method: 首先利用大语言模型配合人工编写的风格示例，将原始详细指令转换为成对的、以意图为中心的弱指令（Weak commands），弱指令单独测试得15.27% SR。然后提出TGIT作为前端翻译器，保持导航器参数冻结，通过从轨迹执行结果中学习（可能采用强化学习或模仿学习），将弱指令翻译为导航器可执行的指令。TGIT在弱指令数据上训练，无需人工标注的平行语料。

Result: 弱指令训练的TGIT将弱指令输入下的SR提升至37.93%，并零样本迁移到真实人类指令（11.33% → 32.51%）；在保留的OpenFly测试集上也提升（4.95% → 20.79%）；此外在CityNav和AirVLN数据集上也表现出恢复性能提升，展示了跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: TGIT通过冻结导航器并学习从轨迹结果翻译弱指令，有效弥合了训练指令与用户真实指令之间的鸿沟，无需重新训练导航器即可大幅提升真实指令下的导航性能，并展示了良好的零样本迁移和跨数据集泛化能力，为解决VLN中的指令分布偏移提供了实用的前端解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents are typically trained on detail-rich, trajectory-aligned commands, whereas users issue short, intent-driven instructions; on a frozen OpenFly navigator, this \emph{instruction gap} drops success rate (SR) from $31.03\%$ to $11.33\%$. To scale translator training, we prompt a language model with human-written style examples to convert original commands into paired, intent-centered Weak commands, which yield $15.27\%$ SR. We introduce the \textbf{Trajectory-Grounded Instruction Translator (TGIT)}, a front-end that keeps the navigator frozen and translates Weak inputs into agent-executable commands by learning from its trajectory outcomes. The resulting Weak-trained translator raises Weak-input SR to $37.93\%$ and transfers zero-shot to real human instructions ($11.33\%{\rightarrow}32.51\%$); it also improves held-out OpenFly ($4.95\%{\rightarrow}20.79\%$) and yields recovery on CityNav and AirVLN.

</details>


### [274] [Plan-and-Patch: Diffusion Language Models for Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10786)
*Syamantak Kumar,Jiang Guo,Hassan Hamad,Hideo Kobayashi,Yi Xiang,Yezhou Yang,Yanjun Qi,Daniele Bonadiman,Jiarong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Plan-and-Patch框架，利用扩散语言模型(dLLM)通过并行解掩码生成结构化程序式计划，并通过填补选定区域实现局部计划修复，保持前后缀不变。实验表明，在无任务特定训练下，扩散规划器(DreamReasoner-8B)的计划修复成功率(53.7%)接近自回归规划器(Qwen3-8B, 27.0%)的两倍；任务特定训练后，两者计划生成成功率相当，但扩散模型将平均生成延迟降低39-46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体执行需要协调子目标、工具使用及中间结果，但环境假设可能失效、工具返回意外结果或动作失败，智能体必须具备计划修复能力。传统方法需重新生成整个计划，易引入不必要变更；而修复仅影响局部区域，保留前后结构更高效。

Method: 提出Plan-and-Patch框架：(1) 使用扩散语言模型(dLLM)通过并行解掩码生成结构化、程序式计划；(2) 计划修复时，仅重新生成受影响区域，条件化于保留的前缀和后缀，实现局部填补。对比扩散规划器DreamReasoner-8B与自回归规划器Qwen3-8B，在Natural Plan、ALFWorld、TextCraft基准上评估。

Result: 无任务特定训练时，扩散规划器计划修复成功率53.7%，自回归仅27.0%；任务特定训练后，两者在ALFWorld和TextCraft上观测成功率相当，但扩散模型平均计划生成延迟降低39-46%。

Conclusion: Plan-and-Patch为长时程智能体提供了更快的计划生成与有效的计划修复框架，扩散语言模型在并行生成与局部修复方面展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Planning is increasingly important for long-horizon agents, where successful execution requires coordinating subgoals, tool use, and intermediate outcomes over many steps. Yet assumptions made during planning may be invalidated by the environment, tools may return unexpected results, or actions may fail. Effective agents must therefore not only generate plans, but also revise them. Such revisions often affect only part of a plan, leaving the preceding and subsequent structure intact. Rather than regenerate the entire plan and risk unnecessary changes, repair can regenerate the affected region conditioned on the preserved prefix and suffix. We introduce Plan-and-Patch, a plan-and-act framework in which a diffusion language model (dLLM) generates a structured, program-like plan through parallel unmasking and repairs it by filling in selected regions while keeping the surrounding steps fixed. We compare DreamReasoner-8B and Qwen3-8B as diffusion and autoregressive (AR) planners. On Natural Plan without task-specific training, diffusion (53.7%) achieves nearly twice the plan repair success rate of AR (27.0%). After task-specific training on agentic benchmarks, ALFWorld and TextCraft, the planners achieve similar observed success in plan generation, while diffusion reduces mean plan-generation latency by 39-46% relative to AR. Our results show that Plan-and-Patch provides a framework for faster plan generation and effective plan repair in long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [275] [Whose Ground Truth? Embracing Ambiguity in Human-Centered AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10805)
*Jingyao Wu,Mohammad Tariqul Islam,Per Rådberg Nagbøl,Julie Gerlings,Giovanni Leoni,Georgiana Fryza,Lars Pilegaard Thomsen,Jakob Mainz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张在人本AI开发中，从假设单一确定性真值转向建模合理的人类判断解释空间，区分有意义的歧义与标注噪声，以更好地反映人类感知、判断和体验的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习和AI系统假设存在单一确定的真值，将人类标注的变异性通过聚合或视为噪声处理。然而，许多人本任务中人类解释本质上具有歧义，同一输入可能有多种同时合理且有效的解释。将这种歧义简化为单一目标会忽略关于人类感知、判断和体验多样性的重要信息。

Method: 作为一篇立场论文，作者呼吁转向建模合理人类判断的解释空间，并区分有意义的歧义与标注噪声。认为这一视角应指导AI系统的表征、学习、评估、部署和治理等全生命周期。

Result: 提出了一个概念性框架，主张在人本AI中采用解释空间建模替代单一真值假设，为AI系统的设计、开发和治理提供了新的指导原则。

Conclusion: 采用建模解释空间的视角将支持更人本的AI发展，使AI系统能更好地反映人类解释的多样性，从而实现更符合人类价值观和需求的AI技术。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly interact with people and make decisions about them, understanding human interpretations becomes an important part of developing human-centered AI. Conventional machine learning and AI systems are largely developed under the assumption that a single definitive ground truth exists, with variability in human annotations often resolved through aggregation or treated as noise. However, for many human-centered tasks, human interpretation is inherently ambiguous, and multiple interpretations of the same input may be simultaneously reasonable and valid. Reducing such ambiguity to a single target risks overlooking meaningful information about the diversity of human perception, judgment, and experience. In this position paper, we call for a shift towards modeling the interpretation space of plausible human judgments, while distinguishing meaningful ambiguity from annotation noise. We argue that this perspective should guide how AI systems are represented, learned, evaluated, deployed, and governed, supporting more human-centered AI that better reflects the diversity of human interpretation.

</details>


### [276] [On the Clock: Towards Punctual and Productive Time-Budgeted AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10833)
*Aaron Wang,Neelabh Madan,Vlad Sobal,Matthew Trager,Michael Kleinman,Elman Mansimov,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究小型LLM代理能否在显式时间预算下有效运行，发现代理难以将时间预算转化为受控的时间使用，通过注入计时信息和RL训练可提升预算遵守，但无法有效利用额外时间提升任务性能，揭示时间遵守与生产性时间分配之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究小型LLM代理是否能在显式的墙钟时间预算约束下既遵守运行时间又有效利用可用时间，解决代理缺乏时间感知、无法预估动作时长及缺乏从可用时间到策略的映射等问题。

Method: 在MLE-Bench Lite上评估Qwen3.6-27B，在Zork I (Jericho)上评估Qwen3-4B；采用两类干预：基于工具链的机制（注入计时信息、强制截止时间）和基于预算感知奖励的强化学习（GRPO）。

Result: 工具链注入计时信息显著提升Qwen3.6-27B的预算遵守且不损失性能，强制钩子进一步收紧遵守；GRPO在Zork I上实现近乎完美的预算遵守并泛化到未见预算，但在MLE-Bench上未提升任务性能。代理虽学会何时停止，却倾向用重复动作填充多余时间，多预算GRPO训练倾向于崩塌为最短预算策略。

Conclusion: 时间预算遵守与生产性时间分配之间存在根本鸿沟，仅靠遵守预算不足以让代理有效利用额外时间提升性能，这仍是预算条件代理的核心挑战。

Abstract: We study whether small LLM agents can operate effectively under explicit wall-clock time budgets by both respecting the allocated runtime and using available time productively. We evaluate Qwen3.6-27B on five competitions from MLE-Bench Lite and Qwen3-4B on Zork I (Jericho), two agentic benchmarks where additional computational time can meaningfully improve performance. In the simplest setting, where the budget is stated only in the prompt, agents fail to translate the stated budget into controlled use of time. These failures arise from gaps in time awareness, since the harness provides no timing feedback, but also because they cannot reliably anticipate the duration of actions, and do not have a learned mapping from available time to an appropriate strategy. We investigate two complementary classes of interventions: harness-based mechanisms that expose timing information and enforce deadlines, and reinforcement learning with budget-aware rewards. Injecting timing information through the harness substantially improves budget adherence for Qwen3.6-27B without measurable loss in performance, while enforcement hooks tighten adherence further. RL with GRPO achieves near-perfect budget adherence on Zork I and generalizes to held-out budgets not seen during training, but does not improve task performance over the untrained harness on MLE-Bench. Once agents are made to respect the budget, they still fail to use additional time to improve task performance. RL-trained policies learn when to stop but often fill extra time with repeated actions, and GRPO training on multiple budgets tends to collapse toward the strategy learned for the shortest budget. Our results reveal a gap between time adherence and productive time allocation, which remains a central challenge for budget-conditioned agents.

</details>


### [277] [Self-Supervised Keyframe Discovery for Horizon-Invariant Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10857)
*Prabin Kumar Rath,Omkar Patil,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Keyframe Mnemonics，一种自监督方法，通过从随机采样的过去观测中学习目标并将其作为奖励来发现关键帧（助记符），从而在非马尔可夫环境中实现无限时域的上下文保留保证，并在合成任务和真实机器人操作任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非马尔可夫环境中，行为克隆策略需在长时域推理上下文信息。现有循环模型存在隐状态崩塌和梯度不稳定问题，注意力模型受限于上下文长度，无法有效处理长时域依赖。

Method: 提出Keyframe Mnemonics：自监督地从随机采样的过去观测中学习目标函数，将其作为奖励选择信息关键的关键帧（助记符），再训练条件于这些关键帧的BC策略。在任务结构假设下，提供无限时域的上下文保留保证，同时保持少量决策相关关键帧在工作记忆中。

Result: 在合成记忆领域，助记符条件BC策略达到100%成功率，并能泛化到比训练长几个数量级的时域而无性能下降。在内存密集型机器人操作基准上，平均绝对成功率比最强基线提升13.9%（23个任务），真实机器人上在20倍更长时域保持80%成功率。

Conclusion: Keyframe Mnemonics通过自监督发现关键帧，解决了长时域依赖建模的核心难题，实现了理论上的无限时域上下文保留，并在仿真和真实机器人任务中展示了卓越的泛化能力和鲁棒性，为非马尔可夫环境下的行为克隆提供了有效新范式。

Abstract: Behavior cloning (BC) in non-Markovian environments is a challenging problem because policies have to reason over contextual information over long horizons. Existing policy architectures rely on recurrent or attention-based mechanisms to capture long-term dependencies. However, recurrent models suffer from hidden-state collapse and gradient instability under backpropagation through time, while attention-based models are fundamentally limited by context length. To address these issues, we propose Keyframe Mnemonics, a novel self-supervised method that $\textit{discovers}$ a set of information-critical observations ($\textit{mnemonics}$) by learning an objective from randomly sampled past observations and using it as a reward for keyframe selection. We then train a BC policy that conditions on the discovered keyframes to model the action distribution. Under certain task-structure assumptions, our formulation provides context retention guarantees over an infinite horizon, while maintaining a small set of decision-relevant keyframes in the policy's working memory. We evaluate our method on synthetic memory domains, where mnemonic-conditioned BC policies achieve $100$% success rates (SR) and generalize to horizons orders of magnitude beyond training without performance degradation. Additionally, we evaluate on memory-intensive robot manipulation benchmark, achieving a $13.9$% average absolute SR improvement over the strongest baseline across $23$ tasks and retaining $80$% SR at $20\times$ longer horizons on a real robot. Code and videos are available at https://keyframe-mnemonics.github.io.

</details>


### [278] [RIT-RAG: Navigating Document Corpora with Retrieval-Induced Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11370)
*Meghanadh Pulivarthi,Swaraj Kumar Biswal,Kushagra Bhushan,Yatin Nandwani,Sachindra Joshi,Dinesh Raghu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RIT-RAG结合内容检索与结构化导航，通过离线构建文档树、查询时诱导子树并由LLM代理导航，解决了现有RAG方法在大规模语料库中难以利用文档结构的问题，在多个基准上显著提升回答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法存在局限：Agentic RAG仅提供孤立文本块，缺乏文档结构导致难以区分相关证据；结构感知方法（如PageIndex）无法扩展到大规模语料库，且依赖单一文档检索器，无法从错误选择中恢复。因此需要一种能在大规模语料库中结合内容检索与结构化导航的方法。

Method: RIT-RAG包含离线和在线两阶段。离线阶段，利用目录或站点地图为每个文档构建树结构。在线查询阶段，首先检索广泛的文本块，根据其位置诱导可管理的子树（可跨多文档），然后由LLM代理导航子树，选择性读取有前景的节点，并在需要时重新构思查询。检索负责提议“去哪里看”，代理决定“读什么”。

Result: 在金融、科学、客服三个基准测试中，RIT-RAG在vanilla、graph-based、agentic基线中达到最高回答准确率。在新基准EntQABench（284万技术文档网页）上，相比最强基线，准确率提升6.8至11.4个百分点（跨三种LLM）。

Conclusion: RIT-RAG通过检索诱导的树结构导航，有效结合了内容检索与文档结构感知，解决了大规模语料库中结构利用难的问题，显著提升了RAG系统的回答准确性，且具有跨文档、可恢复的特性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds language models in external corpora. Agentic RAG enables iterative search, yet exposes the model to isolated chunks without document structure, making it difficult to distinguish relevant evidence from chunks that merely resemble the query. Structure-aware methods such as PageIndex navigate document structure but cannot scale to the structures of large corpora, which do not fit in the LLM context. Hence, they first commit to a single document using a document retriever and cannot recover from a wrong choice. We propose RIT-RAG (Retrieval-Induced Tree RAG), which combines content retrieval with structural navigation. Offline, RIT-RAG builds a tree for each document from its table of contents or sitemap. At query time, it retrieves a broad set of chunks and uses their positions to induce manageable sub-trees, potentially across multiple documents. An LLM agent navigates these sub-trees, selectively reads promising nodes, and reformulates queries when needed. Thus, retrieval proposes where to look, while the agent decides what to read. Across financial, scientific, and customer-support benchmarks, RIT-RAG achieves the highest answer accuracy among vanilla, graph-based, and agentic baselines. On EntQABench, our new benchmark of 2.84 million technical-documentation webpages, it improves accuracy by 6.8 to 11.4 points over the strongest baseline across three LLMs.

</details>


### [279] [Reading the Room: Foundations, Design, and Challenges of Normative Competence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10906)
*Andrea Wynn,Harsh Satija,Seokhyun,Baek,Anqi Liu,Eric Nalisnick,Gillian K. Hadfield*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了大语言模型的规范能力——即仅通过交互推断社区强制执行的规范，而不依赖静态预训练知识。实验表明，基线LLM代理无法学习规范，且表现出无选择归因失效：盲目模仿特异性噪声与强制规则，即使受到惩罚也持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类社区由规范系统治理，规范数量庞大、变化迅速且常具任意性（如着装或语言惯例）。随着AI系统自主性增强，使其与这些规范对齐成为核心挑战。现有方法依赖静态预训练知识，无法应对动态、情境化的规范学习需求。

Method: 构建多智能体社区辩论环境，由合成规范控制发言权，隔离预训练暴露以单独研究规范能力。引入规范模块——用于规范推断的架构组件，系统测试不同规范风格和驱动模型对规范遵循的影响，并引入特异性非规范行为以考察无选择归因失效。

Result: 基线LLM代理即使学习规范能提升准确性也无法学习；规范遵循高度敏感于规范风格和驱动模型，泛化能力缺乏；当特异性噪声伴随真实规范时，代理表现出无选择归因失效——无差别复制噪声与规则，即便显式惩罚不必要模仿也持续存在。

Conclusion: 本文首创性地操作化并评估了LLM的规范能力，揭示当前AI系统擅长行为模仿，但缺乏辨别社会强制秩序的能力。规范能力的缺失限制了AI在动态人类社区中的可靠对齐与部署。

Abstract: Human communities are governed by normative systems: shared standards that produce \textit{norms} dictating acceptable behavior, enforced through community sanctioning. Aligning increasingly autonomous AI systems with these norms is a central alignment challenge, complicated by the fact that norms are vast in number, change quickly, and are often arbitrary (e.g., dress or language conventions). Thus, alignment requires \textit{normative competence}: the ability to discern from interaction alone what norms a community enforces without relying on static pretrained knowledge. We introduce a multi-agent community debate setting, where access to debate is governed by synthetic norms, to study normative competence in isolation from pretraining exposure. We show that baseline LLM agents fail to learn norms even when doing so would improve their accuracy. We then experiment with various \textit{normative modules} -- architectural components for norm inference -- finding that norm-following is highly sensitive to both the style of norm and the model powering the normative module, suggesting a lack of generalizability. Furthermore, when idiosyncratic, non-normative behaviors accompany the true norm, LLM agents exhibit an unselective attribution failure: they indiscriminately copy idiosyncratic noise alongside enforced rules, a pattern that persists even when imitating unnecessary behaviors is explicitly penalized. To the best of our knowledge, our work is the first to operationalize and evaluate normative competence in LLMs, demonstrating that current AI systems excel at behavioral mimicry but lack the capacity to discern socially enforced order.

</details>


### [280] [StoreBench: A Live-Commerce Environment for Evaluating and Training Autonomous Operator Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10942)
*Daksh Raghuvanshi,Ved Vedere,Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出StoreBench，一个实时电商环境基准，用于评估大语言模型在长期规划和经济决策中的代理能力。实验显示当前最强模型（DeepSeek-V4-Pro）仅通过49%任务种子组合，远低于启发式策略（97%）；人类专家表现优于所有模型。GRPO后训练能显著提升模型在未见任务上的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理基准多为静态环境：世界仅在代理行动时变化、奖励为终端判决、通过阈值任意设定。缺乏能测试长时程规划、不确定性下经济判断、且具备生产级后端与确定性重放的动态基准。

Method: 构建StoreBench：基于生产级电商后端的中型在线服装店环境。客户全天候下单、供应商动态调价与失效、市场冲击部分或无预警。代理通过29个商家工具操作，采用窗口化操作预算使模拟时间仅取决于动作序列（消除模型延迟影响）。通过脚本锚定策略校准通过阈值，奖励函数针对已知奖励作弊进行加固，且给定动作序列每轮次完全可复现。评估7个前沿LLM于11个场景（30-45天）及完整模拟年，跨3个世界种子、匹配推理预算。

Result: 1) 无模型平均达到脚本智能分诊策略：最佳DeepSeek-V4-Pro通过率49% vs 启发式97%。2) 人类专家平均综合得分0.708，高于最佳模型0.700。3) 在Claude Code工具链下完整模拟年，多数模型表现大幅提升。4) Qwen3.5-27B经GRPO在5个不相交任务后训练，保留任务平均综合得分从0.136升至0.373。

Conclusion: StoreBench揭示当前LLM代理在真实动态商业环境中仍显著落后于启发式策略与人类专家。环境的动态性、长时程与经济不确定性构成核心挑战。后训练（GRPO）能有效提升泛化能力。作者开放部分训练任务、样本轨迹与评分工具，但保留完整环境以防基准污染。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning environments are now a primary lever for improving large language model (LLM) capabilities in post-training, yet most agentic benchmarks remain static: the world moves only when the agent acts, the reward is a terminal verdict, and the pass bar is set arbitrarily. We introduce StoreBench, a live-commerce environment in which an agent runs a mid-size online apparel store on a production-grade commerce backend, testing long-horizon planning and economic judgment under uncertainty. Customers order around the clock, suppliers reprice and fail, and market shocks arrive with partial or no warning.
