Agents interpret rules, choose opponents, analyze replays, and revise game agents to achieve their highest ranking within fixed match and evaluation budgets. We evaluate \val{completedmodels} model and harness configurations: Opus5.5 with Claude Code earns 6 gold medals, while no evaluated configuration tops the remaining 6 human ladders. Performance is generally weaker in games with more complex rule specifications. Further experiments show that opponent selection and dense feedback support policy improvement, and that agents learn from both on-policy replays of their own matches and off-policy replays of other players' matches. These results highlight HL's potential in adversarial games and identify persistent challenges in game understanding, strategy implementation, and long-horizon policy development.

</details>


### [382] [Overcoming Prior Barriers: Supervised Fine-Tuning under Long-Tail Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12345)
*Haohui Wang,Jiahao Xu,Wangzhi Zhan,Tong Zeng,Dongqi Fu,Hong Li,Swastik Roy,Naren Ramakrishnan,Chris North,Jian Kang,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出先验障碍概念量化预训练模型对目标概念的支持程度，揭示其长尾分布导致头尾概念在SFT时面临不同起点；推导基于先验障碍的预测风险界，设计自适应指令选择方法PASS，通过参考派生概念估计区分性证据并动态分配预算，在四种骨干-预算设置上持续超越七个SOTA基线，消融实验证实自适应分配优于均匀分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练LLM对不同概念的支持程度差异显著：频繁概念表征良好，稀有概念表征薄弱。SFT时头部概念面临低先验障碍，尾部概念需克服高先验障碍，现有指令选择方法忽略这种先验障碍差异，导致有限预算下监督分配不均，无法有效覆盖尾部概念。

Method: 提出PASS方法：(1) 构建参考派生概念作为目标概念的竞争者；(2) 估计每条指令提供的区分性证据量；(3) 基于当前选择下的覆盖不足程度，自适应分配选择预算给先验障碍高且证据不足的概念，联合优化指令效用与监督需求。

Result: 在四种骨干模型-预算设置下，PASS一致优于七个最先进指令选择方法；消融实验表明其自适应预算分配策略显著优于均匀分配基线，验证了先验障碍感知选择的有效性。

Conclusion: 先验障碍是决定SFT预测性能的关键因素，长尾分布导致头尾概念需差异化监督。PASS通过先验障碍感知的自适应指令选择，在有限预算下有效平衡证据获取与覆盖需求，显著提升SFT效率，为数据高效微调提供了理论指导与实用方法。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) adapts pretrained large language models (LLMs) to downstream tasks, but the required concepts can receive substantially different levels of pretrained support. Frequent concepts are more likely to be well learned, whereas rare concepts may remain weakly represented. We introduce a novel notion named prior barrier to quantify how strongly the pretrained model supports competing concepts over the target concept. We observe that prior barriers follow a long-tail distribution, placing head and tail concepts at different starting points for SFT: head concepts face lower prior barriers, whereas tail concepts require additional instructions to overcome their higher prior barriers. Our theoretical analysis further derives a predictive risk bound for SFT under long-tail prior barriers, explicitly characterizing how the prior barrier and accumulated SFT evidence jointly determine predictive performance. Motivated by this prior barrier-dependent demand, we propose PASS, an adaptive SFT instruction selection method that constructs reference-derived concepts and estimates the distinguishing evidence provided by each instruction, and adaptively allocates the selection budget toward concepts that remain insufficiently covered under the current selection. In this way, PASS jointly considers which instructions can provide useful evidence and where additional supervision is needed under a limited budget. Experiments show that our method consistently outperforms seven state-of-the-art instruction selection methods on four backbone-budget settings. An ablation study further shows that PASS's adaptive allocation consistently improves over uniform allocation.

</details>


### [383] [Accurate but Not Humble: Evaluating Epistemic Humility in LLM Agents under Knowledge Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12360)
*Kaiser Sun,Bernal Jimenez Gutierrez,Hongjun Liu,Jingyu Zhang,Jie Gao,Mark Dredze,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出评估智能体“认知谦逊”（EH）的框架，通过知识冲突场景发现任务准确率高不代表EH高，智能体常早期检测冲突但后期无法维持，模型干预能提升EH但常牺牲准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估主要关注任务成功率，忽视智能体在证据与先验信念冲突时如何处理不确定性，缺乏对认知谦逊的系统性度量。

Method: 将EH操作化为三个轨迹级行为维度：识别、解决、升级（ISE）。构建两类知识冲突设置：受控冲突和多步执行中自然发生的冲突，配合无冲突对照组，评估四种智能体。

Result: 任务准确率与EH不正相关；高准确率智能体虽能识别冲突但最终答案未传达未解决的不确定性；轨迹分析显示智能体常在早期步骤检测冲突但后期无法维持或解决；模型层面干预可提升EH但通常以任务准确率下降为代价。

Conclusion: 认知谦逊源于骨干模型、智能体框架和评估环境的交互，单纯提升任务准确率不足以保证EH，需在智能体设计中显式建模不确定性处理机制。

Abstract: When retrieved evidence contradicts an agent's prior beliefs, does it revise its answer, acknowledge uncertainty, or persist with an incorrect conclusion? Existing evaluations of agentic systems focus primarily on task success, offering limited insight into how agents handle such conflicts. We propose to evaluate agents on epistemic humility (EH): the agent's willingness to recognize, act on, and communicate uncertainty during task execution. We operationalize EH through three trajectory-level behavioral dimensions: Identify, Solve, and Escalate (ISE). Through knowledge conflict, situations where the backbone language model's parametric knowledge contradicts the evidence it encounters, or where two contextual sources disagree, we evaluate two conflict settings: (1) controlled conflict and (2) naturally occurring conflict during multi-step agentic execution, each paired with matched no-conflict controls. Evaluating four agents, we find that higher task accuracy does not necessarily correspond to greater epistemic humility: some high-accuracy configurations recognize conflicts during execution but do not communicate unresolved uncertainty in their incorrect final answers. Trajectory-level analysis further reveals that agents frequently detect conflicts in early steps of execution but fail to maintain or resolve them in later steps. Finally, we show that model-level interventions can improve EH, but often at the cost of task accuracy, suggesting that epistemic humility emerges from the interaction among the backbone model, agent harness, and evaluation environment.

</details>


### [384] [Cited but Not Consulted: A Counterfactual Audit of Legal Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12361)
*Saisab Sadhu,Shreeyans Arora,Pratinav Seth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型在法律判决中引用法条/判例是否真实反映判决依赖性。实验发现模型虽能高准确率引用正确法律依据（66.7%-100%），但替换法律依据后判决变化率极低（0-76.7%），且模型更易受对抗指令操控（73.3%-96.4%合规率）。规模扩大和法律专用模型均无法弥合这一差距，揭示生成式法律解释不可作为合规/审计依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型常通过引用法条或判例来为法律判决提供解释，业界普遍将此视为判决合法性的证据。但缺乏系统性实证研究验证：模型命名的法律权威是否真正决定了判决结果？即引用行为是否仅为事后合理化而非真实推理依据。

Method: 采用隐藏状态解码法：固定案件事实，将模型生成的法律权威替换为无关权威，从隐藏状态追踪判决演变。跨7个开源模型（8B-70B）和4个基准（CaseHOLD、ECHR、SCOTUS、ContractNLI）测试，对比引用准确率与判决敏感度；另构建红队评估对抗指令注入鲁棒性。

Result: 模型引用正确权威比例66.7%-100%，但权威替换导致判决翻转比例仅：CaseHOLD 0%-21.7%，ECHR/SCOTUS 30%-76.7%，ContractNLI 43.3%-50%。模型规模与法律LoRA微调均未显著提升敏感度。对抗指令合规率（73.3%-96.4%）全模型均显著高于判决翻转率。结果经提示词噪声和采样混淆控制验证可复现。

Conclusion: 法律权威引用是判决依赖性的失效代理指标：模型擅长事后合理化而非基于权威推理。生成式法律解释不应用于合规审计，因判决同时易受对抗操控，双重风险需引起法律AI部署重视。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly justify legal decisions by naming the statute or precedent behind a verdict, treated as evidence that the decision follows from it. We test this directly: holding case facts fixed, we substitute the named legal authority for an unrelated one and decode a model's evolving verdict from its hidden states. Across seven open-weight models (8B-70B) and four benchmarks spanning judicial and contractual reasoning, when explicitly required to justify a verdict by naming the governing authority, models name the correct one in 66.7%-100% of generations, while the verdict changing when the authority changes is far less consistent: 0.0%-21.7% on CaseHOLD, 30.0%-76.7% on ECHR and SCOTUS, and 43.3%-50.0% on ContractNLI. Neither scale nor a purpose-built legal-reasoning model (a best-effort LoRA reproduction; Section 6) closes this gap. A red-teaming evaluation on five core models finds compliance with an adversarial instruction hidden in the case facts (73.3%-96.4%) exceeds verdict-swap sensitivity by a wide margin, holding without exception across model rankings. Naming a legal authority is thus a poor proxy for a verdict's dependence on it, while the same verdict remains separately vulnerable to adversarial manipulation. Both findings replicate across checks ruling out prompt-wording noise and confounded sampling, and bear directly on the use of generated legal explanations as compliance or audit artefacts.

</details>


### [385] [HANS: A Handwritten Answer Sheet Dataset for Noisy Hybrid Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12363)
*Xiazhen Wu,Wansong Qin,Yangbin Zheng,Liangda Fang,Zhan Li,Xiujie Huang,Liushen Zhou,Quanlong Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了首个面向真实教育场景的手写答题纸数据集HANS，并基于此提出了端到端框架NA-GOT，通过两阶段噪声抑制提升识别准确性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析和手写识别基准主要针对结构良好的印刷文档或孤立数学表达式，缺乏捕捉学生答题纸复杂特征的数据集，如多行推导过程、文本与数学公式混合、划掉等噪声伪影。

Method: 引入HANS数据集，包含数学表达式、自然语言文本、手绘表格及多样噪声模式，附带细粒度标注。提出NA-GOT框架：轻量级特征级噪声抑制模块与解码阶段的噪声感知注意力机制相结合，实现两阶段噪声抑制。

Result: 实验表明HANS对现有方法构成重大挑战，NA-GOT在答题过程识别的准确性和稳定性上均显著提升。

Conclusion: HANS填补了真实教育场景手写识别数据集的空白，NA-GOT有效解决了噪声干扰问题，为智能评分和自动化评分提供了更鲁棒的基础。

Abstract: Intelligent grading and automated scoring technologies constitute critical infrastructure for smart education. However, existing document parsing and handwriting recognition benchmarks are predominantly designed for well-structured printed documents or isolated mathematical expressions, lacking datasets that capture the complex characteristics inherent to student answer sheets, including multi-line derivation processes, heterogeneous mixtures of text and mathematical formulae, and noise artifacts such as strikethroughs. To address this gap, we introduce HANS, the first dataset explicitly constructed for real-world educational scenarios, encompassing mathematical expressions, natural language text, hand-drawn tables, and diverse noise patterns including corrections and deletions, accompanied by fine-grained annotations that establish a reliable foundation for robust recognition research. Building upon HANS, we propose NA-GOT, an end-to-end framework that achieves two-stage noise suppression through a lightweight noise suppression module operating at the feature level, complemented by a noiseaware attention mechanism incorporated into the decoding stage. Experimental results demonstrate that HANS poses substantial challenges to existing methods, while NA-GOT achieves significant improvements in both accuracy and stability for answer process recognition. The dataset will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [386] [OnTrack: Real-Time Monitoring and Intervention in LLM Agent Trajectories via Streaming Structure-Aware Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12375)
*Babak Barazandeh,Connor Swanson,Chinmay Kulkarni,Nikhil Mungel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 OnTrack 流式监控机制，通过对比智能体步骤与成功运行记录，实现毫秒级异常检测与阻断，在 SWE-bench 上显著优于基线，能节省 18% 失败运行算力且准确率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在自主运行时缺乏有效保障，现有方案要么增加每步延迟（保护代理），要么事后分析（日志评估），无法实时防止不可逆操作造成的成本与安全损失。

Method: OnTrack 流式监控机制，对比智能体步骤及依赖与历史成功运行，支持三种数据访问级别（完整参考、仅工具模式、无先验知识），实现计划违规检测、循环/停滞/重复工具调用识别，延迟约 1ms/步。

Result: 在 SWE-bench 轨迹上，基于前 8 步对失败轨迹排序优于内容相似度方法（AUROC +0.057）；采用中止策略可节省约 18% 失败运行算力，中止运行中 83% 确为失败（5/6 中止正确）。

Conclusion: OnTrack 提供了轻量级、实时的智能体监控方案，能在极低延迟下有效预测并阻断失败运行，显著降低算力浪费与安全风险，适应不同数据访问场景。

Abstract: Agents are deployed in applications from trip planners and stock trading to IT incident triage. In most cases, LLM agents work autonomously with minimal rule-based safeguarding, leading to cost and safety issues from irreversible actions. Recent works resolve this either by using a safeguard agent to monitor behavior or evaluating logs post-hoc. The first adds cost and latency to every step; the second delivers its verdict after the run, when tokens are burned and damage is done. To overcome this, we propose OnTrack, a streaming monitoring mechanism that compares an agent's steps and dependencies against recorded successful runs to alert users or block the agent in about a millisecond per step. We study this problem in three regimes of decreasing access: full reference access (historical runs and tool schemas), intermediate access (only tool schemas), and no prior knowledge (only step logs as generated). Expectation of OnTrack's monitoring capabilities reduces as data access drops, ranging from plan violation detection to identifying loops, stalls, and repeated tool calls. Finally, we evaluate OnTrack using SWE-bench trajectories. Based on the first 8 steps, our method ranks failing trajectories below succeeding ones better than content similarity approaches (+0.057 AUROC). With an abort policy, we save about 18% of compute that would be burned on failing runs, where 83% of interrupted runs were actually heading to failure (5 out of 6 aborts were correct).

</details>


### [387] [GeoReform: Reflective Formalization Evolution for Multimodal Geometry Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12391)
*Jialu Wang,Ruichen Zhang,Xiaoou Liu,Hua Wei,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoReform框架，将几何形式化视为可优化策略，通过反思进化提升多模态大模型在几何推理中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将几何实体、关系、约束转为文本表示，但形式化过程非平凡：冗余关系干扰模型，歧义引用导致错误约束，结构注入虽修复部分错误但引入新错误。核心挑战在于如何组织几何事实以支持下游推理。

Method: GeoReform是一个反思形式化进化框架，将形式化视为可优化策略而非固定解析器输出。它执行完整推理流水线，收集失败回合，诊断当前表示缺陷，并变异策略以更好地选择、定位、分组和呈现几何实体、关系、约束及目标。

Result: 在Geometry3K基准上，GeoReform将Qwen3VL-2B准确率从42.0%提升至56.0%。跨几何推理基准的大量实验表明有效形式化对提升多模态几何推理至关重要。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将形式化视为可优化策略并结合反思进化机制，能显著提升MLLMs在几何图表推理任务中的性能，有效形式化是多模态几何推理的关键。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often struggle to identify and use geometric relations in diagrams. Recent methods address this challenge by converting geometric entities, relations, and constraints into explicit textual representations for the model to reason over. However, effective formalization is highly non-trivial: on Geometry3K, structure injection fixes 28 errors but introduces 13 new ones among 200 examples. Redundant relations can distract the model, while ambiguous references to diagram elements can lead it to apply constraints incorrectly. This suggests that the key challenge is not merely extracting more geometric facts, but organizing them into representations that support downstream reasoning. To fully exploit the power of formalization, we further propose GeoReform, a reflective formalization evolution framework that treats formalization as an optimizable policy rather than a fixed parser output. GeoReform executes the full reasoning pipeline, collects failed rollouts, diagnoses defects in the current representation, and mutates the policy to better select, ground, group, and present geometric entities, relations, constraints, and targets. On Geometry3K, GeoReform improves Qwen3VL-2B accuracy from 42.0\% to 56.0\%. Extensive experiments and analyses across geometry reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that effective formalization is crucial for improving multimodal geometry reasoning.

</details>


### [388] [HRIL: Learning Multimodal Synergy via Higher-Order Tensor Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12393)
*Qun Dai,Liangjian Wen,Jiang Duan,Yong Dai,Dongkai Wang,Maolin Wang,Mingjie Wang,Jianzhuang Liu,He Yan,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HRIL方法，通过高阶矩张量与Tucker分解显式建模多模态协同信息，并引入协同感知正则化防止能量集中，在受控协同任务与真实基准上超越现有对比学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督多模态表征学习难以捕捉协同信息——即仅从多模态联合配置中涌现、无法从单一模态恢复的任务相关信号。协同信息体现为模态间的高阶统计依赖，需显式建模多路交互以保留其信息容量。

Method: HRIL构建模态嵌入的经验交叉矩张量表示多路交互，采用Tucker分解得到核心张量，并设计协同感知正则化项防止能量集中于低阶分量，从而保留高阶耦合能力以捕获协同信息。

Result: 在受控协同任务与多个真实多模态基准上，HRIL持续优于现有多模态对比学习方法，在协同交互主导的任务上增益显著。

Conclusion: 显式建模高阶统计依赖并配合协同感知正则化，能有效保留多模态表征中的协同信息容量，为多模态自监督学习提供了新的原则性范式。

Abstract: Self-supervised multimodal representation learning has achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, yet capturing synergistic information remains challenging due to the complexity of cross-modal interactions. Unlike the shared information across individual modalities, synergy arises when task-relevant signals emerge only from the joint configuration of multiple modalities and cannot be recovered from any modality in isolation. This work focuses on how to preserve the information capacity for such synergistic signals in multimodal representations. The key observation is that synergistic information is reflected in higher-order statistical dependence among modalities, which provides a principled target for explicitly modeling joint interactions. Motivated by this insight, we propose Higher-order Representation and Information Learning (HRIL), which constructs an empirical cross-moment tensor over modality embeddings to represent multi-way interactions. HRIL employs Tucker decomposition to obtain a core tensor, complemented by a synergy-aware regularizer that prevents energy concentration and preserves higher-order coupling capacity for synergistic information capture. Experiments on the controlled synergy task and real-world benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing multimodal contrastive methods, with notable gains on tasks dominated by synergistic interactions. Code is released at https://github.com/brightest66/HRIL.

</details>


### [389] [Searching for "Harmful Refusal": A Psychometric Audit of an AI Safety Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12409)
*Christopher M. Stewart,Preston Botter,Natalie Sarabosing,Muye Zhang,Rachel Phinnemore,Shalini Ghosh,Hong Shen,Hoda Heidari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了HELM Safety基准中有害拒答属性的测量有效性，发现HarmBench数据集并非测量单一属性，且整体安全评分掩盖了饱和度与行为混淆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全基准通常报告单一总分，但不同模型在相似总分下可能具有截然不同的属性画像。研究旨在验证有害拒答是否为可单独测量的单一构念，并揭示聚合评分的潜在缺陷。

Method: 采用构念效度框架，先筛选HELM Safety中四个可能针对有害拒答的数据集，发现三个已饱和。对剩余的HarmBench施行两项心理测量检验：多维项目反应理论（MIRT）建模评估单一属性假设，差异项目功能（DIF）分析检测不同开发者模型在同等拒答能力下的评分差异。

Result: MIRT强烈表明HarmBench并非测量单一属性；DIF分析发现存在开发者相关的评分差异，但在范围特定匹配下这些标记基本消失，模式符合聚合效应但无法完全排除领域特定的开发者差异。整体HELM安全评分进一步将HarmBench与其他数据集分数合并为单一数值，掩盖了饱和与行为混淆。

Conclusion: 任何跨数据集和项目的平均安全评分都可能隐藏饱和度并混淆不同行为。主张在用单一评分比较模型前，该评分必须先通过单一属性读取的有效性验证。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks typically report one overall score for a suite of datasets, each of which may target one or more safety-related attributes, so models with similar overall scores can have very different attribute profiles. Comparing models is more tractable at the level of individual attributes, yet it is often unclear whether even a single dataset's scores isolate any single attribute. One plausible candidate for such an attribute is harmful refusal, a model's tendency to refuse dangerous or policy-violating prompts. We examine whether it constitutes a single, measurable attribute in HELM Safety. Using a construct validity framework that stipulates that an attribute must exist before a test can measure it, we start with HELM Safety's four datasets that might plausibly target harmful refusal, but find that three are saturated. We subject the remaining dataset, HarmBench, to two psychometric tests to determine if a single attribute like harmful refusal could stand behind its score. First, multidimensional item response theory modeling strongly suggests that HarmBench does not measure a singular attribute. Second, a differential item functioning analysis finds items where models from different developers with the same refusal ability score differently. These flags largely disappear under scope-specific matching, a pattern consistent with aggregation effects but not sufficient to rule out domain-specific developer differences. Zooming out, HarmBench collapses distinct harm behaviors into one score, and the overall HELM safety aggregate further collapses HarmBench and scores from other datasets into a single top-line number. Any safety score that averages over datasets and items can hide saturation and conflate behaviors this way. We argue that a score should earn its single-attribute reading before models are compared with it.

</details>


### [390] [Ecology of AI Agents: Collaboration Creates a Population Threshold for Takeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12436)
*Erin Crawley,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: AI agents can now conduct real-world cyberattacks, scale up capabilities with the number of agents, and collectively pursue misaligned goals to obtain rewards. Together, these factors raise the risk of a population explosion of misaligned agents: agents could compromise computers and secretly deploy additional agents, creating a self-reinforcing cycle where larger populations develop greater collective cyber capability and expand further. This raises a fundamental question: What determines whether a population of misaligned agents remains contained or takes off into this self-reinforcing cycle? This population-level problem is ecological safety: unlike individual-agent or multi-agent safety with a fixed population, it concerns the dynamics of the population itself. Here, we develop an ecological theory of AI-agent populations based on a population growth equation in which fitness (growth rate) depends on cybersecurity capability. We show that, without collaboration, the population takes off only when individual-agent capability exceeds a critical threshold. With collaboration, however, collective cybersecurity capability increases with population size. This creates a critical population threshold: below it, the population declines; above it, the population takes off, even though individual-agent capability has not changed. In ecology, this phenomenon is known as the strong Allee effect. Because red teaming a small group of agents cannot guarantee ecological safety in larger populations, our theory calls for ecological red teaming and population pacing: gradually deploying larger agent populations in controlled environments, while measuring how cyber capability scales with population size, and estimating the critical population size for takeoff. Capability gains may lower this threshold, requiring re-estimation for each new model generation.

</details>


### [391] [BrickBench: Evaluating Agentic Brick Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12452)
*Peter Kulits,Yiqing Xu,R. Kenny Jones,Cordelia Schmid,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrickBench 是一个用于评估智能体基于文本条件设计乐高套装的基准测试，要求智能体从离散零件库中选择零件，同时推理局部和全局约束，生成既符合语义设计要求又能实际物理搭建的组装方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估缺乏对智能体在离散零件选择、物理可搭建性约束下的联合推理能力的考察，需要一个能同时验证语义对齐、设计质量和物理有效性的基准。

Method: 构建 BrickBench 基准，包含三种不同规模和零件可用性的设置；开发 BrickAgent 环境，支持编码智能体构建、检查和验证设计；从有效性、对齐度、设计质量三个维度评分。

Result: 主流智能体大多能满足可验证的物理和语义要求，但在设计质量上仍显著落后于人类设计。

Conclusion: 发布了 BrickBench 基准和 BrickAgent 环境（http://www.brickben.ch），为具身智能体在离散物理构建任务上的研究提供了标准化评测平台。

Abstract: We propose BrickBench, a benchmark for agentic text-conditioned LEGO-set design. Given a prompt, an agent is tasked with producing an assembly that not only satisfies semantic and design criteria, but that can also be physically built. To do so, it must select parts from a discrete library and reason jointly about local and global constraints. We score validity, alignment, and design across three settings that vary in scale and part availability. We provide BrickAgent, an environment for coding agents to construct, inspect, and validate their designs. We find that leading agents largely satisfy verifiable physical and semantic requirements, but fall short of human designs. We release our benchmark and environment at http://www.brickben.ch

</details>


### [392] [On the estimation and validity of AI time horizons---a statistical look at the METR plot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12466)
*Drew T. Nguyen,William Fithian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: METR的50%时间跨度指标通过样条和项目反应理论重新计算，放宽了线性假设，发现2-30分钟区间内AI难度近乎平坦，时间跨度跳跃的难度非线性，提供了更好的点估计和诊断图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间跨度指标假设AI任务难度与人类完成时间对数呈线性关系，但这一假设可能不成立，需要更灵活的建模方法来准确量化AI能力。

Method: 使用样条函数和项目反应理论（IRT）对228个软件任务和26个AI模型的数据进行建模，将人类完成时间转换为AI难度，通过交叉验证下的适当评分规则评估点估计质量，并生成诊断图评估构念效度。

Result: 样条函数在2-30分钟区间近乎平坦，其他区间接近线性；从3分钟到30分钟的跨度跳跃比30分钟到5小时容易得多，尽管倍数相同（10×）；新点估计在交叉验证评分规则下表现更优。

Conclusion: 时间跨度指标应结合诊断图共同解读，特别是在提出新基准或扩展现有基准包含更长任务时，需注意非线性特性对能力量化的影响。

Abstract: METR's 50\% time horizon measures the human completion time of software tasks that an AI solves with 50\% probability, allowing AI capabilities to be expressed in interpretable units. On 228 tasks and 26 AIs, we recompute the time horizons using splines and item-response theory to relax the assumption that the AI difficulty of a task depends linearly on the log of human time. Our fitted spline can be interpreted as a function that \emph{converts} human time to AI difficulty; it is nearly flat in a region from 2--30 min but close to linear elsewhere. Hence, a time-horizon jump from 3 min to 30 min is much easier than one from 30 min to 5 hours despite the same multiplier of $10 \times$. Overall, we contribute time-horizon point estimates that perform better under a cross-validated suite of proper scoring rules, as well as diagnostic plots for assessing time horizons' construct validity. We suggest that time horizons be interpreted together with the diagnostic plots, especially as new time-horizon-based benchmarks are proposed or existing ones grow to include longer tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [393] [LIFT: A Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer for Unified Retrieval and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10556)
*Keji Miao,Enhao Cheng,Yan Li,Qun Li,Qi Zhang,Jie Yuan,Xiaoyong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 LIFT（生命周期感知交互因子化 Transformer），将用户交互分解为有序的请求、物品、上下文和动作四个状态，并建模为因果序列。检索阶段读取请求状态，排序阶段读取上下文状态，使两阶段共享历史建模同时保留阶段特有信息。在 ML-20M 和淘宝数据集上，LIFT 达到最高联合得分，分别比最强基线提升 4.9% 和 3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联推荐系统中检索和排序两阶段使用相同用户历史，但两阶段在交互不同节点获取的信息不同。现有方法未能有效建模这种生命周期感知的信息不对称，导致联合优化受限。

Method: 提出 LIFT 模型：(1) 将每次交互分解为有序的 Request、Item、Context、Action 四个状态，构建因果序列；(2) 检索任务读取 Request 状态，排序任务读取 Context 状态，实现历史建模共享与阶段特定信息保留；(3) 采用 Role-Conditioned Attention（角色条件注意力）和轻量级 Pre-LN Bias 实例化该表示。

Result: 在 ML-20M 和淘宝两大数据集上，LIFT 在所有评估的联合模型中获得最高 Joint Score，分别比最强基线提升 4.9% 和 3.6%。损失权重扫描显示优越的检索-排序权衡，消融实验与扩展性分析进一步验证了生命周期序列构建、模型组件和容量设置的有效性。

Conclusion: LIFT 通过建模交互生命周期的因果状态序列，有效解决了级联推荐中检索与排序阶段的信息不对称问题，在保持阶段特异性的同时实现历史建模共享，显著提升联合推荐性能并提供良好的阶段间权衡。

Abstract: Cascaded recommender systems use the same user history for retrieval and ranking, while the two stages have access to different information at different points of an interaction. We propose the Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer (LIFT), which decomposes each interaction into ordered Request, Item, Context, and Action states and models them as a causal sequence. Retrieval reads the Request state, while ranking reads the Context state, allowing both tasks to share history modeling while preserving stage-specific information. LIFT instantiates this representation with Role-Conditioned Attention and a lightweight Pre-LN Bias. On ML-20M and Taobao, LIFT achieves the highest Joint Score among the evaluated joint models, improving over the strongest baselines by 4.9% and 3.6%, respectively. Loss-weight sweeps show favorable retrieval--ranking trade-offs, while ablations and scaling analyses further examine lifecycle sequence construction, model components, and capacity settings.

</details>


### [394] [Learning Multi-Step Query Rewriting via Corpus Feedback for Conversational Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10955)
*João Coelho,Hong Wang,Jie Yuan,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出将对话查询重写 (CQR) 重新建模为顺序检索问题，训练智能体通过多步重写-检索循环优化查询，无需人工重写标注，仅用检索质量奖励进行强化学习，在 TopiOCQA、QReCC 和 CAsT 基准上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CQR 方法单步重写，在检索前固定查询，无法利用语料证据修正指代消歧和词汇选择，导致检索效果受限。

Method: 将 CQR 重构为顺序决策过程：智能体在三类重写操作（解析会话意图为独立查询、词汇重述、合成伪文档用于文档到文档匹配）和停止动作中选择，先重写再检索，再根据返回段落条件化下一步重写；先监督微调后用单一检索质量奖励做强化学习，无需人工重写标注。

Result: 在 TopiOCQA 和 QReCC 上优于多个检索对齐基线，跨检索后端稳健，且在 CAsT 基准上零样本泛化；分析显示策略自发学会将伪文档锚定在早期检索段落上，显著提升检索质量。

Conclusion: 顺序重写-检索范式结合检索奖励强化学习，能让模型自主学习基于证据的查询优化，无需人工重写标注即可实现更有效的对话检索。

Abstract: Conversational Query Rewriting (CQR) turns a context dependent user turn into a standalone query for a retriever, and most methods do this in a single step from the dialogue history before retrieving once. The rewrite is therefore fixed before any corpus evidence is available to correct its reference resolution or its vocabulary. We recast CQR as a sequential retrieval problem: an agent rewrites the current turn, retrieves, and conditions its next rewrite on the returned passages. The agent acts in a typed space of three rewriting operations, resolving conversational intent into a standalone query, generating lexical reformulations, or synthesizing pseudo-documents for document-to-document matching, together with a stop action that ends the episode. We train the policy with supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning against a single retrieval-quality reward, using no human rewrite annotations. Across TopiOCQA and QReCC, the agent outperforms several retrieval-aligned baselines, while remaining effective across retrieval backends and generalizing to the CAsT benchmarks without additional training. Further analysis shows that, through retrieval-reward optimization alone, the learned policy develops a behavior of grounding pseudo-documents in passages retrieved by earlier steps, substantially improving retrieval.

</details>


### [395] [H2CE: Modeling Geo-Semantic Interactions for POI Reranking with Heterogeneous Two-Stage Cross-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11277)
*Zhengwei Bai,Moreno D'Incà,Danielle Class,Alessandro Moschitti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: H2CE 是一种异构两阶段 Cross-Encoder，用于延迟受限的 POI 重排序。它通过桶化自然语言描述符和精确标量值的双重数值表示，结合潜在空间聚合融合语义与数值嵌入，采用点wise 过滤 + top-K pairwise 重排的两阶段架构，在本地搜索测试集上实现 67.48% NDCG@5，显著优于 XGBoost LTR 和零样本 LLM 重排器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POI 重排序需在实时服务约束下，建模查询条件下的词汇语义、地理空间邻近性与数值质量信号（评分、评论数）之间的权衡。近距离 POI 可能仅部分满足查询意图，而较远 POI 可能提供更强的语义与质量证据，现有方法难以有效融合这些异构信号并保持低延迟。

Method: H2CE 采用异构双重数值表示：1) 将桶化后的自然语言描述符插入 Cross-Encoder 输入，支持语义-数值注意力；2) 通过专用 MLP 处理精确标量值，保留幅度信息。语义与数值嵌入经潜在空间聚合融合，实现非线性交互。两阶段架构：Stage 1 对所有候选点wise 打分，用于可扩展过滤；Stage 2 在 top-K 候选中进行头对头 pairwise 比较并用 Copeland 聚合，将 pairwise 成本从 O(N^2) 降至 O(N + K(K-1))。

Result: 在 5,743 查询的本地搜索测试集上，H2CE 达到 67.48% NDCG@5，绝对提升 XGBoost LTR 22.82% 与零样本 LLM 重排器 35.89%。Pairwise 阶段在 pointwise 基础上再增 1.98% NDCG@5。消融实验验证了数值特征、潜在聚合、top-K pairwise 重排及对齐训练的有效性。

Conclusion: H2CE 通过异构数值建模、潜在空间非线性融合与两阶段重排架构，有效解决了查询条件下的多信号权衡问题，在保持实时推理可行性的同时大幅提升重排序质量，为本地搜索 POI 重排序提供了实用且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Point-of-Interest (POI) reranking in local search must model query-conditioned tradeoffs among lexical semantics, geospatial proximity, and numerical quality signals such as rating and review count, while remaining practical under real-time serving constraints. A close POI may only partially satisfy the query intent, while a farther one may offer stronger semantic and quality evidence. We present H2CE, a Heterogeneous Two-stage Cross-Encoder for latency-bounded POI reranking. H2CE represents numerical attributes in two complementary ways: bucketized natural-language descriptors are inserted into the cross-encoder input to support semantic--numeric attention, while exact scalar values are processed by dedicated MLPs to preserve magnitude information. The resulting semantic and numerical embeddings are fused through latent-space aggregation, enabling nonlinear interactions beyond scalar weighted sums. H2CE then applies a two-stage architecture: Stage 1 scores all candidates pointwise for scalable filtering, and Stage 2 performs head-to-head pairwise comparison among the top-$K$ candidates with Copeland aggregation, making fine-grained relative tradeoffs explicit while reducing pairwise cost from O(N^2) to O(N+K(K-1)). On a 5,743-query local search test set, H2CE achieves 67.48% NDCG@5, improving over XGBoost LTR by +22.82% absolute and over a zero-shot LLM reranker by +35.89%. The pairwise stage adds +1.98% NDCG@5 over the pointwise model alone. Ablations confirm the value of numerical features, latent aggregation, top-K pairwise reranking, and aligned training.

</details>


### [396] [EVIE: Evidence-Vector-Informed Embeddings for Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11553)
*Zifei Wang,Wei Wen,Qiang Ji,Qian-Wen Zhang,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 EVIE (Evidence-Vector-Informed Embeddings)，一系列原生视觉文档检索器，通过三大创新：(1) 基于证据的数据治理，(2) 双向师生学习结合对称列表式蒸馏与前缀式 Matryoshka 表示学习，(3) 层次凝聚索引压缩 (HAC)，显著提升视觉文档检索的准确性与存储权衡。EVIE-8B 在 ViDoRe V3 达到 66.75 nDCG@10，超最强基线 1.43 分；EVIE-4.5B+HAC 仅用 3.81 GiB/百万页即保持 59.58 nDCG@10，向量载荷降低 128 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索方法难以兼顾细粒度页面理解与高效索引：基于 OCR 的文本检索引入预处理延迟并丢失视觉/结构线索；单向量视觉语言模型将整页压缩为一个向量，限制查询-文档匹配粒度；多向量检索器虽提供更细交互，但索引庞大且准确率仍有提升空间。核心痛点在于表示学习与索引构建过程中未保留查询相关的页面证据。

Method: 三大核心创新：(1) 证据判定数据治理：利用多模态裁判识别含答案的正样本并过滤不可靠负样本。(2) 双向师生学习：结合对称列表式蒸馏与前缀式 Matryoshka 表示学习 (Prefix-MRL)，使单一学生检查点无需重新编码即可服务六个嵌套嵌入维度。(3) 层次凝聚索引压缩 (HAC)：对页面 Token 进行空间正则化聚类并存储语义质心，支持单阶段 MaxSim 检索。

Result: 在 ViDoRe V1/V2/V3 与 JinaVDR 共 138 项任务上验证：EVIE-8B 在 V3 达到 66.75 nDCG@10，超外部最强基线 1.43 分，四套平均 79.51。EVIE-4.5B+HAC 以 3.81 GiB/百万页存储保持 59.58 nDCG@10，向量载荷压缩 128 倍。

Conclusion: EVIE 通过在表示学习与索引构建全流程保留查询相关页面证据，成功突破了现有方法在细粒度理解与索引效率间的权衡瓶颈，为视觉文档检索确立了新的准确性-存储 Pareto 前沿。

Abstract: Accurate and scalable visual document retrieval (VDR) requires both fine-grained page understanding and efficient indexing, yet existing approaches struggle to achieve both. OCR-based text retrieval adds preprocessing latency and can lose visual and structural cues needed to understand complex pages. Single-vector vision-language models bypass OCR, but compressing an entire page into one vector limits the granularity of query--document matching. Multi-vector retrievers with MaxSim provide finer interactions, yet demand large indexes and still leave room for accuracy improvements. We argue that overcoming these limitations requires preserving query-relevant page evidence throughout representation learning and index construction. To this end, we introduce \textbf{\textit{EVIE}} (Evidence-Vector-Informed Embeddings), a family of native visual document retrievers integrating three key innovations: (1) Evidence-judged data governance, which uses a multimodal judge to identify answer-bearing positives and filter unreliable negatives. (2) Bidirectional teacher--student learning with symmetric listwise distillation and prefix-based Matryoshka representation learning (Prefix-MRL), enabling one student checkpoint to serve six nested embedding dimensions without re-encoding. (3) Hierarchical agglomerative index compression (HAC), which clusters page tokens with spatial regularization and stores semantic centroids for single-stage MaxSim retrieval. Extensive experiments across 138 tasks from ViDoRe V1, V2, V3, and JinaVDR validate EVIE. EVIE-8B achieves 66.75 nDCG@10 on V3, exceeding the best external baseline by 1.43 points, with a four-suite average of 79.51. EVIE-4.5B with HAC retains 59.58 nDCG@10 at only 3.81 GiB per million pages, reducing vector payload by $128\times$. Together, these results improve the accuracy--storage trade-off for visual document retrieval.

</details>


### [397] [Overview of the NTCIR-19 Automatic Evaluation of LLMs 2 (AEOLLM-2) Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11598)
*Junjie Chen,Yuxi Dong,Haitao Li,Yiqun Liu,Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文概述了NTCIR-19 AEOLLM-2任务，该任务旨在评估大语言模型在长文本生成场景（特别是深度研究报告）中的自动评估方法，共收到来自10个团队的91个运行结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于NTCIR-18 AEOLLM核心任务的成功，研究者希望进一步探究大语言模型的自动评估方法，特别关注长文本生成场景下的评估挑战。

Method: 引入了新的子任务'深度研究评估'，专注于自动评估由LLM生成的长篇深度研究报告。参赛者开发评估方法，通过将其评分与人工标注的真实标签进行对比来衡量性能。

Result: 论文详细描述了任务背景、数据集构建、评估指标、参赛方法及最终评估结果，共收到10个团队提交的91个运行结果。

Conclusion: AEOLLM-2任务为长文本生成场景下LLM的自动评估提供了基准测试平台，推动了深度研究报告质量自动评估方法的发展。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide an overview of the NTCIR-19 Automatic Evaluation of LLMs 2 (AEOLLM-2) task. Building on the success of the NTCIR-18 core task AEOLLM, we proposed AEOLLM-2 for NTCIR-19 to further investigate automatic evaluation methods for Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly in long-form text generation scenarios. In AEOLLM-2, we introduced a new subtask, Deep Research Evaluation, which focuses on the automatic evaluation of long-form deep research reports generated by LLMs. Participants developed evaluation methods to automatically assess the quality of these reports, and the performance of each method was measured by comparing its scores against human-annotated ground-truth labels. This year, we received 91 runs from 10 teams in total. This paper describes the background of the task, the dataset construction, the evaluation measures, the participants' methods, and the final evaluation results.

</details>


### [398] [SkillContrast: Difference-Guided Text Selection for Agent Skill Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11650)
*Jiandong Ding,Honglei Ji,Ming Liu,Tao Duan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillContrast，一种无需训练的技能选择器，通过对比检索到的技能并保留差异文本及局部上下文，用于预训练重排序器。实验表明其在SameCapRisk-Bench上优于TF-IDF查询选择，能更有效地检索有用技能同时避免风险技能，且大幅减少模型输入token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似的智能体技能常共享指令，但在使用条件上有所不同。基于查询的文本选择倾向于保留共享指令而忽略这些区别，导致无法有效区分有益技能与其标记为风险的同类技能，从而影响检索精度和安全性。

Method: 提出SkillContrast，一种无需训练的选择器。它比较检索到的多个技能，识别并保留它们之间的差异文本及其局部上下文，将这些精简后的差异信息作为预训练重排序器的输入，以补充基于查询的相关性选择。

Result: 在SameCapRisk-Bench的1,235个请求上，SkillContrast比TF-IDF查询选择多获得54-72个clean hits（检索到有用技能且无其标记的风险同类技能），跨2个检索器和2种重排序器规模。消融实验显示差异文本是主要贡献因素。与完整技能主体相比，SkillContrast减少51.1-58.8%的模型输入token，在0.6B模型上clean hits减少10-18，在4B模型上持平或更高。

Conclusion: 候选技能间的相对差异文本能有效补充查询相关性，为选择紧凑的重排序输入提供了更优的信号，实现了检索效率与效果的平衡。

Abstract: Similar agent skills can share instructions but differ in their conditions of use. Query-based text selection may retain shared instructions and omit these distinctions. We introduce SkillContrast, a training-free selector that compares retrieved skills and retains their differing text with local context for a pretrained reranker. On 1,235 requests from SameCapRisk-Bench, it yields 54-72 more clean hits (requests that retrieve a helpful skill without its marked risky sibling) than TF-IDF query selection at identical per-candidate input lengths, across 2 retrievers and 2 reranker sizes. Length-matched component replacements identify differing text as the main contributor in the primary setting, with smaller, mixed context effects. Relative to full skill bodies, SkillContrast uses 51.1-58.8% fewer model-input tokens, with 10-18 fewer clean hits at 0.6B and matching or higher observed clean-hit counts at 4B. Candidate-relative differences thus complement query relevance in selecting compact reranking inputs.

</details>


### [399] [Autoregressive Retriever: Improving Query Understanding from Item Feedback for Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11666)
*Jianfei Zhao,Yifan Wang,Feng Zhang,Xin Sun,Chong Feng,Zhixing Tan,Yang Luo,Boyuan Pan,Xu Kai,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 AutoRegressive Retriever (ARR)，一种多模态检索模型，通过交替检索条目并更新查询嵌入，利用检索到的条目作为反馈来细化后续检索，结合监督微调与强化学习训练，在域内和零样本基准上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态检索通常一次性编码查询并按嵌入相似度排序，查询表示固定不变，无法利用已检索条目澄清信息需求，限制了检索效果。

Method: ARR 采用自回归方式：交替检索一个条目并更新查询嵌入，最终用最终嵌入对全库排序。训练分两阶段：1) 监督微调，逐步对比监督教导编码器利用反馈；2) 强化学习，将反馈条目视为动作，优化其选择以最大化相关条目的最终倒数排名。查询侧适配器使优化可在固定条目索引上进行。

Result: 在域内和零样本多模态检索基准上，ARR 平均性能优于对比基线。消融分析显示：推理时反馈能提升检索质量；训练时引入反馈也能改善初始查询嵌入（即无反馈时的表现）。

Conclusion: ARR 通过自回归检索与反馈机制，有效解决了传统检索中查询表示静态的问题，同时监督微调与强化学习的结合使模型学会主动选择信息量大的反馈条目，显著提升了多模态检索的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Universal multimodal retrieval typically encodes a query once and ranks independently indexed items by embedding similarity. This design supports efficient search, but leaves the query representation unchanged even when retrieved items could help clarify the information need. We introduce the AutoRegressive Retriever (ARR), a multimodal retrieval model that learns both to select informative items and to use their content to refine subsequent retrieval. ARR alternates between retrieving an item and updating the query embedding, then uses the final embedding to rank the collection. Supervised fine-tuning teaches the encoder to use feedback through stepwise contrastive supervision. Reinforcement learning treats feedback items as actions and optimizes their selection using the final reciprocal rank of a relevant item. A query-side adapter enables this optimization against a fixed item index. ARR demonstrates strong retrieval performance on both in-domain and zero-shot benchmarks, outperforming the compared baselines on average. Further analyses show that feedback improves retrieval at inference time and that training with feedback also improves the initial query embedding, before any item is observed.

</details>


### [400] [Chaos in the Text: Revealing the Modality Preference in Mixed-Modality Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11816)
*Yubo Sun,Chunyi Peng,Yukun Yan,Zhenghao Liu,Zhipeng Xu,Sen Mei,Linlin Xin,Zheni Zeng,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了密集检索器在混合模态语料库（文本、图像、文本-图像融合）上的表现，发现模态组成对检索性能影响显著，提出Trident方法通过多视角InfoNCE缓解模态偏好，提升混合模态检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集检索器在单模态语料库上表现优异，但在包含文本、图像及融合文档的混合语料库中，性能对模态组成高度敏感，且存在‘文本混乱’现象：无关文本比无关图像造成更严重的性能下降，这是由于文本表示系统性获得更高相似度分数，导致无关文本排在相关图像前。

Method: 提出Trident框架，为每个文档构建文本、图像、融合文本-图像三个协同正视角，采用多正视角InfoNCE联合优化相关性判别与正视角平衡，适用于CLIP-based和VLM-based架构。

Result: 在视觉文档和自然图像基准上，Trident显著提升混合模态检索性能，降低对模态组成和文本干扰的敏感度，同时提高平均单模态检索性能。

Conclusion: Trident有效缓解了混合模态检索中的模态偏好偏差，通过多视角协同训练实现了跨模态的鲁棒检索，为构建通用混合模态检索器提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dense retrievers have made significant progress on text and image corpora, but whether these capabilities extend reliably to mixed corpora containing text, image, and fused text-image documents remains unclear. In this paper, we systematically examine retrievers across architectures and find that their performance is highly sensitive to modality composition. As image documents are progressively replaced with semantically corresponding text representations, retrieval performance follows a pronounced V-shaped curve, remaining strong on single-modality corpora but degrading substantially when modalities coexist. In particular, irrelevant text causes more severe degradation than an equal number of irrelevant images, a phenomenon we term Chaos in the Text. Further analysis reveals modality preference, whereby text representations receive systematically higher similarity scores, allowing irrelevant text to outrank relevant images. To mitigate this bias, we introduce Trident, which constructs text, image, and fused text-image views of each document as co-equal positives and jointly optimizes relevance discrimination and positive-view balance through Multi-Positive View InfoNCE. Experiments across visual document and natural image benchmarks show that trident improves mixed-modality retrieval on both CLIP-based and VLM-based architectures, reduces sensitivity to modality composition and text distractors, and increases average single-modality retrieval performance.

</details>


### [401] [Project Greenhouse: Progress Toward Fully Open and Sovereign Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11922)
*Jimmy Lin,Sahel Sharifymoghaddam,Lingwei Gu,Nour Jedidi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Project Greenhouse 探索用少量计算资源构建完全开放、主权的代理搜索模型，提出两步法（从头预训练+监督微调）训练竞争力的逐点解码器重排序器，并发布可复现的工件和 Gaggle 模型检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在证明无需依赖第三方开放权重骨干网络，仅用常见数据集和少量 GPU，即可端到端控制模型训练，构建主权、可复现的代理搜索模型。

Method: 采用简单的两步配方：从头预训练，然后在常见数据集上进行监督微调，训练逐点解码器专用重排序器；全程自主控制训练，不使用现成开放权重骨干。

Result: 在少量 GPU 上完成大部分实验，训练出具有竞争力的 Gaggle 模型家族，并发布数据、代码、配置及检查点，支持透明独立复现。

Conclusion: 验证了仅用适度计算资源即可构建完全开放主权代理搜索模型的可行性，为更广泛的论点提供了第一步证据。

Abstract: Project Greenhouse represents our exploration of a simple thesis: We believe that it is possible to build fully open and sovereign models for agentic search with only modest computational resources. As a first milestone, we describe how to build a competitive pointwise decoder-only reranker using a simple two-step recipe comprising pre-training from scratch followed by supervised fine-tuning, starting only from commonly available datasets. Contrary to the dominant approach in the literature, we do not rely on existing open-weight backbones from third parties, and thus we are fully in control of model training, from end to end. We were able to accomplish the bulk of our experiments using no more than a handful of GPUs. This report articulates the importance and benefits of our approach, and we share artifacts that enable transparent, independent reproduction of all aspects of model training. Beyond data, code, and configurations that capture our efforts, we also release checkpoints for our family of Gaggle models, demonstrating the feasibility of our approach and providing a first step toward validating our broader thesis.

</details>


### [402] [NativeScope: Relation-Localized Retrieval over Native Topology with a Correct Anchor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12243)
*Long Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出NativeScope，一种基于原生结构（章节、会话边界、原生顺序）的scope-then-rank稠密检索方法，将查询表示为(A, r, B)，利用锚点A和关系r通过belonging/before/after算子选择原生单元，再用目标词B在选定范围内排序。在QASPER和LongMemEval上，NativeScope显著提升原生单元召回率（文档89.28%，记忆72.50%），优于全文稠密RAG 42.75/22.00个百分点；增益主要来自关系定界而非查询缩短。但依赖可靠的锚点坐标，自动Top-1锚点下记忆召回骤降至35.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密检索仅依据语义相似度排序文本块，忽略了数据系统已存储的结构信息（章节归属、会话边界、原生顺序），导致在需要定位特定结构范围的查询上表现不佳。

Method: 提出NativeScope方法：将查询建模为q → (A, r, B)。锚点A和关系r通过belonging（归属）、before（之前）、after（之后）算子在原生单元层面进行硬定界，得到候选范围；目标词B仅在该范围内对重叠块进行排序。另设内部变体NS-FullQ，用完整问题对同一候选集排序，以区分定界与查询缩短的贡献。

Result: 在1,024 token预算下，NativeScope文档原生单元召回89.28%，记忆72.50%，分别比实例级稠密RAG提升42.75和22.00个百分点。NS-FullQ分别为87.78%和68.50%，与NativeScope差异不显著，表明主要增益源于关系定界而非更短的排序查询。使用自动Top-1锚点时，记忆召回降至35.50%，暴露硬定界对定位接口错误的敏感性。

Conclusion: NativeScope在锚点坐标和原生关系可靠时极为有效，但硬定界机制会继承定位接口的错误；实际部署需解决锚点自动识别的鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Dense retrieval usually ranks text chunks by their semantic similarity to a question. This ignores structure that many data systems already store, including section membership, session boundaries, and native order. We propose NativeScope, a scope-then-rank method for queries with a known anchor and relation. It represents a query as q -> (A, r, B). The anchor A and relation r select native units through belonging, before, or after operators, and the target term B ranks only chunks that overlap the selected scope. An internal variant, NS-FullQ, ranks the same candidates with the full question. We evaluate both methods on 200 controlled document and memory records derived from QASPER and LongMemEval under a 1,024-token budget. NativeScope attains native-unit recall of 89.28 percent for documents and 72.50 percent for memories, improving over instance-wide Dense RAG by 42.75 and 22.00 percentage points. NS-FullQ reaches 87.78 percent and 68.50 percent; its differences from NativeScope are inconclusive, locating the primary gain in relational scoping rather than the shorter ranking query. With automatic Top-1 anchors, memory recall falls to 35.50 percent. NativeScope is therefore effective when anchor coordinates and native relations are reliable, but hard scoping inherits errors from the localization interface.

</details>


### [403] [Compact and Efficient Indexes for Learned Sparse Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12300)
*Franco Maria Nardini,Luca Rizzo,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种显著降低学习稀疏检索索引内存占用的方法，同时保持最先进检索数据结构的效率。基于SEISMIC，论文重新设计了倒排索引和正排索引两个层级：倒排索引用文档代表（medoids）替代昂贵的块级摘要，将元数据从稀疏向量压缩为单个文档标识符；正排索引则通过词汇重排、DOTPACKING8位压缩方案、4位量化码本以及JUMPDOT内核压缩组件和值。实验表明，在同等准确率下，该方法比最佳竞争者快5.3倍且内存减少3倍，在极度内存受限场景下仍快1.9倍且内存减少3.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习稀疏检索索引（如SEISMIC）虽然检索效率高，但内存占用大，限制了实际部署。现有方法在倒排索引和正排索引层面仍有冗余：倒排索引的块级摘要存储稀疏向量开销大，正排索引的组件和值压缩不够极致。需要一种既能大幅降低内存占用，又不牺牲查询速度，且具有通用性（可插拔到其他系统）的压缩方案。

Method: 1. 倒排索引层面：引入medoids（文档代表），用现有文档作为块代表替代稀疏向量摘要，将块级元数据从稀疏向量压缩为单个文档ID。2. 正排索引层面：(a) 重排词汇表使共现组件相邻，用Δ-gaps编码并采用DOTPACKING8（SIMD友好的位压缩方案，融合解压与点积计算）；(b) 值量化：为每个组件拟合紧凑的4位码本；(c) 提出JUMPDOT分块点积内核，针对少量非零条目的查询优化。正排索引压缩独立于SEISMIC，可集成至任何基于正排索引评分的系统（如KANNOLO）。

Result: 在MS MARCO数据集上，使用三种最先进的学习稀疏编码器评估：同等准确率下，查询速度比最佳竞争者快达5.3倍，内存占用减少约3倍；在最内存受限模式下，查询速度仍快达1.9倍，内存占用减少达3.9倍。正排索引压缩方案成功集成到KANNOLO中，验证了其通用性。

Conclusion: 论文通过重新设计倒排索引和正排索引的压缩策略，实现了学习稀疏检索索引的显著内存减少与速度提升。提出的medoids、DOTPACKING8、4位量化码本和JUMPDOT内核协同工作，达到了最先进的速度-空间权衡。正排索引压缩方案的模块化设计使其可作为通用组件插入其他检索系统，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper investigates how to substantially reduce the memory footprint of learned sparse retrieval indexes without sacrificing the efficiency of state-of-the-art retrieval data structures. Building on SEISMIC, we revisit both levels of its design: the inverted index used to select candidates and the forward index used to score them. For the inverted index, we replace costly per-block summaries with medoids, namely existing documents elected as block representatives, collapsing the per-block metadata from a sparse vector to a single document identifier. For the forward index, we compress both components and values. We reorder the vocabulary to place co-occurring components closer together and encode the resulting $Δ$-gaps with DOTPACKING8, a SIMD-friendly bit-packing scheme that fuses decompression with dot-product evaluation; values are quantized with compact per-component 4-bit codebooks fitted to each component's distribution. We further introduce JUMPDOT, a blocked dot-product kernel tailored for queries that contain only a few non-zero entries. Our forward-index compression is independent of SEISMIC and can be plugged into any system relying on forward-index-based scoring, as we demonstrate by integrating it into KANNOLO. A comprehensive evaluation on MS MARCO with three state-of-the-art learned sparse encoders shows that our solutions markedly improve the speed-space trade-off of learned sparse retrieval: at equal accuracy, our indexes answer queries up to 5.3x faster than the best competitor while using about 3x less memory, and in the most memory-constrained regime, they remain up to 1.9x faster while using up to 3.9x less memory.

</details>
